Ledger wipe: `npm run wipe:cartola-ledger -w nw-tracker-server` (report-first; fails on FK children on the real DB). The monthly xlsx upload parses via `/parse/santander.checking_cartola_xlsx`. **A stored month is replaced only by count** (`cartolaImportShouldReplaceExisting`: more movements, or any where it had none) — don't decide by reading the stored PDF for «sin movimientos»: annual cartolas print it for empty months and would re-import (renumber) them every run.
- **`bank_account.transfer_notices` v1: who each checking transfer was with.** Cartolas truncate the payee; the bank's mails state name, RUT, bank, account, e-mail, comment. `fetch:santander-mail-archive --from --to` stages transfer / payment / facturado mails under `cfraser/santander-mail-archive/`; `ingest/src/santander/transferMails.ts` decodes every layout since 2015 into `{kind: outgoing | incoming | between_own_products | schedule_created, date, amount, from, to, comment}`; `import:transfer-mails` (nightly after the card feed, 21 days; `--archive=`, `--days=`, `--dry-run`) sends them. The server (`bankTransferNotices.ts`, migration 214) stores each notice once (`bank_transfer_notices`) and rebuilds every pairing each apply (`movement_transfer_notices`, one notice per movement side): same amount on the account the mail names (`bank_account_numbers`), posted from the mail's day to 6 days later, mails in sending order, each bank row once — so an own transfer's echo and a schedule notice pair with nothing. Same-day preference: a transfer to the counterparty's own account (`transfer_counterparty_accounts`, by RUT or account number; personal data, live DB only), then a single-leg row, then a transfer elsewhere; ties differing only by id are twins paired in order, other ties reported and unpaired. **Banco de Chile mails, and movements written from a mail (`ingest/src/bancochile/transferMails.ts`, `transferNoticeMovements.ts`, migrations 219–220):** `import:transfer-mails` also reads `serviciodetransferencias@bancochile.cl` (mails the recipient; the Rut and account printed are the recipient's) and pairs all mailers' notices against every numbered account. Hourly runs `--days=2`. A notice mailed in the last 3 days whose bank row has not arrived gets its movement from the mail (`mailMovementForNotice`): `incoming` → a credit; between two numbered accounts → one transfer (the second mail of a transfer to an own account at another bank is an incoming notice to an unnumbered account and writes nothing); to a known counterparty → a transfer into its landing account (`landingAccountFor`: its only account, or its one `import:panel|kind=clp|` balance; card numbers left to payment receipts); else a debit. A broker's own deposit mail (Racional's duplicate check reaches 5 days back) writes the same transfer when first and recognizes it when second. Confirmation via the importers' transfer-leg skip (`confirmTransferNoticeMovementForTransferLeg`). The row is dated the mail's day, noted `import:<issuer>-mail|<sent at>|Transf a <payee> (<bank>)` / `Transf. de <sender>` (+ ` — <message>`), recorded with signed pesos in `transfer_notice_movements`, never written twice for one mail even after deletion. Both checking importers keep it and skip the bank's listing as `superseded_by_mail` (same signed pesos, mail day from the business day before the bank day through it, or already confirmed on that bank day), stamping the bank's day as posting day and its description; unlisted within 2 business days fails the step (`overdue`). Its note carries the sender's name, not RUT digits, so `propose-card-reimbursements.ts --payer-note-token` must name the sender for such rows. **A bank row listed after the mail's day is dated the mail's day** (`redatePairedRowsToMailDay`, after each pairing rebuild): only inside Santander's posting window (`bankDateMatchesTransferDate`), each paired side keeps its bank day as posting, so the pairing holds on every later apply. A row another document dated (`movement_bank_postings` with basis `evidence`) on another day is a `redate_conflicts` entry and fails the step; a listing's fetch day (basis `fetch`) is replaced by an earlier mail day, and a mail on the same day turns its basis to `evidence`; a transfer whose other side is a numbered bank account or card without its own posting is left on its date (`redate_skipped`). Details carry `redated`, `redate_conflicts`, `redate_skipped`; historical rows are re-dated by the next apply.
- **`bank_account.incoming_wires` v1 (`incomingWires.ts`, migration 228): dollar withdrawals booked from mail.** Santander USD has no feed, so a Fintual dollar withdrawal comes from three mails. Fintual's «Retiro en dólares confirmado» (`broker.notifications` `withdrawal_requested`: net, gross, destination, pay day — `due_on` = next date with the printed day of month) can be cancelled, so it is only stored (`broker_withdrawal_requests`). Banco Security's «Envío de Transferencia» (MT103 copy: 32A value date and amount, 50K ordering customer, 59F beneficiary) and Santander's «AVISO DE LIQUIDACION DE ORDEN DE PAGO RECIBIDA» (order number, amount; no account → the bank's one declared dollar account) are `incoming_wire_notices` (resend must restate), decoded in `ingest/src/wires/`, sent by `import:transfer-mails` (hourly `--days=2`, nightly 21 days; `--issuer=wires` only these). A wire = notices of one transfer (same account via `bank_account_numbers`, leading zeros ignored; same cents; value dates within 2 days; one per side). A request pairs with the one wire into its destination for exactly its net, from request day to 7 days past pay day, with no notice naming another ordering customer; the pair writes broker cash → account for the net on the value date plus a `cash_fee` of gross − net on broker cash (hand-entered ones in the window count). Every apply re-pairs everything; `incoming_wire_bookings` records each request once and survives movement deletion. A request 3 days past pay day with no wire fails the step; an unclaimed wire is reported.
- **`payment.processor_receipts` v3: who a processor charge actually paid.** A processor-only charge («PAGOS.FLOW.CL (WEB)», «FLOW *…», «PAGO FACIL») is explained by the receipt mail: payee (name, RUT, e-mail), amount, time, order, concept, descriptor. `archiveMails` (`ingest/src/email/santanderMailArchive.ts`) stages a sender's mails; `ingest/src/payments/paymentReceiptMails.ts` has one decoder per processor (Flow, both layouts; Pago Fácil, dated by the mail); a non-receipt from the sender decodes to null, an unreadable receipt throws; `import:payment-receipt-mails` (nightly after transfer mails, 45 days; `--days=`, `--from= --to=`, `--archive=`, `--dry-run`). Stored once (migration 215, `payment_processor_receipts`); paired with expense lines **when the lines are built** (`withPaymentReceipts`, `paymentProcessorReceipts.ts`, last step of the expenses payload), never stored, because card line ids change on re-import: same pesos (an interest plan's line by its printed principal, `ccInstallmentInterestForAccount`), purchase day from the day before payment to five after, receipts in payment order, each line once, nearest day first, then a merchant naming the processor; identical lines are twins paired in order, other ties unpaired. Every line of a paired purchase (cuotas too) carries `payment_receipt`. Another processor or platform = one decoder (+ a merchant hint for a processor).
  - **Shop order confirmations** ride the same kind. Shopify «Confirmación de pedido» (shop domain or `t.shopifyemail.com`, fetched by Gmail query — `archiveMails` takes `gmraw`) → platform `shopify`: shop (subject bracket, else text before «Pedido #…», else sender name), items, «Total $X CLP». Orders and processor receipts pair separately (`matchPaymentReceiptsToExpenseLines`); a charge can have both and the line shows the order. Shops' own confirmations (`ORDER_CONFIRMATION_SOURCES`): Calvin Klein (VTEX; Total must equal the payment block's Valor), adidas («hemos recibido tu pedido» / «Gracias por tu pedido»; preparing/shipping/pick-up mails null), Club Dominó (branch in payee, printed time, total with tip), Eventbrite (only a CLP total; free orders null; untested on a real paid mail), miCoca-Cola («Hemos recibido tu pedido … con éxito» / «Pago Aprobado», contacto@micoca-cola.cl; DispatchTrack mails have no amounts; total = subtotal + descuentos + despacho).
  - **Dollars** (`currency: usd`, migration 216; DynaVap): pair only with a line of the same dollars to the cent — never a peso line at the day's rate, which any purchase of the week could fit.
  - **MercadoLibre: split charges and amountless receipts** (migration 221, `decodeMercadoLibreOrder`). A multi-seller order is charged per seller: «Pagaste $ 15.725 1x $ 8.865 y 1x $ 6.860» → `charges` (`payment_receipt_charges`, must sum to the amount); its lines pair all or none, each showing «n/N»; «6x $ 9.245» alone is one charge in cuotas. An `amount: null` receipt pairs after every amount-bearing one, with the unclaimed line on the nearest day (−1…+5) whose merchant is the store's statement name (`STATEMENT_NAME_FOR_AMOUNTLESS`: «MERCADO LIBRE», «*N PRODUCTOS») or a seller (`SELLER_NAMED_CHARGE`: «MERCADOPAGO *TIENDAUNO») — never a bare «MERPAGO*», which is also MercadoPago's in-person payments; a printed amount stays a hard check. **A MercadoPago-prefixed charge** («MP *MERCADOPAGO …», or the listing's cut «MP *MERCADO»; `MERCADOPAGO_PREFIX_CHARGE`, `receiptStatementNames.ts`) is taken by an amountless MercadoLibre receipt only when no store-named or seller-named charge fits and it is the single unclaimed such charge from the day before to five days after the receipt day (a plan's «Total» line included); two candidates link nothing, a bare «MERPAGO*» never qualifies. Risk: an in-person MercadoPago payment alone in that window is linked to the order. A line so linked reads as the catalog's MercadoLibre, the seller staying its link label. **A refund whose purchase structured state names** (a prepayment's credit, an annulment, a paired nota) carries `refund_of` (the purchase's final names, link label and receipt; «Reembolso · <purchase>», ⓘ shows the purchase's receipt) and its merchant is no evidence for the cut-merchant name rule (`cardMerchantsByAccount`).
  - **Amazon: a receipt per shipment**, because Amazon charges the dollar side once per shipment, the day it ships, for the shipment's total (not the order total). `amazon` receipts are the shipping mails (`decodeAmazonShipment`: «Shipment Total» / «SHIPMENT TOTAL» / «Shipment total», «Total» in the «Shipped: "…"» layout; $0 → null). Shipments of one order no line carries singly pair with one line of their sum, from the latest. Order confirmations are `amazon_order` (`ORDER_SUMMARY_OF`): read only for an order none of whose shipments paired, up to 30 days after placing; a confirmation priced by Amazon's currency converter → null.
  - **LATAM, Uber, subscriptions (migration 233).** LATAM purchase mails (`ingest/src/payments/latamMails.ts`, all layouts incl. «E-Ticket Confirmation» in dollars) → `latam` (itinerary, booking); a fare-difference change → `latam_change`. Uber (`uberMails.ts`): `uber` trips carry `trip` (from, to, Chile-clock start/end, km; the line shows each address up to its first comma, `trip_label`), `uber_eats` the restaurant as payee, `uber_one` with `subscription: true`; amount = what the card was charged (Uber Cash legs dropped); another-currency ride/order → null. Summary mails (`uber_trip_summary` / `uber_eats_summary`) are read only for a trip/order no receipt paired (`ORDER_SUMMARY_OF`, keyed by printed start or xid). A peso Uber receipt also pairs with a dollar line naming Uber at the day's rate within 4 % (`PESO_RECEIPT_CHARGED_IN_DOLLARS`; exact amount ranks first). **A subscription's charge reads «Suscripciones»** (`withSubscriptionCategory`, last step of the expense lines) when only the bank's name decided its category (merchant rule or none, `resolveCcExpenseCategory`'s `fromBankName`) and its link says subscription (App Store receipt whose items all renew, a charge inferred from a cycle or monthly run, Uber One); an «Único» row or line-level category stays.
  - **Receipts abroad, Despegar, stays (migration 234).** Any currency (`currency`). Dollar statement lines carry the original amount (`amount_orig`, `amount_orig_currency` = clp / usd or null): a foreign receipt pairs with the dollar line whose original amount matches to the cent (never by conversion); a peso receipt also pairs with a dollar line whose peso origin is its amount. Uber receipts abroad decode in their currency, dated by the mail. Despegar (`despegarMails.ts`) states airline and own charge apart → `charges`. Lodging (`stayMails.ts`): `booking`, `airbnb` (card rows only; credit-paid → null), `stripe`, `accor`, each with `stay` {check_in, check_out, city}. A Booking stay has no descriptor and pairs from the mail's day to five days past check-out, nearest to mail day or check-in; only a reservation's latest Booking mail pairs (`ONE_MAIL_PER_ORDER`); a line printing the receipt's reference wins a tie. A stay no line carries at its amount links as a guess («≈», `guess_basis`) to every untaken line from the day before check-in to the day after check-out whose merchant has the property's first distinctive word (≥4 letters, accents folded, lodging words and the city excluded).
  - **Vehicle permits (migration 268, `ingest/src/payments/vehiclePermitMails.ts`).** Maipú's PDF (`permisocirculacion@maipu.cl`; `PaymentProcessor.pdfAttachment` stages it with `pdftotext -layout` text) has no labels: fields by position and shape, each agreeing with its copies (folio, year, payment day, plate `AB1234-5` / `ABCD12-K`, two accounting parts summing to the amount, amount printed three times, tasación, expiry, car). Expiry 31/08 = cuota 1 of 2; 31/03 next year = cuota 2 of 2, or the whole year when paid before August (cuota 2 payable only from August). Sent as `municipalidad_maipu` with `vehicle_permit` {permit_year, plate, cuota, cuotas, expires_on} (optional; `permit_*` columns), payee «Municipalidad de Maipú», concept «Permiso de circulación <year> · 1ª cuota» = the link label (no category rule per permit).
  - **Ticket vendors and the event (migrations 255–256, `ingest/src/payments/ticketMails.ts`).** `event` {name, starts_on, starts_at, ends_on, venue} (optional; `event_*`): `puntoticket` (event layout or event-less add-on; items + despacho = total), `toliv` (tolivmarket.com / toliv.com; tickets + comisión = total; guest-list without payment block → null), `ticketmaster_cl` (yearless date = first on/after the mail, weekday checked), `ticketmaster_nz` (NZ$), `paylogic` (euros; re-mails on every state change, so only an order's FIRST mail pairs — `ONE_MAIL_PER_ORDER`, where Booking keeps the latest), `aitickets` and `ticketplus` (no amount: pair by charge name «TICKETPLUS» on its day; courtesy/invitations null). Free orders null. The merchant stays the vendor (vendor catalog: PuntoTicket, Toliv, Ticketmaster, AI Tickets, Ticketplus, Paylogic); link label = event + day (`link_label.event_on`), or what was bought; no category rule per event. Eventbrite fills no `event`. Unpaired by design: in-event bar charges («TUU*toliv»), festival purchases.
- **`store.receipt` v1: a grocery receipt.** `{ document: {kind: email | pdf | photo, key, photo_taken_on}, receipt: {chain, number | null, branch, city, purchased_at | null, purchase_date_source, items: [{position, barcode, description, qty, qty_unit, unit_price, total, discount, discount_labels}], receipt_discounts, payments: [{method, amount}], loyalty_points} }`, one per request. Ingest owns the documents: photo inbox (`ingest/src/grocery/inbox.ts`), OCR and chain parsers (`ingest/python/parse-grocery-receipts.py`, `grocery_receipt_*.py`, the Swift OCR helper), staged corpus and import stamps (`stagedReceipts.ts`). `import:grocery-receipts` (`--dry-run`, `--full`, `--reparse`, `--skip-parse`; exits at once when nothing is pending, so the inbox pipeline runs it every pass) sends receipts whose stamp is stale and stamps outcomes the server marks `final` (one waiting for its card line resends every run). On `replaced`, ingest clears the displaced document's stamp and resends it in the same run (stamped `skipped_duplicate`); a wiped DB needs `--full`. The server (`applyStoreReceipt`, `storeReceiptApply.ts`) owns identity and ownership, receipt + item writes, alias classification, card-line rules, branch learning, and drops the card's caches when it writes a line. `grocery_receipts.source` is the document kind (migration 205).
- **`employment.payslips` v2: liquidaciones de sueldo.** `{ parser_version, payslips: [{document, period_month, employer: {name, rut}, pay_period_label, kind: salary | severance, earnings: {…}, deductions: {pension, health, income_tax, unemployment_insurance, voluntary_pension, other, total}, net_pay, indices: {uf, utm, pension_cap_uf, unemployment_cap_uf}, lines: [{position, side: haber | descuento, section: imponible | no_imponible | legal | other | null, label, amount}]}] }` — all payslips in one request, because deposit pairing is decided over the whole set.
  - **Every printed line** (`payslip_lines`, migration 222, replaced whole on re-import): one column-aware reader for text layouts (the right column starts after the first row's left amount; Talana's non-taxable lines are the trailing haberes summing to its TOTAL NO IMPONIBLE header cell) and one for OCR'd scans («(46,639)» = negative haber); fails unless lines add up to every printed total and haberes − descuentos = líquido; the contract re-checks. Line kinds at import (`payslipLineKinds.ts`: rules on the accent-free label and Talana's « (LQ)», first match wins; an unknown label fails before anything is written; migration 223). The printed AFP line splits into mandatory = 10 % of the (capped) taxable base and commission = the rest.
  - **A contribution's payroll month is `pension_contribution_periods`** (migration 223; one movement may pay several months), written by the AFP certificate read, the AFC import and the AFP ledger rebuild (backfill: `server/scripts/backfill-pension-contribution-periods.ts`); a payslip's contributions are the movements whose period is its month.
  - **Finiquitos are payslips** (`earning_type = 'severance'`, `<YYYY-MM>-finiquito.pdf` beside the month's payslip): the DT electronic form (`dt_finiquito`: every non-zero «- Concepto $ amount» of PAGOS / DESCUENTOS; TOTAL PAGOS BRUTOS, TOTAL DESCUENTOS, SUBTOTAL, SUMA LÍQUIDA must hold) or a notarial scan via OCR (`finiquito_scan`).
  - **Rebuilt payslips** (`payroll_work_earnings.origin = 'rebuilt'` + `rebuilt_basis`, migration 225) via `server/scripts/rebuild-payslips.ts --plan=<json> [--apply]` (report-first; plan in untracked `cfraser/payroll-rebuilt.json`): lines from a stored breakdown, the USD contract as fee less transfer fee in dollars (`contractor_fee` / `transfer_fee`; USD lines carry cents), new payslips with their deposit, `payslip_advances`. Lines must add up to totals and net pay.
  - **Gross vs net (`payrollBreakdown.ts`, `payroll_breakdown` on `/api/income`):** per payslip, month and year: gross by line kind (taxable, non-taxable, indemnities, contractor fees), each deduction group (AFP split into 10 % and commission; `other_deductions` = insurance + fees + a finiquito's combined contributions + advances) and net, in pesos and dollars (peso payslip at the pay day's buy rate, dollar one at its stored rate); deductions ≠ gross − net throws. The income page's «Bruto vs líquido» only picks.
  - **Payroll deductions are gastos** (`payslipExpenseLines.ts`, source `payslip`): health, Isapre top-up, life insurance → «Salud»; AFP commission → «Comisiones AFP»; tax withheld → «Impuestos»; the USD contract's transfer fee → «Comisiones»; never savings (AFP 10 %, AFC, APV), advances or a finiquito's combined contributions. Dated to the payslip's month (`occurred_on` = pay day); fixed category (`purchase_key` `payslip:<payslip id × 1000 + position>`, the assign route refuses the source, no category controls, no big group); out of real-estate bill matching. They carry `pre_tax` (`isPreTaxExpenseLine`): Expenses gastos views and line sections omit them unless «Include pre-tax expenses» (`include_pre_tax=1`, `useExpensesIncludePreTax` per browser); home «Últimos movimientos» never lists them; the flows overview always counts them and **adds them back to income** (`payslipGastosLines`, monthly and daily) so money that never reached the account is not counted twice.
  - **Legal parameters and checks:** `payroll.parameters` v1 (migration 226: `payroll_parameters`, `payroll_afp_rates`) carries Previred's monthly «Indicadores Previsionales» — UF, UTM, caps in UF, each AFP's worker rate, the employer's share into the account (read from the document monthly, never hardcoded), the indefinite contract's AFC rates. `import:previred-indicators` (`--from=`, `--no-fetch`, `--dry-run`) downloads missing months to `cfraser/previred-indicadores/<YYYY-MM>.pdf`, reads them (`previredIndicators.ts`; an unreadable field fails) and sends them; nightly with the payslips. Payslips carry `pension_fund` (optional). `payslipChecks.ts` recomputes each peso salary payslip when read (capped base, AFP at its published rate, health ≥ 7 %, AFC 0,6 % when charged, income tax by monthly brackets — annual factors in UTM, health deductible up to 7 % — AFP credited vs 10 % + employer share) and records differences, never refusing; taxable pay follows the line's printed section, the kind only when none is printed. Newer than the newest published month → `pending`; older month without parameters throws. `/api/income` carries `payslip_checks` («Revisión» column); `/api/tax-return` the year's `payroll_withholding`.
  - **F22 settlement (migration 227, `f22Settlements.ts`):** each filed form's refund (code 87) or payment (code 91) against linked bank evidence in `f22_settlements` (account, day, pesos; the movement for a checking row; a card payment found again by account, day, pesos; vanished evidence throws). Links proposed by `server/scripts/link-f22-settlements.ts [--extra=…] [--apply]` (report-first: a «DEV IMPUESTO» checking credit up to 6 % above the refund — paid reajustado — or a «SII» / «F22» / «TGR» card line of exactly the payment), never matched at runtime; the tax page's «Liquidación del F22» shows asked vs received/paid. **Offsets (migration 229):** a refund kept to pay another year's debt is one `offset` row under the refund's year naming `offset_tax_year`, read by both (`offset_kept` / `offset_paid`; `--offset=<refund year>:<debt year>:<date>:<pesos>`). A refund deposited outside checking is an income line (`F22 AT<year>`), reconciles as `resolved_tax_refund`, no ahorro-split mirror. Plan: `docs/payroll-plan.md`.
  - **Parser and import:** `ingest/python/parse-payroll-liquidaciones.py` (`npm run parse:payroll-liquidaciones`); `import:payroll-liquidaciones` (`--dry-run` lists every field a re-import would change, `--no-strict`) sends `cfraser/payroll-parsing-output/all.json`, exits 1 on a payslip no deposit (or several) pays. Server: `applyEmploymentPayslips` (`payslipsApply.ts`): upsert by document, pairing (`findPayrollAutoLinkMovement`), hand-made links never overwritten. No Python lives in `server/scripts/`.
  - **Buk portal (`ingest/src/buk/fetchMain.ts`, `fetch:buk-payslips`):** two-step sign-in (WORK e-mail in `cfraser/buk-fetch.json`, Keychain `nw-tracker-buk`), the full table at `/profile/employees/<id>/liquidaciones` (not the `?source_request=Shortcuts` variant: it lists three months), each missing month saved as `cfraser/liquidaciones/<YYYY>/<YYYY-MM>.pdf` plus Buk's `.html`. The `buk` layout fails unless section totals equal their lines and haberes − descuentos = líquido; health = 7 % + «Adicional Salud». **Schedule:** the nightly carries `payslips` (`decidePayslipsRun`): `fetch: true` from the 1st through the 15th while the previous month has no payslip, else `fetch: false` (parse + import) while the newest payslip has no deposit; the runner imports `--no-strict`. **Pairing reads the income lines**, which include the daily feed's credits, so a payslip pairs the night the salary lands. When the cartola supersedes that daily row, `prunePartialMovementsSupersededByCartola` moves the payslip link and `checking_income_movement_overrides` entry onto the official row (`carryMovementKeyedIncomeState`) — both reference movements without cascade, so a link left behind would fail the cartola import on the FK.
- **`broker.dividend_statement` v1: Fintual's «Acciones» documents.** `{ broker: "fintual", document: {kind: monthly_statement | certificate, name, label}, dividends: [{date, symbol, gross, withholding, net, per_share, position_qty, record_date, withholding_rate_pct, withholding_jurisdiction, tax_country}], interest: [{date, amount, description}] }`, one per document. Ingest (`fintual/acciones.ts`, `accionesDocs.ts`) reads the PDFs `fetch:fintual-docs` stages under `cfraser/fintual-acciones/` with `pdftotext -layout`, stating the jurisdiction (`US` where the Alpaca cartola prints an NRA line; the certificado names none); `import:fintual-acciones` (`--apply`) prints the report, exits 1 on a conflict. Server: `applyBrokerDividendStatement` (`brokerDividendStatementApply.ts`) pairs with `dividend_payout` rows and writes `movement_dividend_details`.
- **`fund_account.transactions` v1: the Fintual certificado de transacciones.** `{ provider: "fintual", document, max_month, transactions: [{date, investment: {id, name}, medio, clp_in, clp_out, units_in, units_out, unit_value}] }` — only rows that move pesos or cuotas (daily balance rows not sent). Ingest (`fintual/certificadoInbox.ts`, `certificado.ts`) installs an inbox certificado as `cfraser/fintual-certificado-de-transacciones.csv`; `import:fintual-cert` (`--apply`, `--from-inbox`, `IMPORT_MAX_MONTH`) prints the reconcile; the inbox pipeline calls it `--from-inbox` (report only) every run. Server: `applyFundAccountTransactions` (`fintualCertImport.ts`, aggregation in `fintualCertificadoTransacciones.ts`): goal → account mapping, reconcile against curated flows, valor-cuota backfill.
- **`unemployment_fund.documents` v2: the AFC certificate and cartolas.** `{ provider: "afc", certificate: {legs: [{period_month, employer_rut, employer, taxable_income, amount, paid_on}], total}, statements: [{period_from, period_to, opening, closing, contributions, other_income, gain, total_income, commissions, other_outflows, account_use, total_outflows, detail: [{employer, pay_month, amount}]}] }`; the account is the one on the fund's series; the contract re-checks legs = TOTAL and closing = opening + income − outflows. Ingest (`afc/documents.ts`) reads the PDFs; `import:afc-cert` prints the server's report. Server: `applyUnemploymentFundDocuments` (`afcCertImport.ts`) runs the rebuild (contributions, withdrawal units, true-ups, re-measured closing withdrawals, ledger checks) in one transaction rolled back without `apply`, returning the report.
- **`unemployment_fund.balance` v1 and the nightly AFC read (`ingest/src/afc/fetch.ts`, `ingest/src/claveUnica/`, `server/src/unemploymentFundBalanceApply.ts`, migration 240).** `fetch:afc [-- --apply --background]` signs in to personas.afc.cl **with the AFC's own clave** (`ingest/src/afc/login.ts`: `input#rut`, password, the type=button «Ingresar con contraseña AFC»; the page's invisible reCAPTCHA makes its token; a refused attempt is reloaded once; Keychain `nw-tracker-afc`, account = RUT in `cfraser/afc-fetch.json`; no second factor). `--clave-unica` uses **ClaveÚnica** instead, reusable for any ClaveÚnica service (`ingest/src/claveUnica/login.ts`: «ClaveÚnica» button, RUN typed compact, clave from Keychain `nw-tracker-clave-unica` (`CLAVE_UNICA_KEYCHAIN_SERVICE`), Enter then «INGRESA» if the form is still up, «Continuar», then the 6-character code from `codeMail.ts` — sender `no-reply@digital.gob.cl`, subject «ClaveÚnica - Código de validación de autenticación», one base64 HTML part found via the MIME structure; only a mail newer than this run's «Continuar» counts, and after 30 s without one the newest inside the 5-minute validity, because ClaveÚnica re-uses a valid code instead of mailing again). The read: (1) the home summary (`apis.afc.cl/sva-portada/v1`: `saldo`, `fechaUltimaCotizacion`; its occasional 500 is an unavailable balance, never 0) → `unemployment_fund.balance`, recorded in `unemployment_fund_reads` beside `accountMarkClpAtYmd` that day; **report only** (site / app / difference, last credited month vs ledger); (2) the full-history paid-contributions certificate produced **by the page** (`/documentos/certificados` → «Cotizaciones pagadas» → «Histórico» → «Descargar»; the API `sva-certificados/v1/certificado-cotizaciones-pagadas` refuses direct calls «Error sin captcha» — never work around it), kept by browser.ts's download stash (emptied in place: the init script holds its own reference), saved as `cfraser/afc-certs/cotizaciones-latest.pdf` and sent with **every cartola under `cfraser/afc-certs/cartolas/`** as one idempotent `unemployment_fund.documents`. **Cartolas by mail:** `fetch:afc-cartolas` (`ingest/src/email/afcCartolas.ts`, nightly) stages the PDF from `cartolasafc@afc.cl`'s «Cartola cuatrimestral / Anual Seguro de Cesantía» (June, October, February), keyed by mail date + message id, decrypted with qpdf (password = RUT digits without verifier, from the config); `parseAfcCartola` reads both the mailed (`Al 31/12/2025 … $ 3.652.167`, footnote on the Total line) and downloaded (`Al 31-12-2025 … $3.652.167`) renderings. **A cartola true-up is keyed on its saldo DAY** (`AFC ajuste cartola — saldo YYYY-MM-DD`, `afcCartolaTrueUpNoteKey`), because one period's saldo final is the next's saldo inicial; legacy `saldo inicial|final <day>` rows are matched and rekeyed on update. **Schedule:** the nightly carries `afc_fetch` (`decideAfcFetch`: `reason`, `expected_period`, `overdue`) from the **14th of the month after the newest salary payslip's month** until that month's contribution is in `pension_contribution_periods` on the AFC account (Previred deadline the 13th, credit a few days later); no payslip = no read; past the following month the request is `overdue` and the fetcher (`--expect=YYYY-MM --overdue`) fails if the certificate still lacks it. The runner also reads the AFC on any night `fetch:afc-cartolas` staged a mail. Step «AFC (apply)» after AFP UNO (`NW_TRACKER_AFC_APPLY=1` in the ingest plist; report only without). Supervised capture: `-- --capture [--minutes=N] [--code-from-stdin]` tours pages by href (the sidebar is collapsed and unclickable), keeps API calls under `cfraser/afc-captures/`.
- **`mortgage.sheet_payments` (retired).** The Numbers-sheet read is gone (kind, apply, `mortgage_sheet_imports` dropped by migration 308, readers) because it could hang a run on an iCloud placeholder. Cuotas come from MetLife's portal (`mortgage.lender_statement`) or the app's form; one-off repair: `server/scripts/archive/repair-mortgage-ledger-from-sheet.ts`. **The card line that paid a cuota is paired when read, never stored (`mortgagePaymentCardMatch.ts`):** each regular cuota vs mortgage-merchant card lines (`isMortgageCcExpenseMerchant`) of the same pesos from the payment day to 3 days after, each side once; the ledger payload carries `card_matches` / `card_lines_without_payment` («Tarjeta» column + notice); nightly task `mortgage_card_match_check` (`check:mortgage-card-match`, after the card feed) fails when either side has waited 5 Chile business days inside the last 45.
- **`utility.bill` v1: utility bills and gastos comunes statements become the real-estate page's bill rows.** `{ bills: [{provider, service: electricity | gas | water | internet | hoa, service_account (N° cliente as printed), bill_number, issued_on, amount, due_on, period_from, period_to, kwh, m3, estimated_reading, message_id}] }` — provider a slug, kWh only on electricity, m³ only on gas/water, one bill per (provider, bill number). `import:utility-bills` (`ingest/src/utilities/`; nightly after payment receipts, and every hourly with `--only-if-new`, which sends only when the window holds a message id the previous `<provider>-recent.json` lacked; failed sends retried nightly; `--days=` default 70, `--from= --to=`, `--archive=`, `--dry-run`) stages mails under `cfraser/utility-bill-mails/`. **ENEL** («Cuenta Enel de este mes <N° cliente>», `no-responder@chile.enel.com`): boleta number and date, amount, due date, kWh with «Consumo real» / «Consumo estimado» (some print none) and the NEXT period; a bill's own period is the one its predecessor announced, a next period that does not follow throws; labels matched accent-free (the HTML text drops accents). **Metrogas** («Boleta Metrogas Nro. …», `pagoenlinea@emailmetrogas.cl`): boleta, cliente, two-month period, amount, due date; the mail's day stands for the issue date; duplicate mails collapsed; m³ only in an unfetched PDF, so `m3` null. Other mails from those senders → null; an unreadable bill mail throws. **Gastos comunes** (`hoaStatementMails.ts`): senders in untracked `cfraser/hoa-mail.json` (`{senders: [{from, provider}]}`; no file = none). A gastos-subject mail carries one PDF per unit («1203.pdf»), staged with its text by `archiveMails`' `pdfAttachment` (under `cfraser/utility-bill-mails/attachments/`); «Total a pagar» = amount, «Depto» = `service_account` (must equal the file name), the statement date = issue day, «dentro de los N días» = due day; the bill is the month before the statement date, numbered `<unit>-<YYYY-MM>`; the header's month name and any month the subject names must agree (the header's year is not read — it can be misprinted). Other subjects → null; a gastos mail without one unit PDF, or unreadable, throws. **Server (`utilityBillsApply.ts`, migration 244):** stores each bill once in `utility_bills` (a differing resend is a conflict; a missing period is filled from a send that has it) and follows unsettled bills: place from **`utility_service_accounts`** (provider + account number, compared without punctuation or leading zeros → `expense_accounts`; personal data, live DB only; unmapped writes nothing); the charge is the one whole purchase among `loadGastosLinesForRealEstateMatching()` of exactly the bill's pesos, merchant of the kind (`merchantMatchesExpectation`: ENEL → electricidad, METROGAS → gas), from issue day through due + 20 days (gastos comunes: issue + 35); a transfer whose mail names a counterparty must be outgoing (`transfer_counterparty.direction`), and counterparty names matching the place win; several → `ambiguous` unless exactly one is already filed under a bill; none → `waiting`; `overdue` 5 days past the window. An unfiled charge → row created (`assignPurchaseToRealEstateExpense`) with consumption, in the bill's month (`utilityBillMonth`: month of the period's last day — or issue day — when on/after the 15th, else the month before), covering the months the period spans (`utilityBillMonthsCovered`). A hand-filed charge gets kWh / m³ filled when empty. Another place, kind, amount or consumption is a `conflict`, never overwritten. A bill that created/filled its row is settled (`expense_entry_id`, `settled_at`) and never followed again (a hand-deleted row is not rewritten); nothing is written before the charge exists. **Reverse check:** for a place with statements, a gastos comunes payment no bill row holds and no waiting statement could take, made since the first statement and > 10 days ago, is listed in `unbilled_hoa_payments` and printed, never filed. The step exits 1 on a conflict or an overdue bill issued in the last 75 days.
- **`mortgage.payment_receipt` v1 (migration 307, `mortgagePaymentReceiptsApply.ts`): a lender's receipt for a cuota paid online.** `{ lender, transaction_id, paid_on, amount_clp, credit_number, dividend_number, due_on, card_last4 }`, ref = message id; feeder `import:metlife-receipts` (MetLife «Pago de dividendo realizado»; `card_last4` is the feeder's declaration, the mail names no card). The cuota is charged to the BCI Lider card; the receipt plants it at once, before the Lider listing shows it. Stored once in `mortgage_payment_receipts` (lender + transaction id; differing resend = conflict) and planted in the card's open web-paste bucket the same day (batch kind `cc_lender_receipt`, merchant per lender in `LENDER_CARD_MERCHANT`: «TOKU *METLIFE HIPOTECAR»; unknown lender or card throws); `card_line` = `planted` or `on_file`. A charge, not a payment: registered in `cc_planted_charge_lines` (see the Lider feed bullet), so the listing's «TOKU *METLIFE HIPOTE» of the same day is skipped as the same purchase; the statement's «TOKU *METLIFE HIPOTECAR (T)» of the same pesos and day settles it (`reconcileOpenWebPasteAfterPdfClose`). **Feeder (`ingest/src/metlife/`):** `import:metlife-receipts` (`--days=` default 45, `--dry-run`; hourly `--days=3`, nightly default) via `archiveMails`, one request per mail. Card = `paid_with_card_last4` in untracked `cfraser/metlife-fetch.json` (also `rut`, Keychain `nw-tracker-metlife`). **The portal (https://w3.metlife.cl) is read through `mortgage.lender_statement` (next bullet).** `fetch:metlife -- --capture [--code-from-stdin] [--minutes=N]` signs in (RUT + clave, then the «Clave Dinámica» from `accessmanagement@metlife.com`, valid 15 minutes, read by the generic `ingest/src/email/codeMail.ts`, which ClaveÚnica's wraps) and records a supervised session under `cfraser/metlife-captures/`.
- **`mortgage.lender_statement` v1 (migration 308, `mortgageLenderStatementApply.ts`, `mortgageLenderQuotes.ts`): MetLife's portal, read by the feeder.** One `read`: the credit (balance UF, last paid cuota), `pending` (min / max in UF and pesos, fee, amortization, interest, charges) and `history` (paid cuotas' parts and insurance in pesos; the pending one's insurance in UF). The loan is the one whose `lender_pattern` names the lender (`METLIFE`); none or several throw. **Quotes:** a pending cuota goes to `mortgage_lender_quotes` (lender + credit + cuota, with the loan; insurance UF from history) only when its figures differ from the latest stored row (`stored: false` otherwise; `first_seen_at` = first reading's). **Paid cuotas:** one the ledger has is cross-checked (total, principal, extra principal, interest, both insurances to the peso, payment day; for the last paid cuota the balance to 0,0002 UF); any difference is an `IngestConflict` naming every field, nothing written (the quote rolls back too). One the ledger lacks is written via `commitMortgagePayment` (origin `import`) only when it is the credit's last paid cuota, else `waiting`: lender's pesos, day, parts (extra principal given), balance = the credit's, min / max = latest stored quote of that cuota or null (nullable); refused as conflict when principal differs by > 1 peso, the balance chain (before − principal − extra at the day's UF) misses by > 0,0005 UF, the cuota has a charge with no ledger column (default interest, collection fee, other insurance), or a stored `mortgage_payment_receipts` row for its due month states another total. **Readers:** the scenario table's next row (`buildDeptoPaymentScenarioRows(ledger, nextCuotaQuoteForLoan(...))`: min/max from the quote, `quoted: true`, «bank quote» beside «next» in both renderings) and «Próximos pagos» (next cuota at the quote's UF, `estimated: false`; later ones projections). **Schedule (`ingestRunPolicy.ts`):** `decideMetlifeRead` → `metlife_read` on either run kind: `posted` while a receipt of the last 10 days names a cuota due after the ledger's last; else `quote` from 07:00 Chile on the 11th through month end while no stored quote is due next month or later; never within 60 min of the last run that asked (`ingest_runs.metlife_request`), never without a MetLife loan. The 11th gets an extra hourly slot at 07:00 (`metlifeQuoteSlotAtOrBefore`, `extraHourlySlot` of `decideIngestRun`) while the quote is missing, because the bank posts the cuota overnight.
- **The card page's paste box goes through the ingest service.** `POST /api/accounts/:id/imports/cc-web-paste` checks the account is a card master, sends the text to `POST /parse/card.web_paste` (`ingest/src/cards/webPaste.ts`; the server's `ccWebPasteParse.ts` keeps the issuer's sign rule) and imports the answer: a `card.pasted_listing` (`server/contracts/src/pastedListing.ts`; parse-only, not an ingest kind, because a paste names no card). Lines keep the merchant and signed amount as printed; the server normalizes the merchant (`webPasteLinesFromPastedListing`), applies the issuer's sign rule and builds the canonical card line (`cardUnbilledMovementsApply` / `cards/lineWrites.ts`), like every listing source. Needs the service running (400 with the reason otherwise). `liderCardLines.ts` keeps the Lider master lookup and the same-day-same-amount check grocery receipts use.
- **Server tasks and the inbox pipeline.** Run steps that work on the server's own data are server tasks, `POST /api/ingest/tasks/<task>` (`contracts/tasks.ts`, `server/src/ingestTasks.ts`, ingest access rules): `cc_payment_mirrors` (`dry_run`), `synthetic_cc_payments_check`, `cc_bank_cupo_check` (`recheck`), each `{task, ok, report}`. `npm run server-task -w nw-tracker-ingest -- <task>` calls one; root `convert:cc-payment-mirrors`, `check:synthetic-cc-payments`, `check:cc-bank-cupo` point there. `import:cfraser-inbox` is an ingest command (`ingest/src/inbox/pipelineMain.ts`, manifest `inbox/organizeManifest.ts`): loads the root `.env` (qpdf reads PDF passwords from it), runs the ingest commands in order and asks for the tasks — it never opens the database. Market data is synced by the server's scheduler; `npm run sync:all -w nw-tracker-server` is a manual tool.
- **`card.statement` v3: one facturación of a card account.** `{ account: {issuer, number}, statement_number, close, pay_by, next_close, titular_last4, apply, statements: [{currency, document, billed_total, payments_total, lines}] }`; lines are the canonical card line (`server/contracts/src/cards/canonicalLine.ts`, which every card document source converts into once, at its boundary; a line-shape change means ingest and server deploy together): `{kind: purchase | installment | charge | payment | credit_note | credit, transaction_date, posting_date, merchant, amount (debt-positive; installment = its cuota), origin_amount, country, place, card_last4, authorization_code, installment: {number, count, cuota_amount, total_amount} | null, raw_text}`. Ingest (`ingest/src/santander/statementJson.ts`) decodes the Santander JSON — fixed-point amounts, the CodTxs sign map (an unknown code is refused; 071 SERVICIO USO INTERNACIONAL and 701 its IVA are positive). **International rows take direction from their code (`INTERNATIONAL_COD_TXS`):** 2000 / 2003 purchases, 2020 ABONO DE DIVISAS (credit), 2510 NOTA DE CREDITO, 0067 MONTO CANCELADO (payment); don't trust `MontoTransaccion`'s trailing sign alone — the bank has printed a payment with `+`; unknown codes refused. The contract rejects positive payment / credit / nota lines and negative purchase / installment / charge lines. Ingest pairs by extracto, selects and moves archives; `import:santander-statements` sends one facturación at a time, oldest first per account. The server (`cardStatementApply.ts`, `cardStatementCrossCheck.ts`) owns ownership, the PDF cross-check, the stale-echo and routing guards, the write and its verification, and answers each side's outcome (`clean | written | pending | empty | skipped | dirty`), on which ingest archives. **A JSON-owned close is rewritten only when it changed:** the open facturación is resent nightly until its PDF arrives; both currencies are compared with the stored `import_fingerprint` (`filterUnchangedStatementRecords`); unchanged → `clean`, nothing written; any change rewrites both currencies.
- **`merchant.purchase_document` v1: App Store receipts and subscription notices → which app each charge paid for.** `fetch:apple-mail` (`ingest/src/email/appleMail.ts`, All Mail, message-id ledger, 45 days, `--days=`) stages receipts («Your receipt/invoice from Apple.») and subscription mails (confirmation, renewal, expiring, price increase) as HTML under `cfraser/apple-mail/`; `import:apple-mail` decodes them (`ingest/src/apple/appStoreMail.ts`: items with app / product / price / renews / period / artwork, total, card; a notice's app, plan, price, period, purchase / next-charge / expiry dates; a digest → several notices; anything unrecognised throws and stays staged), one source per mail. Stored (migration 204: `merchant_document_sources` — same payload duplicate, different conflict — `merchant_receipts` + items, `merchant_subscription_notices`). The matcher (`deriveMerchantChargeLinks`, `merchantExpenseNotes.ts`) names every App Store charge **when the expense lines are built**, as a link like a processor receipt (`payment_receipt` via `withPaymentReceipts`; «→ app» from a receipt, «≈ app» inferred), never as a note: (1) a receipt pairs 1:1 with the charge on its card of the same amount (peso origin to the peso, or dollars at the day's rate within 4 % for dollar-only feed rows), −1…+5 days, exact amounts then nearest date; a product-only item («1 Boost») takes its app from another receipt with the same artwork or a notice whose plan it is, else it is reported and its charge left alone; (2) a subscription's price on its cycle (anchor ± whole periods, ±3 days) within 500 days of a subscription receipt's charge or a notice; (3) a charge a month after a named charge of the same amount — its link, else a user note (never seeded by a one-off purchase). Kinds 2–3 are guesses (`guess: true`) and wait 5 days so a late receipt can claim the charge; several candidates → the nearest. The page shows the printed app name; the short label (earlier charges' notes, else the name before the tagline) only decides which notes are redundant: `npm run match:app-store-notes -- --prune-notes [--apply]` deletes notes equal to the link's label and appends « · label» to a kept note on a receipt-named charge that lacks it. Nightly: fetch with the mail, import after the card feed; hourly: import when a fetch staged mail.
- **`card.parsed_statements` v2: every card statement the PDF parser read.** Columnar `{full, columns, rows}`: the 50 columns of `cc-statements-parsed-all.csv` (`CARD_PARSED_STATEMENT_COLUMNS`) once, then each line's cells. `parsedStatementRowsToCanonical` (`server/contracts/src/cards/parsedStatementRows.ts`) is the one reader of those cells (Chilean numbers, ISO days, line kind); ingest converts before sending, the server converts what it still receives with the same function. Ledger identities (`dedupe_key`, `row_id`) ride in the canonical line's `document` provenance. `import:cc-statements` (`ingest/src/cards/`; `--dry-run`, `--full`, `--csv=`) sends the CSV; the inbox pipeline calls it after `parse:cc-pdfs`. Server: `applyParsedCcStatements` (`ccParsedStatementsApply.ts`) routes each line by `card_last4` through the registry's redirects, filters unchanged statements and merges each account's changed ones in one `mergeCcAccountFromParsedRows`; a line no account takes is a problem and nothing is written. `import:cc-parsed` stays for manual runs (`--account-id`, `--santander`) and reloads (`--wipe`, `--replace-ledger`), on the same merge. The parser, inbox organizer, qpdf repair / OCR and tests live in `ingest/python/` (scripts `parse:cc-pdfs`, `organize:inbox`, `repair:cc-pdfs-qpdf`, `restore:cc-corrupt-pdfs`, `check:cc-parse`, `test:python`; root scripts delegate), with their own `python/.pdf_deps/`; `statement_values.py` / `cartola_pdf_kind.py` have one copy each; line rules live in `server/contracts/data/ccStatementLineRules.json` (`CC_STATEMENT_LINE_RULES_PATH`). The account page's statement upload asks the service to parse each PDF (`POST /parse/card_statement.pdf` → `card.parsed_statements`; service must run). The parser fails when its source folder holds no PDF — never fall back to `~/Downloads`: it renames what it reads.
- **`pension_account.certificates` v1: AFP UNO, read from its private site.** `fetch:afp-uno [-- --apply --background]` (`ingest/src/afpUno/fetchMain.ts`) signs in at www.uno.cl (RUT + clave from `cfraser/afp-uno-fetch.json` / Keychain `nw-tracker-afp-uno`; the page's invisible reCAPTCHA makes its token; a refused attempt is reloaded once; «UNOnline» opens a drawer whose fields are readonly until clicked, so each is clicked then typed), reads what the home page asks the portal API for — the stated balance (`productos-voluntarios/consulta`) and recent movements (`afiliado-movimientos/movimientos-portal`, CCO) — and requests, with the page's own headers, both certificates as base64 PDFs: contributions (`afiliado-certificado/cotizaciones`, 12 períodos with fecha caja) and movements (`afiliado-certificado/movimientos-pdf`, last 12 months, cuotas by período). `certificates.ts` reads both from `pdftotext -layout` (wrapped rows, glued columns; an unaccounted row throws). The server (`pensionAccountCertificatesApply.ts`) builds the ledger rows in the shape `pensionLedgerShape.ts` gives (see «The AFP account's ledger and price»): one row per day a printed valor cuota first shows in `afp_uno_cuota_a`, every certificate row at that price netted into it (contributions, ones the unemployment insurance paid — 111138, which the home list never shows, so the certificate is always read — commissions, reliquidaciones), pending while the price is missing; each contribution must also be on the contributions certificate; a withdrawal is its own row, never written here (matched when present). Missing rows are written only when nothing disagrees — a ledger row in the window the certificate omits, a present row with other pesos, or a total differing from the stated balance (to 4 decimals) writes nothing and fails the step. **Stated pesos vs the app (`checkPensionStatedValue`):** the app's value (`accountMarkClpAtYmd` plus rows the read would write) on the day that valor cuota shows must equal UNO's pesos to 1 peso, else the read fails (`value_check`: match / mismatch / waiting while the price is missing). The ledger rebuild puts cuota rounding residue on the last row before a transfer-in so this holds. Every read is recorded in `pension_account_reads` (migration 203). **Schedule:** the nightly carries `afp_uno_fetch` (`decideAfpUnoFetch`). The read first sends the home balance as **`pension_account.balance`** (`applyPensionAccountBalance`): cuotas equal to the ledger's (4 decimals) → value check only; different → the certificates are read and sent (`--certificates` forces it). Writes need `NW_TRACKER_AFP_UNO_APPLY=1` in the ingest plist. PDFs are kept (`cfraser/afp-uno-certs/<issued>-cotizaciones.pdf` / `-movimientos.pdf`) only when rows were written from them.
- **`card.unbilled_movements` v1: the Santander card feed.** `ingest/src/santander/cardFeed.ts` decodes `card-movements-*.json` (dates, both `Importe` conventions, D/H → debt-positive, SALDO INICIAL → `close {date, billed {clp, usd}}`, cuota types → `cuota_purchase.first_cuota_bills` `next_cycle` (cuota comercio) / `purchase_cycle` (precio contado), the stamp-tax count, 18-digit cupo rows → `issuer_balances`); `import:santander-movements` sends each file (ref = file name, which keys the feed close and the cupo capture), prints `details` and archives to `imported/` only once applied — the first failure stops the run, a down server included (`SERVER_URL`, default `http://127.0.0.1:3001`; `INGEST_TOKEN` from env or root `.env`). Server: `applyCardUnbilledMovements` (`cardUnbilledMovementsApply.ts`) maps `{issuer, number}` via `masterAccountIdForIssuerCardAccount` and each line via `webPasteLineFromCardListingLine` (`cardListingLines.ts`: debt-positive → issuer web sign via `webPasteAmountFromDebtPositive`) and invalidates each card's caches (in-process writes do not bump `data_version`). The payment-receipt path builds its planted line canonically (`santanderReceiptCardLine`), pinned to ingest's decoding of the same feed row by one literal in both suites. **Server tests never import `ingest/`** (outside the server's TS root): canonical payloads come from `server/src/test/cardListingPayloads.ts`; bank decoding is tested in `ingest/` (`npm run test -w nw-tracker-ingest`, no DB).
- **`card.unbilled_movements` from Lider BCI's site (`ingest/python/lider_bci_fetch.py`, `ingest/src/lider/`).** The site's login sits behind a Cloudflare Turnstile box that Playwright never passed; the fetcher uses **Scrapling**'s stealth browser (`StealthySession(solve_cloudflare=True)`, Python 3.13 venv `ingest/python/.scrapling-venv`, gitignored; `patchright install chromium` after creating it), types RUT + clave (Keychain `nw-tracker-lider`, config `cfraser/lider-fetch.json`) and sends the form only when the Turnstile token is issued, both fields hold what was typed and the button is enabled — the bank blocks the clave after 3 failures. A login that stays on `/login` latches only when the page states a wrong credential (`classify_login_failure`, `ingest/python/lider_login_failure.py`, wording unverified: add the real text when seen); it then writes `cfraser/.lider-login-rejected.json` and every later run refuses until the Keychain item is newer (or `--force`, by hand only), while any other failure (the site's generic «Ha ocurrido un error», unknown text) saves diagnostics, exits non-zero and writes no latch; a Turnstile box that never issues a token writes `cfraser/.lider-turnstile-failed.json` and exits 3, and later runs refuse for 20 h unless `--retry-turnstile` (the nightly's one daily retry, which never overrides the rejected-login latch) or `--force`. `fetch:lider-bci` (default «feed» mode) opens the movements page and stages the bank's raw answers as `cfraser/lider-card-feed/lider-card-movements-<stamp>.json`: `movfacturarpesos` (the complete «por facturar» list in pesos), `movfacturardolar` (after clicking «Internacionales»), `consultasaldos` (cupo in pesos and dollars, dollars with a decimal comma) and the plastic's last4 from `consultadatoscabecera` (all on `api-ssff.retailcard.cl`); `--capture` records every answer and screenshots under `cfraser/lider-captures/`, `--form-only` sends nothing. `import:lider-movements` (`--dry-run`) decodes (`liderCardFeedPayload`: dd/mm/yyyy dates, merchant whitespace collapsed, `monto` already debt-positive, a non-empty `cuota` or unknown `tipo` throws) and sends one `card.unbilled_movements` per file, card `{issuer: "bci", number: <last4>}` — BCI's site shows no contract number, so `CC_ISSUERS.listing_feed.names_card_by` is `card_last4` for BCI (`contract_number` for Santander) and the batch kind `cc_lider_fetch`. No SALDO INICIAL: the listing states no close, so the feed mirror never runs for BCI. Cuotas of existing plans are re-listed at their purchase date and skip as installment overlaps. **Planted charges (migration 328, `ccPlantedCharges.ts`):** lines the app plants before the bank lists them — a Lider boleta (`cc_lider_boleta`), a lender receipt (`cc_lender_receipt`) — are registered in `cc_planted_charge_lines`, and any other listing import skips a line not on file by its own key when a registered planted line on the same card has the same currency and amount, a matching merchant and a date 0–3 days earlier (`MATCH_WINDOWS.planted_charge`; one-to-one, a planted line listed on its own day is taken first; reported as `skipped_planted_same_purchase`): the bank lists some purchases on a later posting day (a Saturday boleta of 1x.xxx listed on the Monday). **Cupo per issuer (migration 326):** `cc_bank_cupo_captures.issuer`; `judgeLatestBankCupoCapture({issuer})` judges each issuer's latest capture and the `cc_bank_cupo_check` task reports every issuer. Rehearsed on a copy of the live DB with the 2026-11-12 listing: 3 lines inserted, 5 duplicates, the boleta's posting-day twin skipped, a repeat send inserts nothing, BCI cupo: bank and app 7 pesos apart (cuota rounding). **Schedule:** the nightly runs fetch (`--retry-turnstile`) + import after the Santander feeds and before the payment mirrors and the cupo check; every hourly poll carries a `lider_fetch` request (`decideLiderPoll` in `ingestRunPolicy.ts`) unless a latch stands, Lider was tried in the last 35 minutes or the 22:00 slot is under 45 minutes away, from the feeder's `lider_state` in the run report (`ingest/src/runner/liderState.ts`, attempts in `cfraser/.lider-runner-state.json`); `ingest_runs.lider_*` (migration 327). The home server needs Python 3.10+ (MacPorts) for the venv.

## Unattended daily bank run

- **The nightly run** (the ingest service's `runNightly`, asked for by the server's scheduler at 22:00 Chile, see «Run protocol»): fetch Santander → the mail fetches → `import:cfraser-inbox` → the card feeds (Santander, Lider BCI), payment mirrors and bank checks → the mail imports → broker mail → Racional, AFP UNO, AFC, payslips when asked → `import:santander-statements`. The service must be a LaunchAgent in the GUI session (`com.user.nw-tracker-ingest`), because headless Chrome is blocked upstream and the fetcher needs a window server. Personal specifics live in PARSERS.md. **Mail is read by one fetcher (`ingest/src/email/mailFetcher.ts`):** every search on All Mail uses a DST-safe `SINCE` (`searchMailSince`); the six document sources (`SANTANDER_DOCS_SOURCE`, `LIDER_BOLETAS_SOURCE`, `LIDER_STATEMENT_SOURCE`, `FINTUAL_ACCIONES_SOURCE`, `APPLE_MAIL_SOURCE`, `AFC_CARTOLAS_SOURCE`) only DECLARE the sender, the subject → document kind and each mail's documents, and `fetchMailDocuments` walks the window (`DEFAULT_MAIL_WINDOW_DAYS`), skips what the document ledger holds, writes and records; `fetch:<source>` is `email/fetchMailMain.ts <source>`. `archiveMails` (the text archives the transfer, receipt, wire and utility decoders read) lives beside it; MIME helpers are `email/mime.ts`. Staged documents go to the server through one loop (`ingest/src/stagedFiles.ts` `sendStaged`): a document the server is done with moves to `imported/` beside it, one answered `retry` or `conflict` stays.
- **The BCI Lider facturación arrives by mail: `fetch:lider-statements`** (`ingest/src/email/liderStatements.ts`) pulls the monthly «Estado de Cuenta Tarjeta Lider Bci Visa» PDF from `estadodecuenta@correo.tarjetaliderbci.cl` into `cfraser/inbox/` — the card's only automated statement path. **The ledger key is the mail date + message id, NOT the attachment filename** (the santanderDocs rule inverted), because BCI reuses the same attachment name every month (a client number) and a filename key would silently skip every month after the first; the saved inbox name is date-prefixed for the same reason. The statement is the bare-number PDF attachment — the «Inserto Seguros.pdf» insert must never reach the inbox (a non-statement PDF there aborts the organizer), and any other attachment shape is an ERROR (non-zero exit), never a guess. The PDF is encrypted (`LIDER_CC_STATEMENT_PDF_PASSWORD`); the pipeline decrypts, and the organizer names it from the peeked close (`peek_bci_lider_meta`). Don't add a bare `PAGAR HASTA` fallback to that peek: it matches the anterior section's «a Pagar … Hasta» and names the statement after the previous month's close.
- **PDF/JSON statement imports never dedupe against web-paste lines.** `importCcStatementsMerge` skips a row whose key/fuzzy twin already exists on the account, but an open web-paste bucket line is NOT durable evidence for a PDF import: the same merge's `reconcileOpenWebPasteAfterPdfImports` deletes the bucket's in-period lines right after the line loop, so a skip against one would delete the purchase from BOTH places. The dedupe/fuzzy scans take `excludeWebPasteSources`, set per statement from its own source (`ccExpenseLineDedupe.ts` / `ccCrossImportDedupe.ts`); web-paste imports keep full scans, since deduping a re-paste against its own bucket and against PDF lines is their point. The BCI `monto_facturado` import check is a HARD gate (no escape hatch; `pdf_total_operaciones` is collected from a single text rendering in `cc_statement_reconcile.py`, never the concatenated dual rendering, which doubles it). The gate's identity: `parsed_operaciones + parsed_cargos_abonos = Monto Total Facturado` — BCI section-3 rows (parser layout `bci_lider_cargos`) net into cargos_abonos by LAYOUT, not the Santander merchant regex (which misses «IMPUESTO DL» and merchant-named abonos like a Lider.cl return nota); PAGOs count as mid-period payments, never cargos.
- **Santander is split by document rhythm: the web session is DAILY MOVEMENTS ONLY, the monthly PDFs come from Gmail.** `fetch:santander` runs three steps — `card-movements`, `checking-movements`, `card-statements` (the facturación **JSON**; the PDF endpoint never succeeds, and the step does not click «Ver estado de cuenta» — the page requests the PDF by itself on each view and the step only logs that answer). The statements step runs only when a statement is due (`statementSchedule.ts`): from the close the account's latest staged national JSON announces (`FechaProxFact`) until a newer extracto is staged, giving up 10 days past it (a card that stopped billing stops counting), and always when nothing is staged; `--force`, `--capture` and `--only=card-statements` fetch anyway. The summary lists the skip as an ok step with its reason. There is no `cartola` step. Both monthly documents arrive as mail attachments from `mensajeria@santander.cl`, fetched by **`npm run fetch:santander-docs`** (`ingest/src/email/santanderDocs.ts`, same IMAP/Keychain app-password path as the broker-email detector): «Estado de Cuenta Tarjeta de Crédito» (the card facturación) and «Cartola Mensual de Cuentas.» (cuenta corriente + the cuenta vista accounts). Attachments are written to `cfraser/inbox/` under the **bank's own filenames**, which is the entire contract — the organizer recognises Santander's attachment naming (`1_<seq>_<account>_<date>_CC.pdf` cuenta corriente, `_CM` cuenta vista, `_LC` línea de crédito) and classifies `80_*` credit-card statements by peeking the PDF text. Mail costs no bank session, no 2FA and no window server.
  - **CC payment receipts register the payment the day it happens.** Santander mails a receipt for every card payment — «Pago Deuda Nacional TCR» (CLP debt, `Monto del pago`) and «Comprobante Pago (abono) de la deuda facturada en dolares» (USD debt; carries `Equivalente en pesos`, the exact checking leg, plus USD amount and fx) — and `fetch:santander-docs` stages the body text as JSON under `cfraser/santander-payment-receipts/` (message-id-keyed in the document ledger). `import:santander-receipts` (ingest decodes it, `ingest/src/santander/paymentReceipts.ts`, and sends a `card.payment_receipt`; the server applies it in `santanderCcPaymentReceipts.ts`; `check:synthetic-cc-payments` raises the overdue alarm; the inbox pipeline always runs both, the receipts after the xlsx import) does one of two things. **No bank row yet** (the daily xlsx only arrives with the nightly session): the checking → card `pago_tarjeta` transfer is **synthesized from the receipt**, dated the payment day (cross-currency shape for the dollar abono: CLP from-leg = the receipt's pesos, USD counter leg = the abono), recorded in **`santander_synthetic_cc_payment_transfers`** (migration 181, twin of the Fintual retiro table), and the card's own credit line («PAGO» / «ABONO DE DIVISAS», built through `santanderMovementRowToWebPasteLine` so it is byte-identical to the feed's row) is planted in the open web-paste bucket (batch kind `cc_santander_receipt`), so checking balance, deposits line and owed walk read the payment during the day. The nightly run then confirms instead of duplicating: the xlsx/cartola debit dedupes as `superseded_by_transfer` and stamps `confirmed_on` (both checking importers call `confirmSyntheticCcPaymentForTransferLeg`), the feed's credit line lands on the one-shot dedupe key, and the mirror converter finds no single-leg debit to pair. A synthesis unconfirmed past two business days fails the receipts step loudly. A payment on the month's last business day is synthesized like any other: the bank row that lands next month brings its posting day (see «Bank posting day»). **Bank row already imported** (a payment after the 14:00 cutoff is dated by every bank feed at the next workday, but the receipt states the REAL payment date): the receipt re-dates the matching checking debit — single-leg, exact pesos, bank date inside `bankDateMatchesTransferDate`'s posting window, across a month boundary too. **`occurred_on` moves; the bank date stays twice** — as the row's `movement_bank_postings` posting day, which the cartola checks read, and in the note, which is the xlsx note-key dedupe identity; `prunePartialMovementsSupersededByCartola` carries the payment day and the posting onto the official cartola row so the correction survives the monthly import. Unmatched receipts stay staged and retry; ambiguity (two same-amount debits in window) refuses; an unparsable receipt throws (a template change must surface).
  - **Planted payment lines are replaced by amount, not wording; card payments entered by hand (migration 232, `ccPlantedPayments.ts`, `ccManualPayments.ts`).** Every card payment line the app plants before the bank lists the payment is registered in `cc_planted_payment_lines` (line → card, `source` = the planting batch kind `cc_santander_receipt` | `cc_manual_payment`, currency, amount, paid day; cascades with its line). When a card import that is not itself a planting (feed, paste) brings a credit line worded as a payment (the payment classifiers, or any merchant opening with PAGO / ABONO) in the same currency and amount (USD to the cent, CLP to the peso), dated from the planted day to 5 days later, and the pairing is one-to-one within the import, the bank's line replaces the planted one: assignments carried (`carryCcExpenseAssignments`), the planted line deleted, valuations re-synced from its date (`replacePlantedPaymentLinesFromImport`, inside `importCcWebPasteLines`). The bank's identical line (the receipt path's byte-identical row) dedupes on the one-shot key and is confirmed in place; several candidates either way change nothing and are reported (`planted_payments` on the import result and in `import:santander-movements`' output). The statement reconcile also settles a planted payment the statement prints under another wording (`plantedPaymentMatchesStatementLine`). A payment made at a branch or from the dollar account, with no receipt mail, is recorded by hand: `npm run cc:manual-payment -w nw-tracker-ingest -- --from=<id> --card=<id> --amount=<n> --currency=clp|usd --date=YYYY-MM-DD [--transfer=<movement id>]` (`POST /api/ingest/tasks/cc_manual_payment`) writes the `pago_tarjeta` transfer (or adopts an existing one) in the source account's own currency (cross-currency refused), plants «PAGO» / «ABONO DE DIVISAS» (batch kind `cc_manual_payment`) and records it in `cc_manual_payments`; running it twice changes nothing. The bank's replacing or identical line stamps `confirmed_on`; one unconfirmed 5 Chile business days after its day fails `check:synthetic-cc-payments`.
  - **The daily checking «ultimos movimientos-Cuenta Corriente.xlsx» is imported by the inbox pipeline.** `fetch:santander`'s `checking-movements` step drops it in `cfraser/inbox/`; `import:cfraser-inbox` hands it to ingest (`import:checking-movements`: decoded by `ingest/src/santander/checkingMovements.ts`, sent as `bank_account.movements`, applied by `applyBankAccountMovements` — the same apply the UI upload reaches through the ingest service's `/parse`) and ingest archives it date-stamped under `cfraser/checking-ultimos-movimientos/imported/`. **Rows dated after today are correct data**: Santander's bank day ends at **14:00**, so a wire after the cutoff posts on the next workday — the xlsx shows that forward posting date, the monthly cartola repeats it, and the cartola import dedupes against the incremental row. Never "fix" the date to the calendar day of the fetch.
  - **The xlsx must name the peso account (`checkingUsd.ts`).** The peso decoder refuses a workbook whose headers are not the «($)» ones (a dollar file would land in the peso account with its cents mangled) or one without the account line, and sends row 2's account number (`account.number`); the server checks it against `bank_account_numbers` (`assertListingNamesAccount`, by the listing's currency). The fetcher step `checking-usd-movements` (after the peso download; also in the hourly movements-only fetches) walks the movements page's product carousel, identifies the dollar account by the transactions request's body (`usd_checking_account_number` in `cfraser/santander-fetch.json`; Apigee `/current-accounts/transactions` on `openbanking.santander.cl`, which the recorder keeps, or the Tibco `mvtosYDeposiDocCtas` fallback — never by what is on screen) and saves request, raw response, screenshot and meta under `cfraser/santander-captures/<stamp>-usd/`. Capture only: no download, nothing to the inbox, never fails the run («USD account not reached» + diagnostics).
  - **The dollar account's feed confirms the ledger and never inserts (`ingest/src/santander/checkingUsdFeed.ts`, `server/src/usdAccountMovementsConfirm.ts`).** `import:checking-usd-movements` (ingest; `--dry-run`; nightly and hourly right after `import:santander-movements`) decodes each staged capture — the Apigee answer lists `movements` newest first: ISO `accountingDate` (the posting day) / `transactionDate`, `operationTime` HHMM, `movementAmount` and `newBalance` as fixed-width digits with two implied decimals and a trailing «-» on a negative figure, `chargePaymentFlag` D/H (must agree with the sign), `expandedCode` (the wording) + `observation` (the reference) = the description, a running `movementNumber`; a «Rec Or de Pago» credit's observation «REC OR DE PAGO <order>» is its `wire_reference`; the bank's error code, a shape change, a zero amount or balances that do not chain row to row (a row left out) throw — checks the request named the configured dollar account in USD and sends it as `bank_account.movements` in dollars, archiving the capture under `santander-captures/imported/`. The server maps a listing in any non-peso currency by its number (`bank_account_numbers` in the listing's currency; unnamed or undeclared is refused; `confirmForeignAccountMovements`) and reads it as evidence: a row a transfer leg on the account explains to the currency's coin inside the peso xlsx's posting window (`findMatchingForeignTransferLegId` over `signedNativeDeltaForAccountMovement` — the wire's booking, a `pago_tarjeta` from the dollar account, a conversion's counter leg) is `confirmed` and its bank day recorded as the leg's posting (`movement_bank_postings`); a wire credit whose booking has not landed is `awaiting_wire_booking` (sent again next run; the wire step raises the alarm); anything else is `unexplained` — never written, and the command exits 1 naming it, because every row this account can show is on file from its own document first (a card payment by hand: `cc:manual-payment`; a conversion: a transfer). Batch kind `usd_account_feed` (whatever the currency: a stored kind).
  - **The ledger key is the attachment FILENAME, not the month.** The facturación arrives as **two same-day mails** (CLP and USD), so a month-keyed ledger would mark the month done on the first and never download its sibling. The bank's filename carries its sequence number and document date, a stable per-document identity, and each month brings fresh sequence numbers: due when the mail lands, retried daily until it does, never fetched twice. `--force` ignores the ledger, `--dry-run` lists without writing, `--days=N` widens the search window (default 45).
- **Hourly e-mail poll:** the ingest service's `runHourly`, at :30 past every hour — the mail fetches (`fetch:santander-docs`, `fetch:lider-statements`, `fetch:lider-boletas`, `fetch:fintual-docs`, `fetch:apple-mail`, `fetch:emails`) and, **when a fetch staged something new this hour** (the fetches' `staged` step result), the full inbox pipeline (`import:cfraser-inbox` — so a monthly facturación imports the hour its mail lands, with the receipts import and the payment-mirror task) and the broker e-mail imports (`import:fintual-emails` / `import:racional-emails` under the plist's apply gates). The gate also counts a photo waiting in `cfraser/grocery-receipts/inbox/` (the one input no fetch stages); the grocery stage is incremental (per-receipt import stamps). Everything else stays nightly (Racional, statement JSONs), and the nightly inbox pipeline is the unconditional retry backstop for anything staged-but-unimported. The server records it (`recordHourlyEmailRun`, `dailyRunLog.ts`) under its **own titles** («Hourly e-mail poll») — never the daily ones, which `lastDailyRunAt` reads for the 22:00 repeat skip. A quiet hour records nothing; failures record every run but only the first of the Chile day badges (`notification`) — the nightly, which runs the same e-mail steps, remains the alerting authority.
- **The hourly Santander balance poll (`bankProductSummaryApply.ts`, `ingest/src/santander/balancePoll.ts`, migration 266).** Every hourly poll logs in to Santander (`fetch:santander --balance-poll`, the Chrome window visible — the runners do not pass `--background`), sends the login's product summary (card cupos + deposit-account balances, `cruceProductosOnline`) as **`bank.product_summary`** v1, and reads only the listings the server names: the server records the summary as a cupo capture and a balance snapshot (source ref `balance-poll-<stamp>`), judges each product at once and answers `fetch` — the card feed for a card that disagrees (or a summary without card rows), the peso checking xlsx for the cuenta corriente, the dollar account's capture for Santander USD; a cuenta vista has no listing and is only reported. The summary carries no feed close, so a card is compared without one; a close the app has not seen reads as a mismatch, costs one card-feed read and settles itself. The step prints `balance poll fetch: <list|none>` (and a `differs:` line per product), which the runner reads to import what was staged (card feed + mirror converter, the USD capture, the inbox pipeline for the xlsx) and then runs `check:cc-bank-cupo -- --recheck`. **A difference that survives its listing is a notification** («Bank balance differs from the app», `ingestTasks.ts`), once per difference: a fresh mismatch notifies when the latest EARLIER observation of the same product agreed or differed by another amount (`previousBankCupoVerdict`, the deposit verdict's `previous`, both by `observed_at` so the summary and the card feed of one session are one observation); the cupo and balance checks do not fail their step on a mismatch (the nightly's included) — only a feed with no summary does. There is no catch-up or payday fetch; old `ingest_runs` rows may carry `catch-up` / `payday` requests.
- **Dividends are booked NET; the gross and the withholding tax live in `movement_dividend_details` (migration 183, writer `movementDividendDetails.ts`).** A `dividend_payout` transfer (equity → cash) carries what the broker CREDITED — the only figure the balance walk and every P/L reader need, and the only one every source agrees on. The tax year needs the gross and the tax withheld abroad (Chile credits it against the local tax on the gross), so each dividend movement may carry one satellite row: `gross_amount`, `withholding_amount`, `withholding_rate_pct`, `withholding_jurisdiction`, `tax_residency_country`, `per_share_amount`, `position_qty`, `record_date`, `pay_date`, `broker_event_id`, `source` (`racional_api` | `fintual_cartola` | `fintual_certificado` | `ibkr_statement` | `manual`), `source_ref` — every nullable field filled only when the document prints it, a rate never computed from gross and tax. The writer (`upsertMovementDividendDetail`) is the one choke point: it throws when gross − withholding is not the movement's amount (a movement booked GROSS), when two documents disagree about the same dividend, and otherwise lets a richer document replace a poorer one (`SOURCE_RANK`: statement/API > certificado > manual) while a poorer one only fills nulls; a same-rank document from another file is provenance only (no rewrite, so the nightly re-read cannot churn). **Sources:** Racional's `/users/movements/dividends` API (`DIV` gross, `DIVTAX`, `amount` net, `executionDate`, `isInterest`) — the crawl saves the raw response as `cfraser/racional-movements/dividends-<stamp>.json` on every run and pairs each new list dividend row with its record by Chile day + net (no detail click; the record carries the route id and the instrument), and `import:racional-movements` writes the breakdown on insert, on duplicates, and from the dividends file for history; Fintual's **Alpaca monthly cartola** («Cartola mensual de Acciones», `fetch:fintual-docs` → `cfraser/fintual-acciones/cartolas/`, message-id ledger because the attachment is named after the month only) whose Income section prints `Cash DIV @ <per share>, Pos QTY: <qty>, Rec Date: <d> $<gross>` plus `Div. Adj(NRA Withheld) … at 15% for tax country CHL; w8w9: w8 -$<tax>` (a dividend with no NRA line was credited in full), and the on-request **certificado de transacciones y eventos de capital** (`…/certificados/`, bruto / impuestos / neto) — `import:fintual-acciones` (ingest: `fintual/accionesDocs.ts` parsers; server: `brokerDividendStatementApply.ts`, through `broker.dividend_statement`) pairs each printed dividend with the ledger's `dividend_payout` at the printed NET within ±5 days (the DRIP-day skew every Fintual source shares) and fails the step on a dividend with no row or several; sweep interest is reported, not booked. A future IBKR statement (Dividends + Withholding Tax sections) writes the same rows. **Trap:** the «Recibiste USD $X en dividendos de T» Racional mail states the GROSS dividend while Racional credits the net after the 15% US withholding — never book the subject figure. Both Racional dividend templates are nudges (`gross_amount` on the event, never `amount`), and the crawl importer's day-sum mismatch is a **`conflict`** that fails the step (`requires_manual` stays for the by-design cash legs). Nothing reads the table on a request path yet; a tax-year rollup is a later tranche.
- **The checking xlsx download never goes through Chrome's download handling.** The SPA builds the xlsx in the page and clicks an `<a download href="data:…;base64,…">`, and Chrome 152+ crashes (SIGSEGV/SIGTRAP on CrBrowserMain) handling that data-URL download under CDP. `browser.ts` installs an init script that catches `a[download]` clicks carrying a `data:`/`blob:` href (both `a.click()` and a dispatched MouseEvent, the FileSaver style), keeps the bytes on `window.__nwInlineDownloads`, and `downloadTo` (wait.ts) races that stash against Playwright's download event and writes the file itself — the bank's filename is preserved, and a real HTTP download still takes the native path. The relaunch-once logic stays for whatever else can kill the browser.
- **The Santander session survives a Chrome crash and a homepage modal.** `runSantander` detects a gone browser (`page.isClosed()` or Playwright's closed/crashed wording), relaunches Chrome **once** per run and retries the same step before continuing (`MAX_BROWSER_RELAUNCHES`; the persistent profile keeps the bank session, so the relaunch normally skips the password login — kept at one so a deterministic crash can't become a burst of logins; a failed relaunch marks the remaining steps skipped).
