UF/FX: `backfill:bcentral-uf` (needs `BCENTRAL_*`), `backfill:yahoo-fx-usd`, `backfill:sbif-fx-eur`, from `portfolioStartYmd` (override `PORTFOLIO_START_YMD=YYYY-MM-DD`; 2015-05-01 covers the equity window).

- **Stock splits** (migration 265, `equitySplits.ts`). `equity_splits` (ticker, ex-date, num:den, source `yahoo`|`broker`|`manual`, write-once; another ratio same ex-date throws). **Prices are split-adjusted** (`equity_daily`, `equity_dividends`, `live_market_quotes` in today's share terms), so return readers read them as stored; **shares are as held** — a `stock_split` movement per holder on the ex-date (single-leg, amount 0, `units_delta` = prior shares × (ratio − 1); in `BROKERAGE_SHARE_UNITS_FLOW_KINDS`, not a deposit). Valuing a day's shares uses `equityAsTradedClose` = adjusted close × later split ratios (`computeEquityMtmClp`, `holdingPriceAt`, position meta). `recordEquitySplit` (one transaction) writes the split, rescales stored prices before the ex-date (newest first against the post-split neighbour, keeping rows a source already adjusted) and each holder's row; refuses an ex-date after Chile today; a split of the ticker within 7 days is the same split (another ratio throws). Sources: Yahoo `events=split` (`recordYahooSplits`), `POST /api/equity-splits`, `npm run equity:record-split -w nw-tracker-server -- --ticker= --ex-date= --ratio=3:1 [--apply]`. **Guard:** a price ×/÷1.6 from its neighbour with no split recorded is refused — live quote not written (fetch error, readers keep the previous close), EOD bar throws (`healIncomingEquityClose`, which also re-frames as-traded closes) — because a broker can announce a split before Yahoo adjusts. Lots: units scale, cost and date unchanged; per-broker holdings scale. **Racional's split mail** is `broker.notifications` kind `stock_split` (`units`, `units_after`); `brokerSplitNotifications.ts` records it on the mail's Chile day (source `broker`) when after ÷ before is a whole N:1/1:N and the ledger holds exactly "before" at Racional the day before, else `conflict` (nothing recorded, step exits 1). The crawl's «Evento Corporativo» row is the holder's `stock_split` within 3 days when exactly one exists, else it blocks its read.

## Instrument registry and price providers (migration 305, `marketSymbols.ts`, `priceProviders/`, `docs/demo-app-features-plan.md` §4)

- **Instrument facts are registered, never read from the ticker.** `market_symbols` ("instrument registry") per ticker: `quote_currency` (references `currencies`; NULL = index; unknown code refused), `market_kind` (`nyse` | `santiago` | `london` | `crypto24` — calendar and sessions; NYSE, Arca, American, Cboe, Nasdaq are one market), `isin`, `display_symbol` (no exchange suffix: CSPX, CFIETFIPSA, SPY — the leaf name, Racional match, Bolsa nemo), `exchange` (provenance), `name`, `instrument_type`, `source` (`yahoo`|`coingecko`|`bolsa_santiago`|`declared`), `verified_at`. `requireMarketSymbol` returns the row or throws (naming `npm run register:market-symbols -w nw-tracker-server`) after one cache reload; `equityMarketKind` / `marketQuoteCurrency` are its fields. The ticker (Yahoo spelling) stays the key everywhere. **No exchange code** (migration 306 dropped `mic`): `market_kind` is the fact; holding declarations to Yahoo's venue label breaks the demo-market publish.
- **`registerInstrument(ticker, facts, source)`** upserts and refreshes the cache; **throws if `equity_daily` has bars in another currency, or a re-registration changes market kind or currency of a ticker with bars** — a stored series is never reframed.
- **Providers are adapters** (`priceProviders/types.ts` `PriceProvider`: `describeSymbol`, optional `search`, `dailyCloses`, `liveQuote`). `yahoo.ts` alone reads Yahoo symbols: `exchangeTimezoneName → market_kind`, USD/CLP rule with pence refusal, `yahooDisplaySymbol` (strips `.SN`, `.L`, `.NYB`…; keeps `BTC-USD`, `^GSPC`, `BRK.B`), `checkYahooSeriesCurrency`. `coingecko.ts` / `bolsaSantiago.ts` wrap `equityCoinGeckoEod.ts` / `equityBolsaSantiagoEod.ts` (daily closes; the Bolsa describes nothing). `priceProviders/index.ts`: `describeSymbol(symbol, provider="yahoo")`, `priceProvider(id)`, `registerInstrumentFrom`, `ensureRegisteredInstrument`.
- **Doors that register:** panel account create (`registerPanelAccountInstrument` before `createPanelAccount` — stock from Yahoo, coin from CoinGecko, option underlying from Yahoo; `createPanelAccount` refuses unregistered tickers, so broker-mail importers mark such buys `requires_manual`), watchlist add (`addManualWatchlistTicker`), the Risky Norris composition sync (each `price_ticker`), migration 305 (SPY), and the demo (`declareDemoInstruments` from `DEMO_TICKERS`, `source: "declared"`, before any account; synthetic path never asks a provider; `loadDemoRealMarket` fails unless Yahoo's `describeSymbol` agrees on market, currency, display symbol; `market_symbols` is re-declared, not carried in the asset). EOD/live syncs throw on unregistered tickers. **`npm run register:market-symbols -w nw-tracker-server -- [--only=] [--apply]`** (`instrumentRegistrationPlan.ts`, report-first): lists unregistered tickers in use and provider rows whose market/currency/display symbol changed (CoinGecko for known coins, else Yahoo; `declared` never re-verified), writes all-or-nothing, fails naming unclassifiable/refused tickers. **`scripts/server-deploy.sh` runs it `--apply` after the client build, before restart** (failure rolls back). Importing `db.ts` migrates the DB it opens — rehearse via `NW_TRACKER_TEST_DB`. Vitest: `src/test/instrumentFixtures.ts` (`declareTestInstrument`, `VITEST_INSTRUMENTS` via `vitest.setup.ts`).

## Options (contracts as accounts)

- **A listed option series is its own account at intrinsic value** (migration 304, `optionContracts.ts`, `optionPosition.ts`, `optionFlowKinds.ts`, `optionAssignment.ts`; `docs/demo-app-features-plan.md` §3). `option_contracts` (account → underlying, right C|P, strike, expiry, multiplier 100, OCC symbol); `accounts.equity_ticker` is NULL (EOD sync, watchlist, `accountsWithEquityTicker` never see it); the profile picks `QuantitySource {kind: "option_contracts"}` BEFORE the share branch; holding `{kind: "option", …, quote: "usd"}` (non-dollar underlying throws). Contracts are SIGNED (short = −n): `unitsDeltaForAccountMovement` per kind (`option_sell_open`/`_sell_close` −|n| on the from-leg, `option_buy_open`/`_buy_close` +|n| on the to-leg, single-leg `option_expire`/`option_assign` −held as stored), `optionContractsThroughDate`, `qtyMarkNativeValue` × multiplier — a short's value is NEGATIVE (`positionMarkFromMeta`, `units_kind: "contracts"`). **Price = intrinsic from the underlying's close** (`holdingPriceAt`: as-traded past close, today's price today; `max(0,S−K)` call, `max(0,K−S)` put, 0 from expiry); time value is not priced (no contract history; stated on the page); a missing underlying close THROWS and no ladder falls back to a book mark. Closed/expired/assigned = 0, chart-inactive. `option_contracts` is a mark input (`OPTION_CONTRACTS_SPEC`, whole-history revalue); `option_assignments` links an assignment's two rows.
- **Flow kinds** (`OPTION_UNITS_FLOW_KINDS`, in `BROKERAGE_FLOW_KINDS`, `BROKERAGE_NON_CASH_FLOW_KINDS`, the reconciliation's non-capital list, `intraDayFlowRank`, client `brokerageFlowKinds.ts`): `option_sell_open` (contract → USD cash, −n), `option_buy_open` (cash → contract, +n), `option_buy_close`, `option_sell_close`, `option_expire` (single-leg, amount 0, −held) and `option_assign` (single-leg, −held) + a `stock_sell` at the strike (`stock_buy` for an assigned put; exercised long reversed), linked in `option_assignments`, written together by `assignOptionContract` (`POST /api/accounts/:id/option-assignments`; a bare `option_assign` is refused). `validateBrokerageTransferEndpoints`: premiums settle in USD cash, whole contracts, direction fits that day's position (open never closes the other side, close never past zero, nothing after expiry); `option_expire` must reach exactly zero on/after expiry. Forms: `STOCK_ACCOUNT_FLOW_KINDS` vs `OPTION_CONTRACT_FLOW_KINDS` (units required, «contratos»).
- **A premium is capital** (`equityBrokerageCapitalFlows.ts`): received = capital out of the contract (`dividend_payout` mirror), paid = in (`stock_buy` mirror), at the day's USD reference rate; the USD-cash loader mirrors it negated so they cancel in the bucket; expiry/assignment move no money. So value − aportes is the position's P/L (fixture `optionContracts.test.ts`). **`loadForeignCashCapitalSortFlows`'s generic branch THROWS on an unlisted kind** (`USD_CASH_GENERIC_CAPITAL_FLOW_KINDS`: kind-less transfer, cash deposits/withdrawals, conversions, `other`, `pago_tarjeta`, `carga_tarjeta`); a new instrument's leg needs its own rule, never "deposit".
- **Creating:** `POST /api/accounts` `account_type: "option"`, `option: {underlying, right, strike, expiry, multiplier?}`: underlying must quote USD; OCC symbol derived (`occSymbol`); default name «SPY 2025-06-20 C 600»; leaf `brokerage_opciones__<occ slug>` under `brokerage_opciones` (kind_slug `options`, `/inversiones/brokerage/opciones`, seeded by `seedNavTree`, hidden when empty); `import_key` `import:panel|option=<occ>|key=<slug>`; never moves buckets. **Tax lots** (`taxLots.ts`, `optionTaxLotEvents.ts`): short lots (`open_short`/`close_short`, own pool; `realizeTaxLots` returns `shortCloses`, `openShortLots`; `dispose` with no long lot throws — shorts are declared, never inferred; `taxLotTerm`: short always short-term §1234(b), long long-term after > 1 year). `optionTaxLotEventsFromRows` / `loadOptionTaxLotEvents` map sell-open → open_short, buy-close → close_short, expiry → close_short/dispose at 0, buy-open → acquire, sell-close → dispose; `optionLotResults` gives slices. Assignment/exercise has no gain of its own; the premium adjusts the share trade (Pub. 550: short call +proceeds, short put −cost, long call +cost, long put −proceeds) via `optionAssignmentAdjustments(stockAccountId)` into `equityTaxLotEventsFromRows` / `loadEquityTaxLotEvents` (optional input). Still open: the Schedule D / F1040 wiring that reads these results (no report calls them yet), the demo's option events, a live chain price. **Their tax:** lots reach Form 8949 / Schedule D via `us1040/wired.ts` (assignment premium into the share's basis/proceeds, 1040 equity walk only); on the F22 an option movement in a Chilean-resident window throws (Ley 20.544 not modelled, `assertNoOptionsInResidentWindow`, `f22/blocks.ts`), in a non-resident window it is `excluded` (`options`, `nonresident`, `taxFacts/options.ts`).

- **Demo options wheel** (`demoData/optionsWheel.ts`, `simulate.ts` `runWheel`, `generate.ts` `createOptionContractAccounts`, `invariants.ts` `checkOptionsWheel`). At IBKR from 2025-01, every third month (15th or next NYSE day), as US resident: cash-secured put ~5 % below close, or covered call ~5 % above while holding 100 assigned shares, on the monthly expiry nearest 45 days (Thursday if Friday closed); premium = 0,04 % of strike per day to expiry — story assumptions, no pricing model. Underlying = on the first securable sale day, the largest NYSE dollar ticker of the broker list (SGOV excluded) whose 100 shares fit the collateral (cash + SGOV reserve) — never hardcoded; an unsecurable put is not written. Outcome = close on expiry (put assigned below strike, call above), never forced. Written through the real model: `Ledger.optionContracts`, `option_sell_open` series → IBKR USD, `option_expire` rows, `DirectMovement` `option_assign` → `assignOptionContract` in date order (SGOV sold first if cash is short); series accounts created per pass after `simulate` (the reset drops them). Assigned shares are IBKR's holding of the ticker's ONE stock account (`heldUnits` keyed `ticker|cash`). Invariants: 0 contracts from expiry, −1 from sale, premiums/legs equal the story to the cent, one 100-share link per assignment, IBKR dollar cash never negative.

## Quote currency / Santiago (`.SN`) tickers

- **`equity_daily` stores `close` + `currency` ('usd' | 'clp')** (migration 152); `live_market_quotes` `kind='equity'` + `currency`. Writers stamp `equityQuoteCurrency(ticker)` (`equityQuote.ts`, from the registry; unregistered throws); readers **throw on mismatch** — fix rows, don't coerce.
- **Bolsa de Santiago instruments are registered `santiago` / `clp`** (the suffix decides nothing): session = Chile day (live ≈ 09:30–17:05 on `isChileBusinessDay`, never weekday-only); EOD display = latest bar ≤ Chile today.
- **MTM**: clp-quoted → `units × close`, **no fx** (`computeEquityMtmClp`, `equityBrokeragePositionMeta`, `ccInvestmentProxy`; `marketSeries` derives `equity_usd = close / clp_per_usd`). USD composites throw on a clp ticker (`watchlistComposite`).
- **EOD sync is its own source, `stocks_santiago`, on the Chile calendar** (NYSE's calendar misses US holidays that are Chilean business days): `equityEodSantiagoSyncDue` (`equityEodSync.ts`) due **17:10 Chile on business days**, **carries over** until the last closed session's bar is in (`isStocksSantiagoStale`, `equitySantiagoEodCaughtUp` over `listWatchlistSantiagoTickersForEodSync()`); series capped at the due session (a forced early run never stores the in-progress bar); 3-min poll only on the close evening (carry-over days 15 min, so an unlisted holiday can't hammer Yahoo). `stocks_nyse` syncs NYSE only; `account_sync_sources` links `.SN` accounts. Keep `CHILE_CLOSED_YMD` current or a closure leaves the source stale.
- **CLP-funded trades**: `stock_buy`/`stock_sell` on clp stocks transfer against **CLP cash** in pesos (no USD leg; `validateBrokerageTransferEndpoints`); `clp_wire` capital flows; the CLP-cash leg feeds aportes (`accountDeposits`). `dividend_payout` is USD-only (throws for clp).
- Client: **`equity_quote_currency`** on summary/detail-bundle drives the stock form (`buildBrokerageMovementPostBody(row, quote)`, `brokerageFlowKindNeeds{Clp,Usd}ForQuote`), never the suffix.
- Yahoo has **no `CFIETFIPSA.SN` history before 2026-07-02**; lookups tolerate gaps. Older: `npm run backfill:bolsa-santiago-eod -w nw-tracker-server` (Bolsa point-history API, ~10y, capped at listing; `equityBolsaSantiagoEod.ts`).

## London market (LSE) tickers

- **`london` is the fourth `EquityMarketKind`** (`londonSession.ts`, migration 303, `docs/demo-app-features-plan.md` §2), from the registry (`equityMarketKind` throws for unregistered tickers whatever the suffix; Yahoo adapter maps `Europe/London`). Every per-market reader is a `switch` ending in `unknownEquityMarketKind` — never a `santiago ? … : nyse` default, which silently prices a new market on New York sessions: live gate (`shouldUseLiveEquityQuote`), held print (`equitySessionClosedForDay` / `equityHeldSessionQuote`), EOD resolver (`resolveLondonEodQuote`), `equitySessionYmdForTicker` / `equityDisplaySessionYmd`, day-change calendar (`tickerDayDisplay.ts`, 0 on UK bank holidays), Yahoo bar labelling (`yahooBarYmdFromUnix`, Europe/London — bars stamped at 08:00), stream/poll gates (`isEquityRegularSessionOpen(kind, now)` for `streamTickInSession`, `subscribedMarketOpen`, `pricedSessionOpenPredicate`), watchlist 1W anchor (`londonSessionsBack(asOf, 5)`), daily `market_day` (any of NYSE/Santiago/London), live prior close (`priorCloseForLiveSession` + `priorLondonSessionYmd`). The synthetic demo market skips non-LSE days.
- **Clock/calendar:** `londonWallClock` (memoized per UTC minute); session **08:00–16:30** on LSE days (`isLondonRegularSessionOpen`, `isAfterLondonRegularClose` from 16:31, `londonDisplaySessionYmd`); `LSE_CLOSED_YMD` 2023–2027 in `marketHolidays.ts` = England & Wales bank holidays per gov.uk (substitute days included); `isLondonTradingDay` / `priorLondonSessionYmd` / `londonSessionsBack`. **24/31 Dec half days are modelled as full sessions.**
- **Quote currency is the registered one and every London write checks Yahoo's** (`checkYahooSeriesCurrency`), because the LSE lists the same fund in pence, dollars and euros: `stocks_london` and the demo loader refuse a mismatched `meta.currency` before storing; `classifyYahooSymbol` refuses pence and codes `currencies` lacks. Prices convert out of the quote currency via `rates.ts` (`holdingPriceAt` → `convertValue`). Still peso/dollar-only and throwing on others: a stock trade's cash leg (`movementUnitsPolicy.ts`), a broker split's cash account, the proxy basket (USD), art. 107 (CLP), option underlyings (USD), `equity_dividends`' CHECK (Phase B).
- **`stocks_london` source** (`syncSourceIds.ts`, `SYNC_SOURCES` in `globalSyncStale.ts`, i18n `importSync.sync.sources.stocks_london`): due from **16:40 London** on LSE days (`LONDON_EOD_SYNC_AFTER_HOUR_LONDON`/`_MINUTE_LONDON`, `isLondonEodSyncWindow`, `equityEodLondonSyncDue`), carries over until caught up (`equityLondonEodCaughtUp` over `listWatchlistLondonTickersForEodSync`; `keepsForcedWhileStale`), day kind `londonDayKindAt`, `stocksLondonNextSync` (`SyncWallTime.timeZone` carries the IANA zone to the client). `syncStocksLondonFromYahoo` = Santiago shape (one request, capped at the due session) + NYSE dividends/splits (`fetchYahooRecentDailyClosesWithDividends`; distributing lines record `withholding 0, jurisdiction IE`). Fast poll only on the close evening (`pollIntervalMsForStaleSources`); `inferSyncSourcesForAccount` links `.L` accounts; `scheduleMeta` reads each source's own `dayKind`. Listed in `syncRunLog.ts`, `globalSyncState.ts`'s legacy split, `/api/sync/force-stale` `equity_eod` alias. Tests `londonMarket.test.ts`, fixture `test/londonTickerFixture.ts`.
- **Demo:** IBKR opens 2023-05 as Chilean resident (`SecondBroker.from`, `ucits: { tickers: ["CSPX.L","VWRA.L"], monthlyUsd: 1_000, sellAllOn: "2024-03-12" }`, `usFrom: "2024-05"`): US$1.000/month from dollar checking (outside the peso cushion's gates) buys both lines in halves on the LSE day on/before the broker day; sold all 2024-03-12 (PFIC warning before the move; the gain flows through `foreignShareGainsForYear(2024)` to AT2025 1104); proceeds idle in the sweep; SGOV reserve + SPY from 2024-05 (US$2k idle cushion). `DEMO_TICKERS` carries both from 2023-05-01 (`EQUITY_ANCHORS`; short-rate table from 2023); leaves named by `display_symbol`. **Render refuses a market asset built for less than `demoMarketSpec()`** — publish a new asset from the home server before the next demo release when the spec grows (`docs/demo-market-publish-plan.md`).

## Deferred: value + currency refactor

**Goal:** cut per-currency *code branching* with `amount` + `currency` splits where they propagate through readers — not storage uniformity. A definitionally-CLP DTO field like `value_clp` is fine; the target is DB storage and parallel `_clp`/`_usd` branches. Precedent: `equity_daily` / `live_market_quotes` (migration 152) — select `value` + `currency`, **throw on unexpected currency**. Migrate opportunistically; no big-bang renames.

- **`valuations` — done** (migration 154; 260 any currency). A row is a quantity in its `currency`: generic readers convert once on its date at the account's side (`storedValuationInUnit`), kind readers assert (`assertValuationCurrency(code, expected, ctx)`, `valuationValue.ts`). Display units are any core currency (`docs/any-currency-plan.md`; remaining fixed storage in its «Remaining work»).
- **`movements` — done** (migration 169): `amount` + `currency`, `counter_amount`/`counter_currency` = to-leg of cross-currency transfers (fx_exchange: CLP from-leg, foreign counter; CHECK forbids counter on single-leg rows and equal currencies). `counter_units_delta` (migration 242) = to-leg units when both sides move units (AFP fund switch), read only through `transferToLegUnits` / `transferFromLegUnits` (`movementTransfer.ts`); null = to-leg takes `units_delta`. Signs: single-leg CLP signed, transfers positive, single-leg USD positive with direction in `flow_kind`. Leg semantics ONLY in **`movementAmounts.ts`**: `movementClpLegOrZero`/`movementUsdLeg` (legacy view, 0/null when absent), `requireMovementClp` throws on CLP-only surfaces (mirror pairs, cartola, depto), `MOVEMENT_CLP_LEG_SQL`/`MOVEMENT_USD_LEG_SQL` (alias-qualify when aliased). Create API takes `amount`+`currency` (+counter); single-leg fx_exchange rejected. Flows DTO rows carry native fields (CLP = signed per-account delta, USD raw); FlowsTable one Monto column («$X → US$Y»); XLSX monto_clp/monto_usd derived. Old-name exceptions: `legacyNoteBackfills.ts` (157/161 hook runs before 169 on fresh DBs) and SQL `AS amount_clp` CLP fields. **Rebuilding an FK-referenced table needs foreign keys OFF** (`FOREIGN_KEYS_OFF_MIGRATIONS`, db.ts): with FKs on, `DROP TABLE` cascade-deletes children. Money tables' currency columns `REFERENCES currencies(code)` (migration 314), so a new currency is a `currencies` row (market tables and `crypto_trade_details` coins keep their own lists).
- **Cash in any currency; flow kinds carry no currency** (migration 318, `cashCurrency.ts`, `foreignCashAccounts.ts`, any-currency C1). `accounts.cash_currency` (references `currencies`, mark-trigger watched): legacy `<key>__usd`/`__clp` leaves name theirs (declaration must agree); a `foreign_cash` leaf must declare (panel `account_type: "foreign_cash"` + `currency`). One reading: `accountCashCurrency` / `foreignCashCurrencyOf` / `isForeignCashAccount(In)` / `listForeignCashAccountIds(currency?)`; quantity `foreign_ledger {currency}`, converted once by `accountValueAt`. Ledger: `signedNativeDeltaForAccountMovement(row, account, currency)` / `sumNativeThroughDate` / `foreignCashBalanceAt` (rounded to the coin); events carry `amt_usd` for dollars, else `amt_native` + `native_currency` (`nativeFlowLeg`, read by `flowEventInUnit`). `HOME_CURRENCY` / `isHomeCurrency` = pesos. Kinds: `fx_exchange`, `fx_purchase`, `deposit`, `withdrawal`, `state_bonus`, `state_bonus_transfer_in`; single-leg foreign `deposit`/`withdrawal` move their own currency; labels name the movement's currencies. Flow-insert trigger re-created in order (`reinstallTableTriggersInOrder`, `tableTriggerReinstall.ts`). Still dollar-only: trades, dividends, option premiums, dollar checking listings, bid/ask (`BID_ASK_FOREIGN_CURRENCY`); the client cash form is CLP/USD only.
- **Card storage per currency (C3):** `cc_statement_lines` `amount` + `currency` + `cuota_amount` (319; legacy peso/dollar view in `cards/lineAmountSql.ts`, generated columns dropped in 324); billing-month snapshot = long `cc_billing_month_metrics(account_id, billing_month, as_of_date, as_of_kind, metric, currency, amount)` (320; derived, rebuilt per account by `recomputeCcBillingMonthBalances`; `listCcBillingMonthBalances` pivots with `facturado_by_currency` / `saldo_total_by_currency`); cupo `credit_card_cupos(account_id, currency, amount)`, feed SALDO INICIAL `cc_feed_billing_close_amounts(close_id, currency, amount)` (321; config DTO lists a cupo per issuer currency); traspaso links `from_*`/`to_*` with currencies (322). Readers use each line's own currency or `lineAmountSql.ts`: open cycle and provisional close bill per currency (`openMonthForeignFacturado`; third currency at its debt-frame rate, `debtCcExpenseLineAmountClp`); «Próximos pagos» and cupo check per currency; statement import takes the issuer's currencies (`requireCardCurrency`, `cards/cardCurrencies.ts`; a third currency reconciles by international rules). Still CLP/USD: the Santander `card.statement` JSON pipeline (`cardStatementApply.ts`), the feed SALDO INICIAL on a provisional close, listing and receipt handlers.
- **`payroll_work_earnings`** (migration 153): `liquido` + `liquido_currency` ('usd' Deel wires); DTO keeps `liquido_clp`/`liquido_usd`; usd rows' CLP = haberes − descuentos (breakdown stored at wire date, exact). Other `_clp` columns are line-item metrics and stay.

## Real-estate expenses (`/flows/expenses/real_estate`)

- **Model:** `expense_accounts` = tracked places, **fully data-driven** (no slug lists): `active_from`/`active_to` (YYYY-MM), `property_account_id` → property master (owned places), `comunidad_merchant_patterns` (comma list). Personal data, live DB only. «Añadir lugar…» (POST `…/places`); picking a property attaches mortgage rows. `places[]` drives sections, series, labels. Bills = `expense_entries` with `expense_account_id`: kind in `category` (`RealEstateBillKind`), consumption in **`kwh` / `m3`** columns. `real_estate_expense_links` ties a bill 1:1 to its paying CC/checking purchase (auto by merchant patterns + amount within bill +0/+2 months, or manual).
- **Purchase-first assign** (`POST /api/flows/expenses/real-estate/assign`): the bill is created FROM an unlinked purchase plus a manual link in one tx (never invented amounts). Pool `GET …/unlinked-purchases`: default category `bills`, place's occupancy (+2-month tail), kind-pattern matches first, multi-select. `PATCH …/entries/:id/consumption`; `PATCH …/entries/:id/bill-month` (rewrites month-end `spent_on`; refuses months putting the purchase outside +0..+2); «mes anterior» toggle (bill = purchase month − 1); `DELETE …/entries/:id` (cascades). Bulk: `npx tsx scripts/suggest-real-estate-links.ts` (report-first `--apply`; contribuciones, same-day/amount dups and odd rents review-only; never MUTUARIA/METLIFE/TOKU mortgage cash; cuota splits dedupe by purchase_key).
- **Mortgage rows are read-only ledger derivations:** kind `mortgage`, `expense_entry_id: null`, amount = what the regular cuota paid (`loan_payments`), `mortgage_parts` = scheduled amortización, extra amortization (`amortizacion_ext_clp`), carrying (interés + seguros); pie/prepagos are capital, out (user's choice); the movement's CLP `amount` is an equity mark, not cash. Chart kinds `mortgage_amortization`, `mortgage_extra_amortization` (drawn below zero — saving, not cost), `mortgage_carrying`; a point's `total` = sum of absolutes. Never stored. `bill_month` = scheduled month from cuota numbers (`mortgageCuotaBillMonths`: modal anchor of month(occurred_on) − cuotaN; throws on inconsistency); `spent_on` = real payment date («pagado» hint).
- **Billed-period framing lives here only;** `/flows/expenses` uses real dates.
- **No double counting:** `flowsManualExpenses.ts` excludes place rows structurally (`expense_account_id IS NULL`).
- **A bill exists only when a whole purchase backs it** (assign, or a bill mail paired with its charge; «Total» lines, never «Por cuota» pieces or financing splits). Repairs: `server/scripts/repair-real-estate-bills.ts --plan=<json> [--apply]` (delete/remonth/assign/cover). Electricity and HOA bills sit one month before their charge (consumption month; buildings issue next month and the receipt names the issue month). **Multi-month payments are one bill with `months_covered`** (migration 245): months end at the bill month (`covers_from`), chart spreads whole pesos (`spreadOverCoveredMonths`, last month takes remainder), table «cubre …»; set via `PATCH …/entries/:id/months-covered` or the `cover` step. Metrogas bills two months. Contribuciones stay in the month paid. A refunded payment is not a bill (keep one; mark the refund in «bills»). **Broken links are reported, never deleted on read:** `findInvalidRealEstateExpenseLinks` sets `broken_link` (`missing_purchase` | `outside_window`), page offers Desvincular; only the reconcile script deletes. Bill links ride the flow on re-import (`flows/cardLineAdoption.ts`, with rejections; never onto a sibling copy) and on cartola replacement (`adoptPartialIntoCartolaRow`, `docs/untangle-flow-identity.md`).
- **Chart by kind, per scope** (`chart_scopes`, `flowsRealEstateExpenses.ts`): blocks `all` + each place; `series` `<place>__<kind>` stacked by place (date order) then `REAL_ESTATE_CHART_KINDS` (mortgage carrying, amortization, rent, gastos comunes, electricity, water, internet, gas, contribuciones, extra amortization on top); legend order = the scope's `kinds` (client keeps no copy); unknown kind throws; monthly points first→last bill. Client counts Rango back from the scope's last point, one hue per kind alternating with a desaturated copy per place (`scheme`); a place page totals that place only. Shared component `StackedExpensesChart` (bar/line, year averages, legend hides series — never changes the Total).
- **DFL-2 places and rent credits** (migration 300, `rentalPlaces300.ts`, `f22/rentals.ts`): `expense_accounts.dfl2` + `dfl2_built_m2` (≤ 140) + `dfl2_reception_year`, set together («Añadir lugar…» or `PATCH …/places/:slug/dfl2`); halving window from `legalParameters.ts` `dfl2ContribucionesHalvingYears` (20y ≤ 70 m², 15 ≤ 100, 10 ≤ 140), served as `dfl2_contribuciones_halved_through`, never stored. Income kind `rent` (`CHECKING_INCOME_KINDS`, `FLOW_KINDS`) requires its place: `upsertCheckingIncomeMovementOverride(id, {income_kind: "rent", rent_place_slug})` (`flow_income_overrides.rent_place_id`, refused otherwise); `GET /api/income/rent-places`. F22: non-DFL-2 rent → **1849** (→ **955** → 158), contribuciones → credit **1637** capped at 1849 × round(157/170, 3) (`f22PropertyTaxCreditCap`; 304 = 157 − 136 − 1637 − 162 − 1018 − 610, `f22/identities.ts`); DFL-2 rent shown, declares nothing (Suplemento 9.3 g); a third DFL-2 rented place in a year throws. Reajuste to December via `ipcReajuste`. **152 is never negative:** net negative DJ 1890 interest goes to 169 (`f22Code158` throws on negative 152). The flows overview counts rent in its own `rent` income bucket.
- **The agency's commission is a bill row** of kind `management_fee` (`RealEstateBillKind`, `REAL_ESTATE_LINKABLE_KINDS`, `REAL_ESTATE_CHART_KINDS` after `contribuciones`, label `expenses.kinds.management_fee`), created from the checking debit (`assignPurchasesToRealEstateExpenses`); `taxFacts/rentals.ts` carries it in `bills` (Schedule E line 11). Demo: one per forwarded rent (`simulate.ts` `placeBills`).

## Expense subcategories (migration 224)

- `cc_expense_categories.parent_id`, one level (deeper throws); DTO `parent_slug`; lines always get a leaf. Chart switch «Categorías / Subcategorías» (`useCcExpenseCategoryLevel`, per browser; fold via `ccExpenseCategorySlugAtLevel`). Pickers show «Parent › Child» after the parent (`ccExpenseCategoryPathLabel`). Seeded `taxes` and `pension_fees` under `bills` (payroll deductions, `docs/payroll-plan.md`). Code-named slugs (`bills`, `deposits`, `no_cuenta`, …) stay top-level.
- **Gastos by month and category are server-built** (`flowsExpensesGastos.ts`, `GET /api/flows/expenses/gastos?unit=&exclude_big_groups=`): one view per mode × level (`<split|total>|<category|subcategory>`) in the unit; excluded big groups leave the chart only — headline totals, month/year rows (trimmed to the mode's last real spend), monthly stacks (through Por cuota's last month in both modes), unfiltered stacks for order, full-history yearly averages. Expenses page and Flows overview pick and clip to Rango. Edits show after refetch, not optimistically. **Big-group usage too** (`bigGroupUsageByMode`: `big_group_usage` per mode; `GET /api/flows/expenses/summary` `active_big_group_slugs` = default exclusion). **The client never holds every expense line (2026-11-12; `flowsExpenseLineLists.ts`).** Each list asks the server for its own lines: `GET /api/flows/expenses/lines?unit=&<selector>[&include_pre_tax=1]`, exactly one selector — `month=YYYY-MM` (the Expenses month modal; sections split on the client from each line's `month_modal_sections`), `account_id=&billing_month=` (a card's facturación modal; the rule which lines a closed / open / provisional facturación lists is `flowLinesForFacturacionMonth` on the server), `list=uncategorized|deposit_matched`, `big_group=<slug>`; pre-tax lines left out unless `include_pre_tax=1` (never with the facturación selector). `list=uncategorized` is the one paged list (`page`, `page_size`, default 1 and 10, at most 100; largest in pesos first, then newest; the response's `page` carries the total): it was the largest one (900 lines, 1,27 MB on the real data); paging params with another selector are a 400. `/summary` carries categories, big groups, `active_big_group_slugs` and the additional-card summary; `/financing-candidates` the financing manager's cards, months and purchases. Each list fetches when shown (modals while open, big groups once expanded) and never holds another selector's rows while loading; optimistic category, big-group and note edits patch every cached list (`flowsExpensesLinesAll`). The old whole-history payload (`GET /api/flows/expenses/credit-card`, ~12 MB, refetched on every live dollar tick) is gone, and no expense route carries `/credit-card` any more (`/gastos`, `/big-groups`, `/purchase-notes`, `/purchase-big-group`, `/financing-links`, PATCH `/lines/:lineId/category`). The client's `countsTowardGastosMes` only moves lines between month-modal sections during optimistic edits, pinned by `server/src/test/gastosMesCountingCases.json` (both suites).

## Manual expenses, line pieces and big groups (migrations 235–236, `expenseLinePieces.ts`)

- **Big groups belong to the expense:** `cc_expense_purchase_big_groups` keyed by `purchase_key` alone (keys are globally unique: `line-pr:`, `installment-h:<account>:…`, `checking-mv:`, `checking-cartola:<account>:…`, `manual:<id>`, `<line key>#split:<seq>`); payroll deductions take none. Categories («Único») and notes stay keyed by (account, key).
- **Manual expenses** (`expense_entries` without a place) for spending no bank/card shows: «Gasto manual» / «Editar» in the month modal; `POST /api/flows/expenses/manual`, `PATCH`/`DELETE …/manual/:id`; note keeps `manual:` prefix (line shows the rest); deleting removes its big group. **Any core currency** (migration 316, `expenseAmountCurrency316.ts`): `amount` + `currency` (→ `currencies`), rounded via `roundToCurrency`, picker from `/api/currencies`; the line carries `amount` {value, currency}, pesos/dollars at the day's rate (`convertFlow`, source `day`; peso expense keeps buy-rate dollars); edits restate flow and evidence. Place bills stay pesos by CHECK; bill readers use `placeBillPesosSql` and throw otherwise (`placeBillAmount.ts`). Legacy `income_entries` has `amount` + `currency` (read as day flows); income `amount_usd` is a credit's own dollars, null for pesos (dollars at the day's buy rate, `incomeLineUsdLeg`).
- **Line pieces** split a card/checking purchase line into lines with own day (`cc_expense_line_splits.spent_on`, null = line's day), amount, category; big group and note on the piece key. Partial coverage allowed (`pieces_remainder` stays on the line), exceeding the line throws (`expandLineSplitsInDrafts`). Purchase lines only (never a deposit-paired portion); no piece before its line. `GET`/`PUT /api/flows/expenses/line-pieces`; `seq` is the piece's key for life; removing drops its big group and note. Single-purpose withdrawals need no pieces. **Pieces are in their line's currency** (`flow_parts.amount` in `flows.currency`; pesos by share, remainder by what's left; DTO `currency` + `line_amount`); a mismatched piece throws; loan payment parts stay pesos.

## Home dashboard tables: wealth flows, latest expenses, Rentabilidad

- **«Flujos de patrimonio»** = net_worth flows without spending: `GET /api/groups/:slug/flows?exclude_spending=1` (`buildGroupFlows(…, { excludeSpending })`) drops checking movements whose every line in `loadFinalizedCheckingGastosLinesReadOnly` passes `countsTowardGastosMes` (`checkingSpendingMovementKeys`, `checkingSpendingMovements.ts`). Investment deposits, internal transfers, «no cuenta», partly-spending movements, own transfers, income, stock legs, mortgage and pension rows stay; filter options describe what remains. Only home passes the flag; card spending lives in statement lines, not flows.
- **«Últimos movimientos»** (`GET /api/dashboard/latest-movements?limit=` default 15, max 100; `latestMovements.ts`, `LatestMovementsResponse` / `LatestMovementRow`): newest first by `on`. Expense rows = every «Total»-mode `buildFlowsExpenseLines` line, counted or not (transfers, «no cuenta», deposits, card payments — card-to-card facturado shows both lines —, notas and annulled purchases; `listedAsLatestMovement`), a cuota purchase once as its whole-price plan line, never cuota or split lines; no payroll deductions. Card payment rows (`card_payment`, `latestCardPayments.ts`): each `pago_tarjeta` into a card master once, «Pago <card> · desde <source>», amount in the transfer's currency (divisas: pesos out, dollars at `paid`; dollar payment: the PAGO line's pesos), evidence lines in its ⋯ panel and not listed separately. Income rows from `cachedFlowsIncome`. **A card credit is dated by arrival, never its printed transaction date** (`movementOn`): `flow_first_seen` (migration 310, earliest Chile day the line's flow was written, kept through re-imports and merges; no backfill), else a later printed posting date, else the statement close (transaction date while an open facturación is ahead). Nothing after Chile today. Rows drawn with `CreditCardExpenseLinesTable`, amounts native + display unit (`formatLineAmount`). «Show more» is inert.
- **Rentabilidad on home:** `groupHasPeriodReturns` (`portfolioGroupTree.ts`: net_worth or investment groups; also gates the benchmark row and `rentabilidadDailyWarm.ts`). Home reads `GET /api/groups/:slug/period-returns` (`getGroupPeriodReturns`: consolidated monthly rows + daily-chained windows + 1D/1W); `benchmarkComparisonForGroup` uses the same builder. GroupInfoBase props `flowsTitleKey`, `flowsExcludeSpending`, `afterFlows`.

## Refunds inside gastos; additional-card reimbursements (migration 217, `checkingExpenseRefunds.ts`, `additionalCardReimbursements.ts`)

- **A checking credit can be a refund of spending** (`checking_expense_refunds`: movement → category): listed apart in income (`refund_lines`), a negative line in its category in expenses (`checking_refund: true`, `checkingExpenseRefundLineDrafts`). Category lives on the refund (`assignFlowExpenseLineCategory`). Marked/unmarked only by the user (`POST`/`DELETE /api/income/movements/:id/refund`, «Reembolso de gasto»; `markCheckingExpenseRefund` accepts only single-leg credits on checking-list accounts).
- **A card credit is spending coming back** (`markCardRefunds`, `flowsExpenses.ts`; `isGastosRefundLine`): every negative card line except a card payment (`isCcPaymentMerchant`), ABONO DE DIVISAS, traspaso de deuda, or a nota paired with its purchase carries `card_refund` and counts negatively in its category (a no_cuenta/internal-transfer category stays out). Paired notas net with their purchase. Debt moved between own cards is `checking_internal_transfer`: the peso leg of «TRASPASO A DEUDA NACIONAL» and a «RECAUDACION …» paying another card's facturado (repair tool `server/scripts/archive/repair-flows-overview-cash-lines.ts`).
- **Additional cardholder charges are `additional_card`** («Tarjeta adicional», auto-tagged on origin in `additional_card_last4s`). The Santander feed's `TipoBen` → listing `holder` (absent on pending rows); an `additional` row stores the account's active additional card as origin (`activeAdditionalCardLast4ForAccount`: latest statement's additional origin; none throws), tagged the night it lists. His wires back are refunds in that category, so it nets to what he owes. «Tarjetas adicionales» summary (`additional_cards`: month/year, running balance) from lines: `isAdditionalCardChargeLine` (cc, additional origin, still `additional_card`; recategorized = user's own), `isAdditionalCardReimbursementLine`. New wires: `npx tsx scripts/propose-card-reimbursements.ts --payer-note-token=<token> [--apply]` (from `server/`).

## MACH account rebuilt from its mails (`server/scripts/build-mach-account-from-mails.ts`)

- MACH (BCI cuenta vista, no statements) is a `clp` account under Checking accounts (`listMovementBalanceCashAccountIds` includes such leaves → gastos/income lines). Built once from a plan (`--plan=<json> [--apply]`, IMMEDIATE, ends at exactly 0 or throws): card top-ups («MACH ONE CLICK» / «MACH WEBPAY ONECLICK») are card → MACH `carga_tarjeta` transfers (card legs emit no deposit), with those card lines set to internal transfer line by line (own category beats the merchant rule); corriente withdrawals/wires via mirror conversion; «Le pagaste» = debits in the plan's category, «Te pagaron» = refunds; a stand-in refund credit for exactly any shortfall that would go negative; a closing debit to 0.
- Notes `import:mach-mail|…`, read like `import:santander-mail|…` (`isMailRebuiltCheckingNote`, `listCheckingMovements`). **From July 2021 the app's own list replaces the guesses** (`server/scripts/apply-mach-app-history.ts`, `cfraser/mach-mail-archive/mach-app-movements.json`): corriente transfers move to MACH's day (corriente's day kept as bank posting day); app rows replace stand-ins and closing, leaving one stand-in before the first row.

## CMR Falabella card (`server/scripts/import-cmr-statements.ts`, `ccIssuers.ts`)

- CMR (retired) is an issuer in `CC_ISSUERS` — the one issuer list (rules statements don't state; card order under Pasivos). Built once from mailed statements (`pdftotext -layout` into `cfraser/cmr/text/`; each must reproduce its printed billed total from the previous plus lines). Payments are merchant «PAGO», «Devolucion Compras» → «NOTA DE CREDITO»; «Servicio Administracion» / «Impuesto ITE» are section-3 (`ccStatementLineRules.json`); «Ajuste de sencillo» pairs are internal transfers. «PAGO EN LINEA (PROM.) CMR FALABELLA» debits are `pago_tarjeta` (`CC_PAYMENT_DESC_RE`); CMR → MACH top-up is `carga_tarjeta`.
- **Issuer data:** each `CC_ISSUERS` row has `statement_currencies: {primary, secondary[]}` (Chilean three: `clp` + `["usd"]` when active), `installments`, `unbilled_listing`. Close rule `closedBillingMonthsFromStatements` (`cards/facturacion.ts`, pure): primary statement in, and each required secondary (`requiredStatementCurrenciesForAccount` = primary ∪ secondaries the stream carries) in or proven not coming by the later-close rule; slot = `Map<currency, statement>`; `hasEveryStatementTwinForBillingMonth` takes the set. Anchors written for cards with plans or issuers without cuota products (`hasBillingLedger`); `cardStoppedBilling` gives listing-less cards one cycle of grace. **Lonestar** (fictional US demo bank): `{primary: "usd", secondary: []}`, pay-by close + 25, next cycle day after close, no cuotas, no listing; its payment and fee lines are in `ccStatementLineRules.json`; the reconcile accepts interest/fees on top of purchases. Fixture `ccLonestarUsdCard.test.ts`.
- **CMR payments settle the same period's purchases**, which the billing detail doesn't model (it writes no anchors for cards without plans), so CMR's month-end valuations are the running sum of lines, written by the script and checked against the owed walk; nothing recomputes them (`upsertCreditCardValuationsFromLedger` skips plan-less cards).

## Open-month CC bucket — truncated merchant duplicates

- The open month gets **two renderings of one transaction**: the nightly feed (`import:santander-movements` → `importCcWebPasteLines`, `cc_santander_fetch`) with the full merchant on settled rows, and a manual web paste — or the feed's own pending rows — cut at **exactly 15 chars**. The one-shot key `merchant | amount | date` won't collide (and dates often shift a day), so both would survive.
- **`removeTruncatedMerchantDuplicateLines`** (`ccTruncatedMerchantDedupe.ts`) runs after every paste/feed write and drops the shorter one once the fuller twin exists: scoped to `import:web-paste%`, same amount to the cent and currency, **identical merchants never match** (real repeat spending; the key handles true dups), fires only with both present. (1) **Truncated** (`isTruncatedMerchantOf`): shorter exactly 15 chars and a strict prefix, dates within `RESTATEMENT_WINDOW_DAYS` (4). (2) **Pending → settled with terminal code** (`isPendingAuthorizationOf`): shorter < 15 chars, longer ends in a spaced 4-digit code (` NNNN`) whose core is the shorter name or it plus more words, SAME date (settling keeps the date). Glued codes (25-char overflow) imply a 15-char cut → rule 1. Idempotent; planner `planTruncatedMerchantDuplicateLines`, script `server/scripts/remove-cc-merchant-duplicate-lines.ts` (report-first; `--apply` = evidence-date read-before-delete + revaluation + billing recompute).
- **The bank's feed can itself list one charge twice** (different `Ciudad` formatting, e.g. `APPLE.COM BILL` / `APPLE.COM/BILL`): bank data, re-inserted if deleted; it resolves at the facturación via `reconcileOpenWebPasteAfterPdfImports`. Same for pending rows restated with an IDENTICAL merchant a day later — deliberately not deduped.
- **Pending international authorizations** in the feed have `DescripcionRubro: null` / `TipoBen: null`, `Comercio` cut at 15; the settled row (same `Fecha`, `Importe`, full name + code) replaces them a day or two later; rules 1–2 remove the pending twin.
- **Cuota-billing reference rows never import.** At a close the feed/web table re-lists billed cuotas as `CUOT: <cuota №>OPER: <plan №>` at the cuota amount; the schedule already bills them (billing never changes owed) and the overlap skip can't match them. `ccWebPasteToCsvRecords` routes any `isCcCuotaBillingReferenceMerchant` line (incl. the 15-char cut) to a reported `skipped_cuota_billing` bucket (batch log + import panel, never silent), unconditionally (plan-gating would let a not-yet-converted plan's cuota-1 through). Old rows: `server/scripts/remove-cc-cuota-billing-lines.ts` (report-first, `--apply`).

## Lider boletas → grocery catalog

- **The Boleta Digital mail is the daily Lider source** (other card charges come by statement or paste). `fetch:lider-boletas` (IMAP `contacto@info.lider.cl`, subject «boleta») stages `Boleta.pdf` + `meta.json` under `cfraser/lider-boletas/staged/<date>-<msgid>/` (ledger by message id; `--all` backfill). `parse-grocery-receipts.py` walks both staging roots; `grocery_receipt_parse.py` picks the chain from the header RUT (`RUT: 76.134.946-5` store, bare `76134941-4` hiper/delivery, `81.201.000-K` Jumbo) or, on a cropped photo, the issuer name opening a line («CENCOSUD RETAIL S.A.»; RUT and name disagreeing throws), then `grocery_receipt_lider.py` / `grocery_receipt_jumbo.py`; unknown throws. Items: barcode, description, qty (decimal; `x 1.744 KG` lines = kg items, derived per-kg price), unit price; discounts in **two scopes**: directly under an item attaches to it; after a blank line (Mi Club `RF CANJE PESOS MCL` always, whole-receipt coupons) is receipt-level (`grocery_receipts.receipt_discount_clp`), never an item's price. **Fails unless Σ items − item discounts − receipt discounts = Σ payment legs** (TARJETA LIDER BCI / EFECTIVO net of VUELTO / PESOS MICLUB CANJEADOS / TBK) **and «TOTAL NUMERO DE ARTIC VEND» = parsed count** (units by qty, each weighed item once). Date = printed local datetime, never the mail's UTC date.
- **Paper photos go through Apple Vision OCR** (`grocery_receipt_ocr.swift`, compiled on demand by `grocery_receipt_text.py` into gitignored `ingest/python/.ocr_bin/`, keyed by sha256; reads HEIC, EXIF orientation; not Tesseract — no HEIC, eng only). `assemble_lines` rebuilds rows from blocks: de-skew by median angle, rows from confident blocks, low-confidence blocks attached to the nearest row (never bridging two), digit-less low-confidence junk dropped. Per dir: `ocr.json` (blocks, cached by engine version + sha), `ocr.txt`, and **`ocr.corrected.txt`** (human repair; parsed when present, `text_source` = `ocr_corrected`); a failed re-parse deletes the stale `parsed.json`. OCR tolerances (optional `$`, `,`/`.` thousands, digit-less item-section lines skipped as `ignored_lines`) are backed by the fail-fast checks.
- **Storage** (migration 172): `grocery_receipts` + `grocery_receipt_items`. **Identity `receipt_key` = `<chain>|<receipt_number>|<YYYY-MM-DD>`** (migration 178, NOT NULL UNIQUE; formula in `groceryReceiptKey` and the 178 hook — keep in step); `source` / `source_key` belong to the owning document. Import (`storeReceiptApply.ts`, fed by `import:grocery-receipts`) ranks documents: `email`/`pdf` > `photo`; higher rank replaces the owner, lower/equal is `skipped_duplicate` (first writer wins, so photos can't ping-pong), and an identity resolving to another document's row THROWS. Roots (`defaultStagingRoots`): `cfraser/lider-boletas/staged/` and `cfraser/grocery-receipts/staged/<date>-<source>-<key>/` (meta `{source, source_key}`, parsed `chain`; missing/unknown throws). **Drop zone `cfraser/grocery-receipts/inbox/`:** `ingestGroceryReceiptInbox` (`ingest/src/grocery/inbox.ts`) moves photos (`photo`) and PDFs (`pdf`) into `staged/<mtime-date>-<source>-<sha12>/receipt.<ext>`; exact duplicates → `grocery-receipts/duplicates/`; any other file type fails the batch before anything moves. Catalog `grocery_products` + `grocery_product_aliases` (alias = (chain, barcode) else (chain, description)); **items stamp `product_id`** (`product_source` alias|manual) so alias re-points never rewrite history; assignment fills only NULL; a changed printed description returns the item to unclassified. Run by `import:cfraser-inbox` when the inbox has a file or `hasPendingGroceryReceipts`; idempotent upsert by (receipt, position). **Import stamps** `imported.json` (stamp version + parsed sha + row + statuses): current stamp with its row still present/owned → `unchanged`, no writes. Outcomes a later run can change (`pending_branch`) aren't stamped. `-- --full` ignores stamps, `-- --reparse` forces parsing, bump `IMPORT_STAMP_VERSION` to force all.
- **Card movements are per-chain** (`CHAIN_CARD_RULES`; none = `chain_items_only` unless a match rule exists). Lider: only when paid with the Lider/BCI card and dated inside the open facturación (`lastPdfBillingMonthForAccount` period end); closed months are statement-covered. Written via web paste (`cc_lider_boleta`) with the bank's exact merchant from **`boleta_sucursal_merchants`** (`cfraser/cc-cards.json`), so dedupe collides with the feed/statement. **Each printed branch rendering is its own key** (paper omits the mail's local suffix); unknown branches are never guessed.
- **Jumbo receipts are photos only and never write a card line** (Santander's feed carries the purchase; `grocery_receipt_jumbo.py`, `CHAIN_MATCH_RULES`). Store/comuna under the CENCOSUD line (head office «KENNEDY 9001» isn't the store); quantity line (`N X $price` / `0,326 KG X $price`) printed ABOVE the item (may wait one item; each item binds the first fitting waiting line, kg ±1 peso); discounts below. Fails unless items = SUB TOTAL, discounts = DESCUENTOS, SUB TOTAL − DESCUENTOS = TOTAL, payments net of VUELTO = TOTAL. 13-digit codes must pass EAN-13 (`?` in `ocr.corrected.txt` = unreadable → null). Pairs T. CREDITO pesos with a same-pesos JUMBO/CENCOSUD card line on the printed day (`findReceiptCardLine`): `matched` | `no_card_line` (> 45 days, final; else `awaiting_card_line`, retried) | `ambiguous_card_line`. **Cropped photos** (`resolveReceiptFacts`): no number → key `photo-<sha12>`; no date → the card line's date within 7 days up to the photo date (only from a `YYYY:MM:DD.<ext>` filename, `photoTakenOnFromName`, meta `photo_taken_on`), else the photo date; else declare `#! purchase_date: YYYY-MM-DD` in the correction file (never overrides a printed date; disagreement throws). `purchase_date_source` = printed | declared | card_line | photo.
- **New stores are learned from card lines, never declared first** (migration 184, `groceryBranchLearning.ts`). Map = registry seed (`boleta_sucursal_merchants`) + **`grocery_branch_merchants`** (learned | manual; a declared entry contradicting a learned one throws). An unknown-branch card-paid open-month receipt is flagged, not failed: `card_line_status = 'pending_branch'` (+ `card_line_account_id`), no line, step exits 0, badge «Sucursal nueva · sin línea de tarjeta». `learnGroceryBranchesFromCardLines` runs inside `mergeCcAccountFromParsedRows` (paste, feed, statements; result `grocery_branch_learning`) and at each receipt import (a receipt pending at its month's close gets one try against the statement): exactly one candidate (one-shot, not an importer-written `<chain>-boleta|` line, not already learned from) stores (chain, branch) → merchant minus « (T)» and marks `matched`; zero stays pending; two same-day-same-amount lines or receipts → `ambiguous`, pending. Other statuses `created` / `covered`.
- **UI** `/flows/expenses/groceries` (node beside Inmuebles, shown when receipts exist): cleaning workbench (unclassified groups by occurrence, multi-select assign/create, similarity suggestions), per-product **price history** (effective / list / per-base-unit; branch, city, brand filters), receipts with items and a **source badge** (`GroceryReceiptRow.source` → `groceries.receipts.source.*`, desktop column + mobile card row). Server computes (`groceryProducts.ts`, `/api/groceries/*`).
- **Product config** (migration 174): `grocery_products.base_unit` ('un' | g/kg | ml/l | m) is dimension and display scale; **'un' is the COUNT dimension** (multipack content N; unconfigured = 1). Alias `content` stored in the canonical small unit (g/ml/m) so display changes never rewrite it; a cross-dimension change clears contents. Normalized price = effective package price ÷ content, null when unconfigured (kg rows convert directly on mass products). **Brands are global** (`grocery_brands`, unique; aliases carry `brand_id`, delete only unreferenced). **Merge** (`mergeGroceryProducts`) re-points aliases, restamps ALL items incl. manual (identity statement), deletes the source; the OPEN product survives. **Mixed package sizes disable per-package modes** (`heterogeneous_packages`: distinct contents or any weighed row → per-base-unit only). Detail UI: base unit, merge-into, per-alias brand (pick-or-create) + content (dimension-restricted unit).

## Checking / cuenta vista ledger anchor

- A checking balance is **derived from movements**; a synthetic **anchor** movement (`import:cartola|anchor|<period>|saldo final`, dated before the first real movement) carries the opening balance so the walk lands on the latest cartola's `saldo_final_clp`. `ensureCheckingLedgerAnchor` re-derives it when an import advances the latest cartola period.
- **The derivation MUST use the balance reader's accounting** — `sumClpThroughDate` → `signedClpDeltaForAccountMovement` (matches `account_id OR from_account_id OR to_account_id`, signs each leg, applies `flow_kind`). Anything else gives an anchor that disagrees with the display.
- **Bank posting day (migration 199, `movementBankPostings.ts`).** `occurred_on` = best evidence of when money moved; `movement_bank_postings(movement_id, account_id, posted_on, occurred_on_basis)` keeps the day a bank document filed it on one account when different, and what `occurred_on` rests on (migration 329: `evidence` = a document stated the day; `fetch` = the listing's fetch day, an upper bound a transfer mail may move earlier) (Santander's 14:00 cutoff → next workday, possibly next month). One row per (movement, account); sparse. **Readers that reconcile against a bank document read `COALESCE(posted_on, occurred_on)` via `BANK_POSTING_JOIN_SQL` / `BANK_POSTED_ON_SQL`**: anchor derivation and month table (`nonAnchorClpFlowTotals`), `checkingMovementBalanceAtMonthEnd` (`sumClpThroughBankPostedDate`), timeline months, xlsx/cartola dedupe (`partialMovementSupersededByCartola`, `findMatchingCartolaMovementNoteInDb`, the cartola import's count) and `findMatchingInternalTransferLegId` (a leg with a posting matches only that day, else the business-day window, across months). Everything else reads `occurred_on`. **Writers** record any bank date they learn (`recordBankPosting`, throws if the movement doesn't touch the account): `claimTransferLegForBankRow`, a hand transfer superseding a bank row, receipt re-dates, Fintual promotions, both mirror conversions (each deleted leg's date, its stored posting carried through conversion and undo), the checking listing (fetch day) and transfer-notice pairing (mail day). So mail-sourced writers never wait for the bank row across a month boundary. Regression: «bank posting day» in `checkingCartolaBalances.test.ts`.
- **Forward-posted rows count from today; history keeps bank dates.** The daily checking listing no longer stores a row after its fetch day (see `bank_account.movements`), but a row any other source dates after Chile today still reaches the ledger. `displayLedgerCutoffYmd` (`movementTransfer.ts`): a read before today is strict (`≤ date`), a read at/after today has no upper bound, so a value never steps twice. Applied at two choke points: the ledger balance readers (`checkingMovementBalanceClpAt`, `clpCashBalanceClpAt`, `foreignCashBalanceAt`) and the merged deposit timeline (`buildMergedDepositMap` re-dates such events to today, bank date kept as `posted_on`), so flow-adjusted P/L reads 0 for the row every day. Don't add "skip events dated > today" caps in deposit readers: they'd create the opposite phantom. **Reconciliation stays strict:** `nonAnchorClpFlowTotals`, the anchor derivation and `checkingMovementBalanceAtMonthEnd`; the deposit-match loaders in `checkingCartolaLoaders.ts` read `loadMergedDepositInflowEventsBankDated` (pairing is about real dates); the balance cache key carries the applied cutoff. Listings keep the bank date with `AccountMovementApiRow.forward_posted` («ya en el saldo»). Equity unit walks are not covered.
- **The bank's own charges are `cash_fee` (`checkingBankCharges.ts`).** Plan maintenance fee («COM.MANTENCION PLAN»), línea de crédito interest and overdraft tax are stored as `cash_fee` by both checking importers, decided from the description: deposit loaders skip the kind, so they are the account's P/L, not withdrawals. Gastos still list them. Backfill: `server/scripts/tag-checking-bank-charges.ts`.
- **Transfer rows have `account_id` NULL** (the account is in `from_account_id`/`to_account_id`), so `WHERE account_id = ?` misses every transfer leg (and the CLP leg is unsigned). Since the anchor re-derives only when a cartola advances the period, such a bug stays latent. Regression: «cartola-derived anchor with transfer legs» in `checkingCartolaBalances.test.ts`. **When touching any balance/anchor helper, check that transfer rows are in scope.**

- **One source for a checking account's movements (`listCheckingMovements` in `checkingCartolaLoaders.ts`).** Expenses, income, deposit matching, payslip pairing and income overrides ask for an account's inflows/outflows, never a document: cartola rows plus daily «últimos movimientos» rows not yet replaced (superseded-but-unpruned left out), nothing else — no anchor, no hand adjustment. When the cartola supersedes a daily row, `prunePartialMovementsSupersededByCartola` moves the payslip link and income override onto the official row (`carryMovementKeyedIncomeState`; both reference movements without a cascade, so a leftover fails the import's FK). Source document is still read from the note there and in import-side key mechanics; a structured source column would retire that.
- **Foreign checking accounts' rows are gastos and income lines too (`listForeignCheckingAccountIds` / `listForeignCheckingMovements`).** Any cash ledger in a non-peso currency (`foreignCashCurrencyOf`) under Checking accounts; rows carry `amount_native`, `currency` and pesos/dollars at the day's stored mid via `rates.ts` (`foreignCheckingAmountIn`). Dollar lines keep `amount_usd` native; third-currency lines state `amount` (`foreignCheckingLineMoney`), income credits their `native` leg (`incomeLineLegs` → `FlowLegs.native_amount`), refunds their `native`. Below, «dollar» means «foreign» for these lists; the deposits reconciliation reads dollar deposits against the dollar accounts' months only. A `usd`-kind checking account's debits are expense lines (`flowsCheckingGastos.ts`: category rules and «Único», no deposit matching, pesos at the day's `fx_daily` — a row moving no dollars or a day with no rate throws), its credits income lines (`flowsCheckingInflows.ts`), so a US pay stub pairs with its net credit; the funnel reads both lists (`loadCheckingGastosLinesForExpenses`). A single-leg dollar row with `units_delta` on a non-dollar-cash account (401(k)/HSA payroll buy) is a deposit event at the day's rate (`accountDeposits.ts`), pre-tax via `PAYROLL_FUNDED_KINDS` (`afp, afc, us_401k, hsa`). A listed single-leg `withdrawal` debit is a «used» dollar lot (`usdCashTaxLotEvents.ts`; realized under both oficios, deferred under `none`). Notes may sit on its gastos lines (`ccExpensePurchaseNotes.ts`); a credit marked a refund (`checkingExpenseRefunds.ts`) is a dollar-expense refund (`amount_usd`, pesos at the day's rate, in `refund_lines` and a negative dollar line in its category); `flowsDepositsReconciliation.ts` counts a dollar units deposit at its dollars as `accountDeposits.ts` prices it, reads «checking present» from the dollar checking months, and treats those accounts as the funding source — out of `loadNetWorthCapitalOutflowCandidates` / `loadDepositMatchCandidates`, like peso checking. Mirror pairs pair same-currency rows.

## Historical mirror pairs + movement search

- **Mirror pairs** (`/panel/mirror-pairs`): two pre-transfer-model single-leg rows (retiro + same-amount depósito on different accounts, inflow 0–5 days after outflow) become one transfer dated the **outflow day**; original legs live in **`movement_mirror_merges`** keyed by the transfer (ON DELETE CASCADE; the note is a human summary); undo is the server function `undoMirrorConversion` (no HTTP route). **Any currency:** both legs one currency, equal to its coin — pesos with no flow_kind (sign = direction), others a `withdrawal` + `deposit` single-leg pair, no units, each on a cash ledger held in that currency (positive amount, direction in `flow_kind`); an `fx_purchase` credit never pairs, nor do two currencies. The merge row's `out_amount_clp` / `in_amount_clp` hold the legs signed in the transfer's currency (legacy column names); undo writes `withdrawal` / `deposit`. Leg DTO: `amount` (signed) + `currency`; link-established pairs stay pesos. Candidates: `listMirrorPairCandidates` (`server/src/movementMirrorPairs.ts`) — high confidence = unique both directions + cartola business-day window (`bankDateMatchesTransferDate`) + no month straddle; straddles are ambiguous but never blocked (each deleted leg's date stays as the transfer's posting day on its account). Excluded legs: flow_kind/USD legs, both-legs-cuotas, dap either side, afp/afc **inflows**, deposits already in `expense_deposit_links`/`checking_gap_deposit_mirrors`/`payroll_work_earnings`, Buda/ahorro-split notes. Rejections in `movement_mirror_pair_rejections` (migration 155; cascade on full rebuilds). **Link-established pairs**: 1:1 `expense_deposit_links` (auto/manual, `checking-cartola:` keys via `movementForCheckingPurchaseKey`, equal amounts) are `linked: true` high-confidence, no window; the link cascades at conversion. Multi-allocation/partial/synthetic links and Buda-note deposits stay out. One transfer carries one `units_delta` (abs; cuota readers use `transferLegUnitsThroughDate`). **Month-precision legs** (cuenta_ahorro_vivienda, dated month-end): window = whole month ± 7 days (`monthPrecisionPairAllowed`), bank window waived, the transfer takes the **real-day (checking) leg's date** (re-import dedupe and anchors untouched; checking-straddle block skipped). `loadTransferLegSignedFlowEvents` (`accountDeposits.ts`) buckets each side on the day **that side's valuation evidence moves**: month-precision legs keep their original merge date (no −X/+X monthly couplet); **every other leg uses the transfer's own date**, since balances and `transferLegUnitsThroughDate` key off the movement (the settlement date gives a phantom ±X couplet). Month-precision kinds: `monthPrecisionAccountKinds.ts` (`accountIsMonthPrecisionDated`, import-free for `accountBucket.ts` / `flowsCheckingGastos.ts`). Converted cartola credits leave the income pool; merge transfers into checking self-resolve in the reconciliation. **Same-day siblings are indistinguishable by amount and date** (greedy lower-id pick can absorb the wrong row): ambiguous candidates carry `out_alternatives` / `in_alternatives`, shown with notes, any convertible instead (`resolveMirrorPairRef`); an **auto** link with a same-amount competitor in window is ambiguous (manual stays high); a credit classified as income (`checking_income_movement_overrides` kind / force-include) or a debit categorized as spending (a `cc_expense_unique_purchases` category outside unclassified / deposits / internal transfer / no_cuenta, or a `cc_expense_line_splits` split) is never a leg. Repair: `server/scripts/repair-mirror-merge-legs.ts --plan=<json> [--apply]` (report-first, IMMEDIATE, idempotent; `swap` a leg for its sibling, `undo` a non-transfer — both restore originals under ORIGINAL ids; undo records a rejection — or `reject`; month-end balances and anchors verified unchanged in-transaction).
- **Checking↔CC payment mirrors (`ccPaymentMirrors.ts`):** a checking «Traspaso a T. Crédito»/PAGO TARJETA debit on any account under Checking accounts (`accountIdsUnderCheckingAccounts`) pairs with the card's payment evidence — a payment line (`isCcPaymentMerchant`: exactly PAGO / MONTO CANCELADO / ABONO, the one shared rule) or the `monto_pagado_anterior` header (date via `requireHeaderPagoIso`, throws unless ISO) — exact amount, ±4 days. UI: /panel/mirror-pairs «Pagos de tarjeta», POST `/api/movement-mirrors/convert-cc-payments`. Conversion writes one transfer checking → CC master dated the **card's credit date**, `flow_kind = 'pago_tarjeta'`; the merge row keeps the cartola leg (`out_*`) and references the evidence (`in_statement_line_id` / `in_statement_id`; migration 167: exactly one in-ref). Statement evidence is untouched (the transfer's card leg is inert; CC valuations ignore movements) and `pago_tarjeta` emits **no deposit/aportes events**. Undo restores only the checking leg. **Nightly auto-convert:** `npm run convert:cc-payment-mirrors` (in `import:cfraser-inbox` after the receipt step); fail-closed; the debit's date stays as the posting day on checking. **Foreign-debt payments:** a «Egreso por Compra de Divisas» debit pairs with the card's ABONO DE DIVISAS in the bought currency (by the LINE's currency, so the web-paste bucket qualifies), by date window + one-to-one uniqueness + implied-rate band (0,5×–2× that currency's mid on the abono's day, `DIVISAS_IMPLIED_RATE_BAND`), evidence keyed in that currency (`ccPaymentEvidenceKey`: smallest coin, `amount_foreign`), traspaso-linked abonos excluded; converted to the migration-169 cross-currency shape (CLP from-leg = pesos that left checking, counter leg = the abono, dated at the card's credit date). **Khipu:** «Transf a khipu …» debits are candidates, pairing only with CLP card evidence of the exact pesos one-to-one; unpaired stay plain debits.
- **A closed month's month-end anchor adds what a later facturación bills (`unbilledAtMonthEndAdjustmentsClp` in `ccBillingBalances.ts`).** Beyond the statement frame (facturado + cupo), it adds every charge and payment dated ≤ calendar month-end that a LATER facturación bills (or none yet), chosen by billing (`facturacionMonthByStatementDate`), not a date window after the close (which misses Santander's close-day purchases — its next cycle starts ON the close day — and lines posted a cycle late). The stream includes dated header-payment legs (`cc_header_payment_legs`). The daily walk keeps its window from its anchor (`postCloseLiveBalanceAdjustmentsClp`). Re-stamp: `scripts/restamp-cc-anchor-frame.ts`.
- **A statement billed what it printed, credit included (`statementHeaderFacturado`, `normalizedPostCloseLines`).** Import, JSON writer and every billing reader (`facturadoFromStatement`, slot totals, `usdToClpAsDebt`) take the printed «Monto total facturado a pagar» whatever its sign; 0 = nothing billed; only a missing header falls back to lines (`scripts/backfill-cc-printed-facturado.ts`). A line dated ≥ 2 cycles before its statement (a nota carrying its purchase's date) takes that statement's close as its post-close date, so walk and anchors agree.
- **Dollar header payments (migration 218).** A header leg has currency + `amount` (old international statements print the abono only in the header, «ABONO REALIZADO»). `scripts/link-cc-usd-header-abono-legs.ts` (report-first) writes header abono − the statement's negative lines (refunds count, traspasos don't) as a dollar leg, dated on the period's one plausible-rate «Compra de Divisas» debit (which the converter pairs), else the previous pay-by. The walk values dollar legs at their statement's debt fx; cuota retirement reads peso legs only.
- **A dollar payment is valued at the debt fx of the facturación it paid (`normalizedPostCloseLines`):** the debt fx of the latest dollar statement closed before it (as `facturacionPaidOn` pairs), not its own statement's fx; a linked traspaso keeps its CLP twin's pesos. Missing/unreadable dollar statements: `scripts/estimate-cc-missing-usd-statements.ts --plan=cfraser/cc-usd-estimate-plan-0161.json` writes `import:estimate|…` statements with one «COMPRAS INTERNACIONALES (ESTIMADO)» charge (gap between printed balances either side) and an «ABONO DE DIVISAS» on the paying divisas debit (dollars = its pesos at the day's dólar × 1,021, the median divisas spread); textless scans get header abono from printed totals, an estimated charge and dated dollar legs.
- **Statements from outside the PDF corpus are named `import:<kind>|…`** (`isCcStatementPdfSource`): web pastes, Santander statement JSON, mail-rebuilt (`import:santander-mail|`), CMR mailed PDFs (`import:cmr|`). Import-sync coverage requires an on-disk PDF only for the rest.
- **Undated header payments get dated legs (migration 213, `cc_header_payment_legs`).** The parser dates «monto pagado período anterior» only when one body line carries it; undated headers are invisible to the cuota retirement (dated payments only). A leg: card, statement close (not row id — re-imports replace rows), day + pesos of the paying bank debit (`source = bank_debit`), or the previous pay-by (`pay_by`). `listCcPaymentEvidenceRows` and `listClpCcPaymentEventsForAccount` read legs as dated payments; pairing claims by payment key, so legs pair separately. Writer: `server/scripts/archive/link-cc-header-payment-legs.ts` (report-first, IMMEDIATE): dated payment lines in the period counted once (card groups repeat them), remainder matched exactly by unlinked card-payment checking debits in (previous close, close + 4 days], a remainder explained by a credit note skipped (BCI counts notas in «monto pagado»). A remainder ≠ debit may be a «disponible para pago» payment whose overpayment carries as negative saldo anterior. «Traspaso … a Línea Crédito» is not a card payment (overdraft line; `CC_PAYMENT_DESC_RE` still keeps it internal in checking gastos).
- **A converted payment finds its evidence by the payment, never by row id (`ccPaymentMirrorEvidence.ts`).** Re-imports give lines new ids (`cc_statement_lines.id` has no AUTOINCREMENT, ids get reused), PDFs take over JSON closes, pasted PAGOs settle into headers. Identity = card, currency, date, amount (pesos to unit, dollars to cent); the pairing side is the transfer's card leg (`to_account_id`, `occurred_on`, the USD counter leg or the pesos). `listCcPaymentMirrorCandidates` excludes converted payments by that key and throws when one has no evidence; `usdPaymentLineClpResolver` finds a line's divisas purchase via the evidence rows. `mergeCcAccountFromParsedRows`, the `--wipe` reload (one transaction) and web-paste line delete throw, naming the pairing, when they'd remove a paired payment. `in_statement_line_id` / `in_statement_id` are provenance only; `server/scripts/repair-cc-payment-mirror-evidence-refs.ts [--allow-kind-change] [--apply]` re-points stale ones and checks every pairing has evidence.
- **Movement search lives in the flows tables** (`/search` redirects home). `FlowsTable` filters: year/type/account/bucket (`bucket` = deepest portfolio-tree bucket; liability accounts resolve via kind to `liabilities_*` nodes; options carry `label`/`label_i18n_key`, rendered via `resolveNavTreeLabel`), `q` (note + account + counterpart + flow-type label), `date_from`/`date_to` (inclusive), `amount_exact` XOR `amount_min`/`amount_max` + `amount_currency` (clp default; compares the rounded |leg| in that currency, a row without that leg never matches). Consumers: the **net-worth dashboard (master view, `/api/groups/net_worth/flows`)**, group and account pages. Shared logic: `applyFlowFilters` + `parseExtraFlowsFilterParams` (`server/src/flowsApi.ts`), parsed by both flows routes in `routes/dashboard.ts`. **Movements only** — CC lines are searched from the Expenses page.

## XLSX export (account / group pages)

- **Exportar** (account toolbar + `GroupInfoBase`) → modal (range presets incl. per-year, sections, unit) → `GET /api/accounts/:id/export.xlsx` / `/api/groups/:slug/export.xlsx` (`from`/`to` inclusive YYYY-MM, `sections=closings,aportes,pl,movements`, `unit`). Builder `server/src/exportWorkbook.ts` (SheetJS; sheet per section, Spanish titles, raw numbers), reusing `monthlyPerfForAccountId` (Cierres + P&L), `getMergedDisplayDepositInflowEventsForAccount` (Aportes; withdrawals negative; `acumulado` over full history then range-filtered), `listAccountMovementsForApi(Bulk)` (Movimientos). **Cierres, P&L and Aportes are in the requested unit, named in the header** (`cierre_<unit>`, `pl_nominal_<unit>`, `monto_<unit>` / `acumulado_<unit>`; each aporte at its day's rate via `flowEventInUnit`, running total rounded to the unit's coin); **Movimientos keeps stored legs** (`monto_clp` / `monto_usd` on every row, `monto_<code>` for other currencies, never converted). Group export = member rows with a `cuenta` column (`listAccountsForGroupTab`, synthetic ids filtered). Client: `ExportToolbarButton` (`client/src/components/export/ExportModal.tsx`) + `downloadFile.ts` (filename from Content-Disposition).

## Wealth percentile (/wealth-percentile)

- **Static distribution table, refreshed by hand once or twice a year** (`server/src/wealthDistributions.ts`; procedure in **`docs/wealth-percentile-data-refresh.md`**). Anchors: UBS Global Wealth Databook 2023 (end-2016 → 2022) and UBS GWR 2026 (end-2025). Editions are not comparable, so medians never mix across editions: 2023/2024 are geometric interpolations (`†`); a year without a printed median is calibrated on the Gini table + Millionaire Index (`‡`). The Spanish Latin-America GWR is the only source of per-adult CL/MX/BR levels, kept as printed. `interpolatedRecords()` hardcodes the 2022→2025 pair — generalize it before the end-2026 row.

## Tax return (/tax-return)

- **A local Formulario 22 per año tributario** (`f22/draft.ts`, `f22/payload.ts`, `GET /api/tax-return?tax_year=`, `TaxReturnPage`): filed return, third-party informed figures, and a draft adding what the app knows. Import per year: `server/scripts/import-sii-tax-year.ts --year= --dir=cfraser/sii/AT<year> [--apply]` (migration 198): «F22 Compacto» PDF → `sii_f22_filed` (`siiF22Compacto.ts`, checks 304 = 305, 170 = 158 − 750, 91 = 90 + 39); `DJ_<code>_<year>_<rut>.xlsx` → `sii_informed_dj` (`siiInformedDj.ts`, keyed by column letter + header path).
- **One set of tax-year facts; returns map them (`server/src/taxFacts/`, `server/src/f22/`; docs/demo-app-features-plan.md §5 items 13, 15).** `taxFacts/` states what happened in a `DateWindow`, in its own currency, with source, and nothing a law decides: `dividendFacts`, `interestFacts`, `equityAccountDisposalFacts` / `cryptoDisposalFacts` / `fundDisposalFacts` (lot-walk disposals in trading currency; lot method is the caller's), `rentalFacts`, `loanPaymentFacts`, `chileanPayslipFacts` / `usPayStubFacts` / `feeReceiptFacts`, `fundHoldingFacts`. Income source is `taxFacts/payers.ts` alone (`FOREIGN_BROKER_COUNTRY`, other brokers Chilean; `US_BANK_ISSUERS`; read from `broker_cash` roles and `bank_account_numbers`). No conversion, reajuste or code there. F22 blocks under `f22/` (`salary`, `fees`, `rentals`, `art107`, `crypto`, `foreignShares`, `foreignIncome`, `dividends`, `usdFx` (F22-only), `estimates`) apply Chilean rules; `blocks.ts` reads a window's blocks, `resident.ts` / `nonresident.ts` regimes, `chain.ts` arithmetic, `sources.ts` filed form + DJs, `offsets.ts` pools, `draft.ts` composes. A new reader of tax-year ledger data adds a fact, never SQL in a return. Refactor parity: `server/scripts/capture-tax-payloads.ts` before/after, byte-identical.
- **Fees from boletas issued; lender interest through the cap (migration 296).** With no filed form, the fees box comes from `sii_issued_fee_receipts`: 547 gross, 494 presumed expenses (30 %, ≤ 15 UTA), 770 APV régimen B when no employer reported the year (no DJ 1887; else 765), 110 = 547 − 494 − 770, 198 retention credit; a boletas-only year has base `fees`; fees add to an informed/payroll base. Contributions on fees (900) are not drafted (rates not on file); filed forms carry them. DJ 1898 = interest PAID (751); 750 drafted by art. 55 bis (`mortgageInterestDeduction`: ≤ 8 UTA, cut between 90 and 150 UTA of gross) in every base. The page labels any code `taxReturn.codes.<code>` names (`i18n.exists`).
- **The chain must reproduce the filed return before adding anything:** 158 = 1098 + 155 + 152 + 1032 + 1104 + 748 − 169, 170 = 158 − 750, 157 = IGC table (December UTA; `IGC_TABLE_UTA`, AT2021+), 136 = 157 × 152 / 158, 304 = 157 − 136 − 162 − 1018; `buildF22Draft` throws if filed codes don't give filed 158/170/157/136/304. Additions: crypto (`f22/crypto.ts`: 1032, net loss → 169; average cost, commissions excluded, cost and result reajustados to December by the SII's index-ratio %), foreign dividends (1104 net, 748 withheld, 1018 credit capped at 35 %, at the 31-Dec observado; missing gross/withholding detail throws), foreign share gains (`foreignShareGainsForYear`). The payment section (39, 90, 91, …) is shown as filed.
- **Every año tributario has a tab:** `availableF22TaxYears` (AT2017 → next April's) plus older filed years. Base precedence: filed form, informed DJs, imported liquidaciones (`f22/salary.ts`: 1098 / 161 / 162 as DJ 1887 reports — haberes less TOTAL NO IMPONIBLE and worker AFP, health ≤ 7 % of taxable base, cesantía, each month actualizado by the SII year-end factor), or nothing (app codes only, no tax). Unparsed months are listed, never guessed. An open income year is provisional (latest UTA, observado and IPC stand in for December). The difference column needs both values. A dividend without gross/withholding detail is pending and left out of 1104/748/1018. App-stated codes (1032, 1104, 748, 1018) replace filed values. The compact reader handles AT2019–AT2026 layouts.
- **Ledger estimates (`f22/estimates.ts`)**, only when the base is liquidaciones or nothing: fund redemptions (155 / 1869, losses → 169; non-APV Fintual goals, FIFO, IPC-reajustado cost, result actualizado) and mortgage interest (751; 750 via art. 55 bis). Flagged `estimated` («≈»). Open: the lender's reported 2024 interest disagrees with the ledger.
- **IPC reajuste uses the printed index ratio inside a base** (`officialIpcVariationPctBetween`; chaining one-decimal variations can differ); a base change (printed ratio > 1 point off the published variation) is bridged by that month's variation.
