- **One legal source, one reajuste, one set of F22 identities (`legalParameters.ts`, `siiOfficialIpc.ts`, `f22/identities.ts`).** `legalParameters.ts` (leaf, dated law): IGC tables by AT (`igcTax`, `igcMarginalRate`; monthly `iuscTax` in UTM), pension cap by year (unemployment cap = 1,5×), 10 %, health 7 %, AFC/AFP rates, minimum wage, gratification cap, boleta retention schedule, independent contribution and presumed-expense rates, the SII's one-decimal rounding; an unlisted year throws (refresh each January — the demo needs next year's caps and minimum wage). Read by the F22 draft, payslip checks and the demo. Every IPC reajuste goes through `ipcReajuste({round, floorAtZero})` (`pct`, `yearEndPct`, `december` → `decemberReajuste(incomeYear, latestMonth)`), with `monthBeforeYmd`. F22 identities (170, 305, 91) live in `f22/identities.ts`, used by both the compact reader and the draft. Taxable pay, legal health and the impuesto único base are decided once in `payslipLineKinds.ts` (`payslipHaberIsTaxable`, `legalHealthClp`, `iuscTaxableClp`).
- **Art. 107 LIR (`art107Instruments.ts`, `f22/art107.ts`, migration 230 `art107_instruments`).** `art107_instruments` (ticker → `fund` | `share`; `CFIETFIPSA.SN` seeded as fund) decides — never the `.SN` suffix: share = N°1 (1809), fund = N°2 (1813). Lots via `taxLots.ts` (FIFO default), each disposal priced under both resident options: `cost_paid` (pesos reajustados by IPC from the month before purchase) and `close_dec31` (purchase-year 31-Dec close from `equity_daily`, stand-in for the CMF official close, reajustado from November; null without a close or for open-year lots). Draft takes the lower (`default_option`) and reports both (irrevocable once filed). Regime by sale date (`art107RegimeForSale`): `inr` before 2022-09-02, `tax_10pct` 2022-09-02 → 2026-12-31 (flat 10 % único, outside IGC), `inr` from `ART107_INR_FROM` = 2027-01-01 (listed, no code, no carry). Codes: 1813 / 1809 = `tax_10pct` results, 1814 = sum, 1815 = previous AT's negative 1816 reajustado (filed form's, else the app's: `carried_loss_source`), 1816 = 1814 + 1815, 1829 = max(0, 1816), 1830 = 10 % of 1829; `305 = 304 + 1830 − 198 + 900` (draft and `assertF22Identities`); 304 / 158 / 170 / 169 never see these. Losses pool only within art. 107. Fund distributions are línea 2 dividends: 105 estimated from CLP `dividend_payout` out of the fund (`art107DistributionsForYear`), replaced by DJ 1922 B3; 610 = DJ 1922 B4 credit; `computeF22Tax` adds 105 to 158, subtracts 610 in 304; 105 is in `CAPITAL_LOSS_POOL_CODES`. `f22/foreignShares` skips art. 107 accounts. DJ 1891 «Monto Total Ventas» and DJ 1922 B1 («…actualizada») are the informed cross-checks in the payload's `art107` block (`informed_sales_clp`, `informed_result_clp`); the caja's `cash_fee` stays out of the return.
- **Foreign currency is tax lots on one walk shared by the card rate and the tax (`foreignCashLotWalk.ts`, `foreignCashTaxLotEvents.ts`, `f22/foreignFx.ts`, `bcentralObservado.ts`; `docs/tax-usd-lots-plan.md`, docs/any-currency-plan.md C2).** One FIFO slice queue per foreign cash account in its `cash_currency` (integer minor units, `currencyMinorDigits`), keeping original date and cost (Oficio 233/2018: FIFO or LIFO, never average), with request-time reservation of broker withdrawals, own transfers carrying slices intact (same currency only) and caller-priced inflows. **A conversion between two foreign currencies** disposes of the first (`convertedTo`, nothing carried) and acquires the second (source `conversion`): the cost tracer carries the consumed slices' pesos; the tax module prices the new lot at its own observado and realizes the old under 2573 and 2390 (deferred under `none`). Cost tracer `foreignCashCostLots.ts` (`feeCostToNet: true`, one `own_transfer` lot per carried slice) and tax module (`feeCostToNet: false`) share the walk. `foreignCashTaxDisposals({posture, purchaseCost})` lists every disposal, priced at that currency's Banco Central observado (`observadoClpOnOrBefore`: dollar `fx_daily_bcentral`, euro `eur_daily` — Oficio 2573/2022; other currencies throw; ≤ 7 days carried): `stock_buy` (realized under `oficio_2573`, deferred under `oficio_2390` / `none`), `pago_tarjeta` or any outflow outside scope (realized under 2573 and 2390, deferred under `none`), reconversion to pesos (realized unless `none`), `cash_fee` at zero proceeds tagged `fee` (never deductible — Oficio 1474/2020, Oficio 2208/2022); anything else throws. `foreignFxTaxGainsForYear` reajusts the RESULT to December (cost not reajustado, Oficio 2390), sums currencies (`byCurrency` shares) and routes: `idpc_1901` (default; AT2026 line 58 d: code 1901 into 158, 25 % IDPC reported and credited in line 5, never in the 169 pool, losses lost) or `igc_1032` (into 1032, losses → 169). `buildF22Draft(taxYear, today, {foreignFx: {route, posture, purchaseCost, load}})`; payload `foreign_fx` rows carry `currency` + `amount` and `by_currency`; the «Diferencias de cambio» section is report-only, one line per currency. Dollar-only readers (foreign shares, year-end rate) keep `dollarObservadoOnOrBefore`.
- **A declared tax residency decides the F22's regime (migration 302 `tax_residency_periods`, `taxResidency.ts`, `dateWindow.ts`, `GET|POST /api/tax-residency`, `DELETE …/:id`, Settings «Tax residency»; docs/demo-app-features-plan.md §1).** Residency is DECLARED from a day (`cl` | `us`; one open period, a new one closes the previous the day before; only the latest deletable; the demo declares `person.taxResidency` before the April loop) — never inferred; every return says so (`residency.assumption`, i18n `taxReturn.residency.assumption`; LIR art. 4, Oficio 2306/2016). Undeclared = Chile-resident. `residencyForIncomeYear` → `resident` / `nonresident` / `split` (a return to Chile or two changes in a year throws). **Non-resident year:** Chilean-source income only (LIR art. 3) under the **Impuesto Adicional, art. 60 inc. 1, 35 %, línea 72**: rent runs 1849 → 955 → 158, then 32 = max(0, 158) (the SUB TOTAL; 750/765 never reach it), 34 = max(0, round(35 % × 32) − 76), 304 = 0, 305 = 304 + 34 + 952 + 1830 − 198 − 833 + 900 (`f22/identities.ts`, `assertF22Identities`; `computeF22Tax(…, "nonresident")`); DFL-2 rent exempt (DFL 2 art. 15); art. 107 sales keep línea 66's 10 % único at general-rule cost (N°7) with broker withholding (N°6) — not INR. **Undeclared items stay in the payload as `excluded` with a reason**: US salary, foreign dividends/interest/share gains, dollar lots (`nonresident`); crypto (`treaty_art13_8`; SII position UNVERIFIED); Chilean fund redemptions/distributions (`fund_unico_withheld`, Ley 20.712 art. 82 B; 951/952 only when the gain is undeterminable; treaty art. 13(8) on cuotas UNVERIFIED); Chilean bank interest (`withheld_at_source`, arts. 59 N°1 / 74 N°4); 750/751, 765, 1637 (`no_igc`). A Chilean salary or boleta in a non-resident window throws (art. 60 inc. 2 not modelled). **Departure year is split:** every loader takes a `DateWindow` (`payrollTaxYear`, `rentalsForYear`, `cryptoTaxGainsForYear`, `foreignShareGainsForYear`, `usdFxTaxGainsForYear`, `art107ForTaxYear` (+ `costChoice`, `carry`), `loadF22Dividends`, `foreignSalaryForYear`, `foreignInterestForYear`, `fundRedemptionGainsForYear`, `mortgageInterestForYear`); resident part through the declared day, Adicional on Chilean-source items after; combined codes = resident part's + 32/34 (the part's own 1849/955/158 in `nonresident.codes`), art. 107 summed with one carry, 305 over both; yearly DJs (1887, 1890, 1894, 1899) stay whole in the resident part; DJ 1898 is replaced by ledger interest through the day. LIR art. 103's departure return is noted (`art103_departure_return_built: false`), not built. The filed check reproduces each regime's chain. `tax_filed` / `tax_draft` = 304 + 34.
- **A Form 1040 per US tax year (`server/src/us1040/`, migration 301; spec `docs/us-1040-readings.md`).** One pure module per form, `(facts, law) → lines`, tested in `forms.test.ts`: `scheduleB.ts`, `form8949.ts`, `scheduleD.ts` (US$3.000 limit, carryover worksheet), `qualifiedDividendsWorksheet.ts`, `form8960.ts` (NIIT), `schedule1.ts`, `schedule2.ts`, `dualStatus.ts`; `identities.ts` (2024 numbering roles; 2025 prints 7a, 11a/11b, 12e — 12c dual-status —, 13a, adds 13b: `f1040PrintedLine`, `f1040RoleOfPrintedLine`, `f1040YearHasLine`; 13b on 2024 throws); `facts.ts` (taxFacts → dollar items); `fx.ts` (the one rule: `fx_daily` mid of each item's day, ≤ 7 days back — basis at purchase, proceeds at sale, income at receipt); `pfic.ts` (one predicate: Santiago fund per `art107_instruments`, London ETF, fund account outside a treaty pension; APV ASSUMED an Art. 18(4) pension, `APV_TREATED_AS_TREATY_PENSION`); `draft.ts`; `payload.ts` → `GET /api/tax-return/us` (`UsTaxReturnResponse`). **Residency decides** (`usResidencyOfYear(residencyForIncomeYear(year))`): full → ordinary worldwide return; dual-status → 1040 for the resident window, no standard deduction, plus the nonresident statement's US-source dividends at the rate on each pay day (`usDividendWithholdingRateForChileanResident`: 30 % before 2024-02-01, 15 % after), added to line 16 (`DUAL_STATUS_STATEMENT_TAX_LINE`, an unquoted reading); nonresident with US stubs → not drafted (`usReturnStatusForYear`: 1040-NR; `drafted: false` + W-2s; `buildF1040Draft` throws); undeclared → v1 (W-2s + US bank interest, full-year resident) with note `residency_undeclared`. **What enters:** interest and dividends outside US retirement accounts and treaty pensions (Schedule B; qualified = US-listed non-PFIC minus `INTEREST_DERIVED_FUND_TICKERS` e.g. SGOV; dollar dividend without `movement_dividend_details` throws); US tax withheld by a foreign broker = Form 1042-S payment on 25c, never foreign tax; disposals FIFO at every broker (A/D via US broker, C/F without 1099-B, I/L for coins from 2025), crypto `feePolicy: "included"`, historic cost (no step-up), PFICs to Form 8621; carryover in from the prior year's draft. **Schedules composed in `us1040/wired.ts`, each reading a named constant with its alternative:** Schedule E per rented place (`scheduleE.ts`: rents, agency commission, loan interest and fire insurance (desgravamen out), contribuciones, gastos comunes, ADS 30 years, `LAND_SHARE` 20 %; pre-residency rental `PRE_RESIDENCY_DEPRECIATION = "from_placed_in_service"`, alt `fresh_on_arrival`; converted residence at lesser of cost and value), UF via `fx.ts` `clpPerUfOn`; Form 8582 (`form8582.ts`, modified AGI without the passive loss, suspended loss carried → Schedule 1 line 5, Form 8960 4a); Form 8621 Part IV per `isPficInstrument` (`form8621.ts`; first year from `PFIC_ARRIVAL_BASIS_DAY`: greater of value and cost on Jan 1 for units then owned, later units at cost; later years from prior state → 8z and 8960 5a; sale's capital part a long-term 8949 row, `PFIC_CAPITAL_LOSS_TERM`); option lots (`optionLotResults`: written always short-term, bought by dates, assigned/exercised no row — premium enters the share walk via `equityAccountDisposalFacts(..., { optionAdjustments: true })`, 1040 only); Form 1116 passive (`form1116.ts`, `classifyChileanTaxes`: Chile's tax from the F22 non-resident part via `f22TaxOnUsWindow` in `f22/year.ts` — code 34 (filed when stored) and art. 107 1830 (not creditable) — only when Chile taxed the window; translated at the year's average `fx_daily` (`FTC_ACCRUED_TAX_TRANSLATION`, `usdClpYearlyAverage`, §986(a)(1); alt payment-day spot); limit on rents as 8582 passes them plus foreign dividends and interest; ten-year carryforward, no carryback → Schedule 3 line 1 → line 20; other countries' dividend withholding shown, not credited). Open years read through today. **Carryovers** (capital loss, passive loss, FTC, 8621 state) come from the fully composed prior draft; `buildF1040Draft` memoizes each year once per call and stops before the first drafted declared year. **The two returns read each other only via readers asserting `taxReturnOrder`** (`assertReadsEarlierReturn`): `f1040TaxForF22` (`f22/draft.ts`; a nonresident alien year reads `nonresident_alien_not_drafted`, no credit until a filed 1040 is stored) and `chileanTaxForF1040`; `f22/foreignIncome.ts` and `f22/year.ts` import no 1040 module (no cycle). **Not modelled** (payload notes): qualified-dividend holding period, wash sales, §988 (incl. UF loan repayments), Form 2210, 8621 Part V (flag `pfic_part_v_possible`), FTC carryback, covered-call straddles, Form 8833 positions (flagged), state return (`no_state_return`); FBAR / 8938 are month-end flags (exchange coins out, payerless broker cash read as Chilean), never filed. **Order:** `taxReturnOrder(incomeYear)` (`server/src/taxReturnOrder.ts`): Chilean-resident year → 1040 first (F22 foreign-salary credit reads line 24); split/non-resident → F22 first (Form 1116 reads Chile's tax); the demo's `aprilSettlementsFromTaxModules` and `writeDemoFiledReturns` follow it. Filed storage: `us_f1040_returns` / `us_f1040_filed_lines` / `us_f1040_settlements`, `recordFiledF1040` (write-once, `assertF1040Identities`), `linkF1040Settlement`. W-2s from stored stubs (`usW2.ts`); law from `usLegalParameters.ts` (unlisted closed year throws; open year uses the latest listed with `law_provisional`).
- **The F22 across the border (`f22/foreignIncome.ts`; sources quoted in the module header: AT2026 form and per-line SII instructions, Circular 31/2021, observation G08, the Chile–US convention).** A resident's US W-2 salary (`jurisdiction = 'us'` payslips via `usW2.ts`, never `payrollTaxYear`) is **1030, línea 13** (161 = 1098 + 1030; in 158): **gross less US income tax only** — Circular 31 §1.2.2.2.2 allows only Chilean-law compulsory contributions, so Social Security, Medicare, 401(k), HSA and premiums stay in — **at the 31-Dec observado, no IPC reajuste** (art. 41 A N°7 a, Circular 31 §1.3.2.2). **Credit** = the 1040's line 24 (filed when stored, else draft; FICA not creditable, convention art. 2) at the same rate, tope individual only (min of tax and 35 % of gross, `legalParameters.ts`), incremento in 748, credit in **1018 (línea 45)**, capped at the IGC on that income by Circular 31's one-decimal proportion (`f22ForeignSalaryCreditCap`: 157 × round₁(100 × (1030 + its 748) / 158) %); no refund of excess. **746 (línea 49) is a Chilean company's pass-through dividend credit (Certificados 43/44/70/71), not a person's foreign dividends** (those are 1104/748/1018); a filed 746 is a 304 credit (`f22Code304`: 157 − 136 − 1637 − 162 − 1018 − 610 − 746), never drafted. US salary before `CHILE_US_TREATY_FIRST_INCOME_YEAR` (2024) throws. **Foreign broker sweep/SGOV interest** (`savings_earnings` on a `FOREIGN_BROKERS` holder's dollar cash) and US bank interest (`usBankAccounts.ts`) are 1104 at 31-Dec observado, no credit; dividends via `movement_dividend_details`. Payload `foreign_income` (`TaxReturnForeignIncome`), F22 tab «Foreign income». **DJ 1898 is one column per lender** (`informedDjSum` sums; the art. 55 bis cap spans them); `mortgageInterestForYear(year, loanAccountId?)` reads one loan. **Demo April loop** files in `taxReturnOrder`'s order: 1040 settlements (`aprilSettlementsFromTaxModules`: refund «IRS TREAS 310 TAX REF» 21 days after filing ~8th, or payment «IRS USATAXPYMT» on the 15th, on US checking, via `recordFiledF1040` + `linkF1040Settlement`) and the F22's; the simulator's first 1040 estimate is W-2 + interest (`f1040LinesFromW2s`), so extra passes are needed for dividends and salary. Known: a resident's dollar spending abroad stays a dollar-lot disposal (user's decision). **DJ 1898 columns are headed by the lender (`lenderNames.ts`):** `lenderDisplayName(loanAccountId)` from the `lender_pattern` role (vendor catalog name for the first known pattern, else the pattern as a name; no role throws); the demo's `taxDocuments.ts` uses it. The draft reads columns by the interest header, so the heading changes nothing computed.

## Projections (/projections)

- `GET /api/projections` (`server/src/projections.ts`, pure `runProjectionEngine`): one **real** (today's-money) trajectory in the display unit — accumulation to 65 (born 1992-01 → 2057-01; invested compounds monthly + aporte, RE/cash flat real), then three drawdown strategies over the **drawdown pot** = invested + `liquidate_other_pct`% of the rest (default 0, FIRE framing) to `end_age`. `monthly_rent` adds passive income to every strategy; the fixed-income strategy funds only `target − rent` (SWR fixed-real %, % of balance, fixed real income; depletion ages in `summary`). Invested trajectory = `proj_invested`. Nominal line = real path × the unit's inflation (`inflation_pct[unit]`, query `inflation_<code>_pct`, defaults `PROJECTION_INFLATION_DEFAULT_PCT`: CLP 3,5 %, USD 2,5 %, EUR 2,0 %, served as `inflation_default_pct`; a core currency without a default throws) — all units share one trajectory. Money inputs (`monthly_aporte`, `monthly_income`, `monthly_rent`) are in `<key>_currency` (default the unit), converted at today's stored rate (`projectionParamsFromQuery`), echoed in the unit. Defaults: aporte = trailing-24m personal deposits into retirement+brokerage; base = last `getDashboardOverviewBlock` point; params bounded (`ProjectionParamError` → 400). USD milestones (250k–2M) as constant reference columns at today's stored rate.
- Client `ProjectionsPage` (`/projections`, nav link seeded by `seedNavTree`): inputs show server-resolved params; overrides persist in localStorage `nw-tracker.projections.overrides.v2` as changed query params (money inputs with their currency; old `nw-tracker.projections.overrides` migrated once by `projectionsOverrides.ts`; empty = default; Restablecer clears); unit follows the global unit; AppLineChart with dashed reference lines.

## Net-worth portfolio tree (`portfolio_groups`)

- **Single nav tree** from `net_worth`: sidebar and group pages use `portfolio_group_items` (`accountIdsInPortfolioGroup` in `server/src/portfolioGroupTree.ts`).
- **`group_kind`**: `bucket` (routable leaf/hub with accounts), `nav_bucket` (sidebar only — home cards use children with `dashboard_bucket_slug`), `liability_group`, `reference`.
- **Pasivos › tarjeta de crédito holds its cards directly (migration 262).** No issuer groups or pages (`/liabilities/credit-card/<issuer>` 404s): `seedLiabilitiesTree` links every operational card master (not superseded) to `liabilities_credit_card` as `liability_group_items`, in issuer order (`listCreditCardMasterAccountIdsInNavOrder`). Issuer = `credit_card_account_config.issuer` (import key is identity only); `listCreditCardMasterAccountIdsForIssuer` serves `import:cc-parsed --santander`. A nav-retired card leaves the nav tree but stays in `member_account_ids`, tab rows and charts. Pasivos group colour (migration 263): `liability_groups.color_rgb` (`PATCH /api/liability-groups/:slug/color`), else the largest member's (`colorRgbOfLargestBalanceAccount`); nav node carries `liability_group_id`. «Cuentas en esta vista» (`enrichNavTreeWithAllAccounts`) places accounts the nav omits under the deepest group whose `member_account_ids` names them, throwing when none does — never by slug matching. The Pasivos root's members are its children's (`withLiabilitiesChildren`, `navTree.ts`).
- **`kind_slug`**: behavior id (`afp`, `cuenta_corriente`, …) on leaf buckets — use `kindSlugForAccount` / `accountKindSlugForAccountId`, never parse `__`.
- **`accounts.primary_portfolio_group_id`**: deepest portfolio link (migration `098_…`); set by `seedNavTree`.
- **API**: prefer `?portfolio_group=retirement_afp_afc` on `/api/accounts`, valuation TS, consolidated tables. Legacy `?group=&subgroup=` resolves via `resolvePortfolioGroupSlugForLegacyTab`.
- **Home bucket cards**: `getDashboardLayoutCards()` unwraps `nav_bucket` under `net_worth` (`inversiones` → brokerage + retirement; `cash_eqs` → `cash_savings` only).
- **Bucket totals**: `accountIdsInPortfolioGroupForTotals(portfolio_group_slug)`, not `dashboard_bucket_slug` tags. `portfolio_groups.slug` / `kind_slug` are stable ids, not `__` heuristics.
- **A managed portfolio is one bucket under Acciones (migrations 190, 192–194, `linkBucketSubtree` in `seedNavTree.ts`).** A broker-managed portfolio (Racional «Portafolio IPSA»: ETF + the caja its 1 %/yr fee is charged from as `cash_fee`) is a brokerage bucket with its OWN asset group, members' leaf asset groups **reparented** under it — leaf slugs and behavior kinds (`accountBucketKindSlug`) unchanged; `portfolioCajaClpAccountId`'s `brokerage_cash__caja_*` glob still finds the caja. Filed apart the fee reads as a huge loss on a tiny cash account; together the % is (holding P/L − fee) ÷ portfolio. It sits under `brokerage_acciones` as «IPSA» (`/inversiones/brokerage/acciones/ipsa`, slug `brokerage_portafolio_ipsa`, members «CFIETFIPSA», «Caja IPSA»). **Portfolio groups may hold accounts beside sub-buckets** (credit-card and liability trees keep «sub-groups XOR accounts»): `assertNavGroupItemsShape` requires each account in ≤ 1 group and linked groups / expense places unmixed; it runs in the seed transaction (violation rolls back). `linkBucketSubtree` links own leaf accounts plus, as child groups, buckets filed under it (`portfolio_groups.parent_id`) whose asset group nests in its own, by name. A sub-bucket beside accounts reads as a leaf of that page: one line per first-level child in Agrupado by balance (`mixedChartChildren`; Sin agrupar opens it), one table row with server card metrics and `card_metrics_by_slug[slug].row_pct`, one breakdown line, a non-expanding sidebar entry. The Panel offers a bucket as account home when it has no sub-buckets or already holds accounts (`resolveBucketParentAssetSlug`, `listLeafPortfolioGroupBuckets`). A new portfolio = asset group + leaf reparent + `portfolio_groups` row, no code; nav label = data label. Caveat: the Racional fee import needs exactly one `brokerage_cash__caja_*` account; a second portfolio needs that lookup keyed on the bucket.
- **Broker cash balances are cash, not investments (migration 241).** Racional CLP (`import:panel|kind=clp|key=clp`) and USD (`…|kind=usd|key=usd`) sit under Efectivo › Cash savings (`cash_eqs__cash_savings__racional_clp__clp` / `…_racional_usd__usd`), like Fintual's; no Brokerage › Cash bucket exists (demo: `cash_eqs__cash_savings__usd`). Portfolio cajas keep `brokerage_cash__caja_*` under the unit's asset group (matched by slug). So a `stock_buy` from Racional CLP is an aporte into Brokerage and sale proceeds a withdrawal. `accountUsesEquityMtm` must exempt CLP cash as it does USD cash (the `stock_sell` proceeds leg is the wallet).
- **Panel / nav / dashboard** share the tree: `GET /api/meta/sidebar-nav` → `net_worth`. `/api/meta/asset-tree` (legacy `asset_groups`) is deprecated; don't use it in new UI.

## Group page charts — server-side "Agrupado" bucketing

**Grouped chart series come from the server; the client never re-aggregates.** `server/src/groupChartBuckets.ts` (bucket selection ported from the sidebar `NavTreeNodeDto` walk) emits sibling fields on existing responses, so the toggle is instant:

- **Valuation** (`getGroupValuationTimeseries(..., { groupedBlocks: true })`, `valuationTimeseries.ts`): `nav_grouped_blocks: { grouped, ungrouped }` + `nav_grouped_proportional`, or `liab_grouped_block` + `liab_grouped_proportional` (Pasivos, no toggle). Built from the **pre-clip consolidated** block with `__group_val_total` / `__group_dep_total` (and Total) copied **verbatim**, then each block display-clipped independently.
- **Monthly P/L bars** (`getGroupMonthlyPlSeries(..., { groupedBlocks: true })`, `groupMonthlyPlSeries.ts`): `nav_grouped_bars` / `liab_grouped_bars`, keys `pl_nav_<slug>` / `pl_liab_<slug>` = per-row sums of member `pl_<id>`; unbucketed accounts keep their bar, so bars add to `delta_total`.
- Only group HTTP routes pass `groupedBlocks: true`. Client `groupPageChartViews.ts` only **picks** by toggle (`resolveGroupPageChartContext` reads toggle visibility from payload presence).
- **Pasivos buckets** read each liability node's `member_account_ids` (incl. retired cards); a card bucket is the card — never name heuristics.
- Bucket labels carry `name_i18n_key` (translate at render, never cache `t()`; deposit-line keys `charts.groupAccumulatedDeposits` / `charts.accumulatedDeposits`).

**Tail clip is display-only** (`timeseriesTailClip.ts`, final step): nulls sold-out trailing series and clips each line's aportes with its value, but **never** rewrites an aggregate. `__group_val_total` is the only line excluded from deposit bundling; `__group_val_total` / `__group_dep_total` are never recomputed post-clip. Don't Σ clipped series on the client (totals would disagree with the summary card).

## An account's value: what it holds, converted once (`docs/holdings-currency-plan.md`, `accountValue.ts`, `holdingPrices.ts`, `valueConvert.ts`, `rates.ts`)

- **Value at a date = quantity of what the account holds, converted ONCE into the display unit.** `accountHolding(id)` (`holdings.ts`): a currency (pesos, dollars; property and mortgage in UF), a stock (quote currency), a coin (dollar price series), fund cuotas (peso-priced) or a card. `accountHoldingQtyAt(id, ymd)` = quantity; `holdingPriceAt(instrument, ymd)` = unit price in its quote currency (past day: stored observation on/before, history tier; today: live quote in session, held print after close, display-session bar, newest bar; a fund's live/per-date Risky Norris proxy); `accountValueAt(id, ymd, unit)` = qty × price → `convertValue` (`valueConvert.ts` over `rates.ts`). Never build pesos and convert back. Historical stack `accountHistoricalQtyMark` (`accountMarkClpAtYmd.ts`: branches return quantities; an instrument is the asset only on days its price is on file, else fall through); `accountMarkClpAtYmd` = that in pesos.
- **Spread side = what closes the position.** Assets: foreign → CLP at bid, CLP → foreign at ask; liabilities flip (`holdingIsDebt`). UF and EUR have no spread. Stored days read `fx_daily` with bid/ask observed that day or later, else mid ∓ half `FX_BID_ASK_SPREAD_CLP`. Flows convert per event at the mid (`flowEventInUnit`, `convertTs`, `clpToUnitAtMid`), as do benchmark levels.
- **Today reads the live rate** (USD/CLP while the fx day is open, `fxForLiveMtm` in `rates.ts`). `accountTodayValue(id, slug, opts, now) → {value_clp, native_value, native_currency, side, as_of_date, priced_on}` is the one today value (dashboard row, header, today's mark, perf live row, every chart live point); other units convert the NATIVE value with `todayValueInUnit(id, v, unit)`. `priced_on` is informational only.
- **`accountValueClpToUnitAt(id, clp, ymd, unit)`** converts a peso-native account value at the account's side and re-reads a value native in another currency (`accountValueCurrency`) from its holding. `clpToUnitAt(clp, ymd, unit, {side})` is for amounts that aren't one account's value (cards netted from cash, liability totals at `side: "debt"`). Bucket/net-worth sums in a unit = Σ each account's value in that unit per day (`buildDashboardBucketDailySeriesInUnit`, daily series, overview, ATH) — never Σ pesos ÷ one rate.
- **A card's value in a unit goes through one seam, `cardValueInUnit.ts`** (owed walk in pesos at the debt side); the card phase will replace it with per-currency sub-balances. The property and mortgage are still the depto ledger's pesos (peso quantities) until the loan phase.
- **Stored valuations are in any currency (migration 260, `valuationValue.ts`).** A `valuations` row is a quantity in `currency` (any lowercase code). Generic readers convert once on the row's date at the account's side (`storedValuationInUnit`: `valuationLatest.ts`, `bookValuations.ts`, `valuationTimeseries.ts`); the historical stack (`accountMarkClpAtYmd.ts`) carries book marks in pesos, so a non-peso book mark with a carry window throws. Kind-specific readers assert their currency (`assertValuationCurrency`: card CLP anchors, AFP/Fintual peso marks). Property rows are UF appraisals, left out by every mark reader (`valuationsAreAppraisals`); the mortgage has none (migration 261; the depto ledger values it, and property/mortgage chart month-ends come from the ledger, `augmentChartDatesForDeptoAccounts`). `GET /api/accounts/:id/valuations` returns `value` + `currency`; `POST` writes pesos and refuses a property.
- **The daily cache stores quantities** (`markSeriesStore.ts` cells `{qty, asset}`); unit values are built at read from memoized day price and rate (`accountValueSeriesOnGrid`), one series for every unit. Rate rows (`fx_daily`, `fx_daily_bid_ask`, `uf_daily`) trim only accounts whose quantity is a rate-built peso figure (cards — from the row's own day —, the depto ledger, book marks), never shares, coins, cuotas or dollar ledgers (`setRateIndependentAccountsResolver`). Price rows (`equity_daily`, `fund_unit_daily`) trim their holders (which asset a day holds depends on price availability).

## Nav card metrics — server-side

**Card figures (balance, deposits, cumulative P/L, period Δ, %) come from the server; the client never re-sums rows for a nav node.** Every card renders Día/Mes/Año at once (no period preference, no `title_delta`). `server/src/dashboardNavCardMetrics.ts` walks the net_worth tree and emits `card_metrics_by_slug: Record<slug, { child, parent }>` on page-bundle dash, nav-context and nav-snapshot (each `{ day, month, year }`; `child` = strip detail card, `parent` = the node's own page card; e.g. net_worth parent = Σ strip-children child metrics; inversiones parent bakes in `inversiones_period_metrics`). Scope rules (membership, cash_eqs savings-only period P/L, chart-inactive coverage) live only here (`dashboardNavCardMetrics.test.ts`).

- **One series behind card and table (`accountPeriodMetrics.ts`).** A row's month/year/lifetime deposits and P/L = `accountPeriodMetricsFromMonthlyRows` over `monthlyPerfRowsForAccount(id, slug, unit)` — the rows the group consolidation sums (`groupMonthlyPerfConsolidation.ts`: valuation-series perf for invested accounts, ledger walks for checking / USD / CLP cash / cuenta de ahorro) via the same picker (`pickMonthlyPerfRowsByMonth`, one row per month with flows summed). Card = Σ rows, «Detalle por mes / año» = Σ per month, equal to the cent in every unit (`dashboardCardMetricsVsConsolidated.test.ts`); lifetime P/L = «P/L acum.», never `current − deposits` (a break means a hole in the series). The USD frame keeps the peso's move inside P/L on every row, property included — never substitute CLP at today's rate. **A sold-out line keeps its zero** (`trailingZeroRunClipStartIndex`): densified null month-ends must not count as the zero run, or the liquidation zero and its realized loss vanish. **The live month row's USD frame is the mark date's fx** (`usdFromClpAtLiveFrame`); crypto positions are dated on their pesos' framing day (`accountPosition.ts`: UTC session with a live quote, else mark date), not the bar's `trade_date`. One today value (`accountTodayValue`) and one conversion (`accountValueClpToUnitAt`) feed card, header, perf live row, today's mark and chart live points.
- **One month series per account, every reader.** Every month-P/L surface reads `monthlyPerfRowsForAccount` through `pickMonthlyPerfRowsByMonth`; its invested-account builder `valuationSeriesMonthlyPerformance` (`accountPerformance.ts`) has no other production caller. (1) **Group P/L charts** (`groupMonthlyPlSeries.ts`, `GET /api/groups/:slug/performance-monthly`): bars = picked rows; `delta_total` / `ytd_group` / `accumulated_earnings` = consolidated `nominal_pl` / `ytd_nominal_pl` / `cumulative_nominal_pl`, one point per month (current dated today); checking included, excluded accounts not, so Σ bars = `delta_total` = detalle row (`groupMonthlyPlSeriesVsConsolidated.test.ts`). The home «Δ mensual y depósitos por clase» combo is served (`inversionesPerfSeries.ts`): bundle `inversionesPerf` (= `buildInversionesConsolidatedMonthly`, deposits joined) and `GET /api/dashboard/inversiones-perf-daily`, levels anchored on the monthly consolidation; the client only clips and rolls years. **Day-mode P/L combos are served (`dailyPerfLevels.ts`):** `GET /api/daily-series?…&perf_levels=1` adds index-aligned `perf_levels` {`delta_total`, `ytd`, `accumulated`} — day `pl` with levels anchored on the scope's monthly rows (groups `getGroupConsolidatedMonthlyRows`; accounts and the mortgage section `monthlyPerfRowsForAccount`; `anchorDailyLevels` makes the window's first month-end equal the monthly row). Client maps `pl` to bar keys (`servedDailyPerfComboPoints`); `perf` is its own query entity (never a placeholder for the plain payload) (`dailyPerfLevels.test.ts`). Net worth's daily P/L needn't sum to the monthly. (2) **Account readers** (detalle, Rentabilidad, `/api/accounts/:id/performance-monthly`, XLSX export, benchmark row, tail-inactive detection) read `monthlyPerfForAccountId(id, unit)` = `{account_id, bucket_slug, monthly, yearly}` (`accountDetailMonthlyVsCard.test.ts`); the page hides the section for checking and cuenta de ahorro. Ledger-cash rows carry `pct_month` (`flowAdjustedPct`). (3) **Cuenta de ahorro** closed months take deposit events per `(prev month-end, month-end]` at each event's rate (`ledgerCashNetFlowBetween`); `balanceOnlyMonthlyRowsAsc` requires `netFlowBetween` (balance − interest would put fx revaluation in flow); dispatched by kind (leaf `cash_eqs__cuenta_ahorro_vivienda`), never bare slug. (4) **Credit card rows** = `accountPeriodMetricsFromMonthlyRows(monthlyPerfRowsForAccount(card))` (`creditCardMonthPerf`: −financing per CALENDAR month); day P/L to dollars via `convertTs` at today; Pasivos / credit-card cards follow (`dashboardCcCardVsDetalle.test.ts`). No rows → «—», not 0. (5) **Yearly rows and running totals built once** (`monthlyPerfRollup.ts`): `rollupMonthlyPerfYearly` serves every «Detalle por año» (`consolidated_yearly`, account `yearly`, paginated `period=year`; `MonthlyPerfDetailTable` throws without `yearlyRows`); a year with no P/L or % reads null, never 0; `withRunningPlTotals` builds YTD/cumulative; `monthEndCloseForAccountInUnit` (accountPerformance.ts) is the month-end close.
- Client `portfolioNavDashboardCards.ts` only **picks**: `requireNavCardMetrics(dash, node)` (fail-fast on missing slug), used by `mainValueForNavChild` / `navParentMainValue` / `navChildCardHasActivity`, `PortfolioNavAccountsSummaryTable` and `navCardBreakdown.ts`. **Balances are server-side:** each entry's `value` — `child` (detail card and parent table row: the bucket total for a bucket's own node and every cash child, CC-netted, else Σ counted member rows), `parent` (summary card and table «Total»: one bucket total, Σ bucket totals for net worth/hubs, else Σ subtree rows), `members` (Σ counted member rows, never netted: breakdown group lines), `fx_missing` / `sync_stale`; `liquidated.value` for «Liquidadas». Input: the payload's `buildDashboardNwBucketTotals`. Single-account rows are 1-row projections (`cardMetricsByPeriodForAccountRow`). The client's one copy, the counts-toward-totals filter (`accountGroupTotals.ts`), is pinned by `server/src/test/accountCountsTowardGroupTotalsCases.json` in both suites.
- The localStorage nav snapshot (`dashboardNavSnapshotCache.ts`) is a persisted payload (`queries/persistedPayloadCache.ts`, key `nw:cache:<name>:v<version>`, version = `schema_version` from `DTO_SCHEMA_VERSIONS`); shown with its own numbers, spinning to live ones when the bundle lands (no scaling/random seeding; `nw:numberFlowMount:*` keys swept at boot). A unit is served only by its own cache — never synthesize USD from CLP; a first USD visit keeps the dimmed CLP cards until USD lands, blank only when neither unit has data. **A cached snapshot must cover the current nav tree (`navSnapshotCoversNavTree`):** every net_worth group node and the Pasivos root needs a `card_metrics_by_slug` entry, else `readDashboardNavSnapshotCache` drops it (checked against the sidebar-nav cache) and `useDashboardNavSnapshot` resets one in the query cache — otherwise `requireNavCardMetrics` crashes pages across reloads. Only localStorage placeholders are checked; a live payload missing a slug throws.
- **Membership comes from the tree, never slug prefixes.** Each group node carries `member_account_ids` (as `portfolio_group_items` and liability/credit-card items link them, chart-inactive leaves included); `navMetricsAccountIdSet` (`dashboardNavCardMetrics.ts`) = nav leaves ∪ that list. Prefix matching breaks on a managed portfolio's reparented leaves. A chart-inactive sub-bucket row follows the sold-out rule (`navChildCardHasActivity`: shown while any period has activity).
- **A card line's label is the account's name** (rows carry no `nav_label`; strip breakdowns and accounts table read `name`; no kind-slug short labels). The real-estate card lists several properties at the first level with valor and hipoteca under each (`buildRealEstateCardBreakdown`); one property keeps head line = property.
- **A hub the strip spreads into children's cards gets its own summary card in row 1.** `portfolioStripSummaryHubs` (`portfolioNavFromApi.ts`, same predicate that spreads the hub) adds one per such hub, from the tree, never a slug, drawn by the same `NavSummaryCard` as the hub page's hero (`parent` variant + `value.parent` via `navParentMainValue`), so home Inversiones ≡ `/inversiones`. With several summary cards each is titled (the page's own too). Row 1 holds only summary cards; on desktop the next card starts a new row (`cards.css`).

## Data provenance (`DataOrigin`)

API payloads must **not** use top-level `source: "db"` / `"csv"` — the client always reads SQLite. Provenance describes how data entered:

| Layer | Field | Examples |
|-------|--------|----------|
| Account | `accounts.import_key` (identity; `notes` = same string as human provenance) | `import:excel|key=mortgage`, `credit_card_master|santander|<last4>`, `import:panel|ticker=QQQ|key=…` |
| Row | DTO `origin` | `import_document`, `manual` (from `cc_installment_purchases.source`: `pdf` → `import_document`) |
| Market data | (future) | `api_sync` / `api_yahoo` for `equity_daily` |

Shared type: `server/src/dataOrigin.ts` (`import_document` \| `manual` \| `api_sync`).

## Import sources vs runtime

**Rule:** files under `cfraser/` are **import inputs only**. HTTP, dashboard, valuation, and sync read **SQLite**; missing data → error or “run import”, not a silent file fallback.

- **Depto:** one movement ledger per property (`loadDeptoLedgerForProperty(id)`, cached `depto.ledger|rows|<property id>`; `deptoLedgerForAccount` / `deptoLedgerForLoan` / `loadAllDeptoLedgers`). Pairs come from `propertyLoanPairs()` (`accountRoles.ts`: a `property` role holder and its `loan_of` account); extra properties are `import:property|key=<k>` / `import:loan|property=<k>` (`propertyAccountImportKey` / `loanAccountImportKey`; migration 299's triggers seed the roles). Every reader («Próximos pagos», valor/hipoteca overlays, mortgage sections, card pairing, real-estate mortgage rows) loops over the pairs; the lender portal reading names its loan by `lender_pattern`. The ledger joins property-account movements with **`loan_payments`** (migration 258; docs/holdings-currency-plan.md phase 4): one row per payment movement, `leg` `property` | `loan`, `loan_account_id`, cuota, origin, and the bank's figures in its own currency — `balance_after`, `min_payment`, `max_payment` in `loan_currency` (UF); `principal`, `extra_principal`, `interest`, `insurance_fire`, `insurance_life` in `payment_currency` (pesos); readers throw on any other pair. Nothing derived is stored (parts' UF = pesos ÷ UF día, 4 decimals, the pago 5; net equity; `pagado_neto_uf`; `pago_acumulado_clp`; deltas/acumulados are computed from `uf_daily`). The appraisal is a **`valuations` row of the property account in UF**, forward-filled by the ledger; a property's valuations are its appraisals (`valuationsAreAppraisals`, `valuationValue.ts`), so generic mark readers skip them and its chart samples month-ends from its first ledger row (`augmentChartDatesForPropertyAccounts`); its value is net equity (`deptoNetEquityUfBySnapshotDates`). Rows are **sequential daily balances**: order = `deptoLedgerChronoCompare` (date, then decreasing crédito restante for same-day prepago+cuota); `assertDeptoPrepagoSequencing` fails when a prepago raises the balance (regular cuotas may: grace-period capitalization); snapshot fills use exact-date cutoffs with prepagos included — never a month-end cutoff expansion or a prepago skip (one-day equity spikes, ± P/L couplets). `mortgagePaymentCreate` writes the movements (human notes, mortgage `flow_kind`) plus both `loan_payments` rows; manual-payment recompute updates in place. No `depto_snapshot`: the RE card synthesizes valor/hipoteca from the property + mortgage rows.
- **CC installments:** `import:cc-parsed` / PDF → `cc_installment_*` / `cc_statements`; no runtime CSV.
- **CC parse is incremental.** The per-PDF parse cache key is **(PDF sha256, parser version) only** — never mtime/size (they drift on copies). A cache hit never reads the PDF: the readability peek/OCR and qpdf repair run on misses only, and the hit validates cached meta (`require_statement_meta`). Two downloads of the same facturación under different names make the incremental import ping-pong the statement's row between source names: put exact duplicates (and a re-download named by its pay-by date) in the card slot's `duplicates/` (excluded by `is_excluded_cc_pdf_path`, like `pending-review/`). `server/scripts/remove-cc-duplicate-download-statements.ts` (report-first) removes copies stored as their own closes.
- **Scanned documents use one OCR model (`ingest/python/ocr_tessdata.py`).** A different `eng.traineddata` build reads scans differently (and fails the reconcile). `use_expected_tessdata()` (card, payroll, checking-cartola OCR) takes `TESSDATA_PREFIX`, else the first standard folder (Homebrew, MacPorts, `/usr/local`, `/usr`) whose model has the expected sha256, else throws listing each folder. The home server keeps a copy of the laptop's model and sets `TESSDATA_PREFIX` in its ingest plist. Changing the model = re-parse every scan, then update the hash.
- **CC statement import is incremental (migrations 171, 292).** The import (`card.parsed_statements`; `import:cc-parsed` by hand) skips a statement whose `import_fingerprint` is unchanged: a hash of the statement's and each line's stored columns (`cards/statementStorage.ts` defines it; `ccStatementFingerprint.ts` filters), written by `importCcStatementsMerge` because only it knows which row the statement resolved to (the close-date fallback can match another `source_pdf`). It hashes what the ledger keeps, so two renderings of one statement hash alike; identity, derived columns (kind, origin label, dedupe keys, ids) and lines dropped as repeats stay out. A parser change touching only unstored fields (plan principal columns, layout, canonical row id) needs `--full`. A statement that does import gets the full reconcile. **`--full`** forces the from-scratch pass (`import:cfraser-inbox -- --full` passes it through).
- **CC statement source ownership:** `cc_statements` is UNIQUE on (account_id, card_group, source_pdf, statement_date), so two sources for one facturación would duplicate it. Ownership is per (close, currency) and **the PDF outranks the JSON** (`ccStatementJsonSource.ts`) — the JSON arrives first but is thin (the international endpoint is dateless with all-NULL headers, so a JSON USD statement has lines only), and first-writer-wins would lose the later PDF. `npm run import:santander-statements [-- --apply]` (ingest sends `card.statement`; server `cardStatementApply.ts` over `santanderStatementImport.ts`) cross-checks PDF-owned closes (report only) and writes unowned ones through `mergeCcAccountFromParsedRows` as `import:santander-json|<currency>|<close>`; a later PDF supersedes a JSON-owned close inside that merge (JSON rows deleted, lines cascade, `json_closes_superseded_by_pdf`; their keys go into `replaceStatementKeys` so the reconcile doesn't count them). The cross-check (`pairStatementLines`) pairs by normalized merchant + |amount|, then amount + merchant prefix («matched by prefix»: the PDF layout can glue the charge-type column, «… COMPRAS P.A.T.»; the parser is left alone because a version bump re-parses the corpus), then by rendering (`matched_by_rendering`: a JSON-only trailing 4-digit terminal code, punctuation). JSON statements are real statements elsewhere: they count for facturación close and open-web-paste supersede (`isPdfStatementSource` = not web-paste), join traspaso links, and dedupe in a `json|`-prefixed key space. The import refuses when the bank's Cuenta→master disagrees with the registry's plastic routing (`assertNoCardRoutingConflict`) and skips an international response that re-serves an older cycle (`usdStatementIsStaleEcho`). **Currencies pair on (account, NumExtracto)** (`assembleSantanderStatementBatch`, `ingest/src/santander/statementJson.ts`): the international file takes close, pay-by, next close and titular card only from the same extracto's national (extracto from the staged name `<Cuenta>-extracto-<NNN>-…` or a capture's `requestBody.INPUT.NumExtracto`; none or `extracto-0` throws); without a twin it is «unpaired»; the batch throws only when ambiguous (one extracto with two closes or differing copies, two extractos for one close). Writes go per (account, close); `--apply` archives a superseded, fully verified facturación (not the account's newest extracto) into `santander-statement-json/archive/`; dirty groups stay («KEPT»).
- **Account identity:** `accounts.import_key` (unique, nullable; migration 159) is the find-or-create key and well-known lookup (`import:excel|key=mortgage`, `import:fintual|cert|key=…`, `credit_card_master|<issuer>|<last4>`, `import:panel|…`); every creator writes it; `notes` holds the same string for humans, never read by code. Card→account resolution uses `credit_card_account_config.card_last4` (every master has a config row).
- **Per-card CC behavior is config:** redirects, superseded masters and classification tokens load from gitignored `cfraser/cc-cards.json` (`server/src/ccCardRegistry.ts`, `ingest/python/cc_cards.py`; missing = empty registry for demo/CI). Tests use `server/src/test/ccCardsFixture.json` (`NW_TRACKER_CC_CARDS`, in `vitest.config.ts`); never hardcode real last4s. Real values: PARSERS.md.
- **Fintual certificado:** `npm run import:fintual-cert` (ingest sends `fund_account.transactions`; server `fintualCertImport.ts`), run by `import:cfraser-inbox` (report only) when one lands. Non-destructive: `--apply` only ADDS rows no flow covers (matching includes mirror-merge/manual transfer legs, exact amount ±5 days); never deletes or overwrites, keeps externally-set flow kinds (the APV-A state bonus looks like a personal deposit there). Deposit kind goes to `movements.flow_kind` at import (state bonus / traspaso; plain deposits NULL). The account ensure sets `fund_series_key`.
- **Fintual retiros are synthesized from the mail.** «Pagamos tu retiro» states amount, payment date, goal and cuotas, so `import:fintual-emails` writes the goal→checking transfer at once (else the NAV drop sits in P/L until the bank leg; the hourly poll can't fetch it). One row whichever side lands first: an imported credit is **promoted in place** (`fintualWithdrawalPairing.ts`); a synthesized transfer absorbs the bank's later row through the checking importers' `superseded_by_transfer` skip (`findMatchingInternalTransferLegId`, signed amount + posting window) in xlsx and cartola. Syntheses are recorded in **`fintual_synthetic_retiro_transfers`** (migration 177, message-id UNIQUE; leaf `fintualSyntheticRetiros.ts` avoids an import cycle); the skip stamps `confirmed_on` (the only trace, since it inserts nothing). `import:fintual-emails` exits non-zero when a synthesis is unconfirmed past paid_on + 2 Chile business days (else the next anchor re-derivation silently absorbs a phantom credit). Last-business-day retiros are synthesized too; the next-month bank row records its posting day (see «Bank posting day»). **Retiro to the Fintual balance** («… quedaron disponibles para invertir en Fintual … equivalente a N cuotas»): `paid_to: "fintual"`, goal → «Fintual CLP» (`import:panel|kind=clp|key=fintual_clp`) with the cuotas, no synthesis record. «Devolvimos tu saldo» (balance wired back after 7 days) is manual.
  - **Gmail IMAP `SINCE` returns nothing on a DST-transition day** (evaluated in the account's zone): `fetchBrokerEmails` searches from watermark − 1 day, then − 2 if empty (`searchSinceCandidates`); the exact-timestamp filter drops seen mails.
  - **Scan files overlap, so mails collapse by Message-ID** before sending (`collapseBrokerEmailEventsByMessageId`, `ingest/src/brokers/brokerEmail.ts`; the `broker.notifications` contract refuses a repeated message id): `SINCE` is day-granular and `stagedScans.ts` concatenates every `scan-*.json`, and a duplicated retiro would promote twice and roll back the batch. The fetcher drops messages `<= last_seen_at` (`fetch.ts`); `refusePromoteCollisions` sends a second in-batch claim on a credit to `requires_manual`. **The pairing window is `[paid_on, paid_on + 5]`** (`findUnpairedCheckingCredit`): a wire never posts before payment, and a two-sided window claims unrelated earlier credits. Repair: `server/scripts/unpromote-checking-credit.ts --movement-id=NN --note='<original partialMovementNote key>'` (report-first, `--apply`; the note is the xlsx dedupe identity, byte-exact), then re-import.
- **AFP UNO:** cuota ledger built from the full-history Certificado de Movimientos and the SP's official valor cuota, one account per fund manager (`server/scripts/split-afp-ledger-into-fund-accounts.ts`; see «The AFP account's ledger and price»). Cuotas = Σ `units_delta` + transfer legs, no snapping. New rows from the nightly certificate read (`pension_account.certificates`), price from `afp_uno`.
- **Statement line sections are one rule set:** `server/contracts/data/ccStatementLineRules.json` (payment literals, ABONO DE DIVISAS, traspaso tokens, CLP/USD section-3 patterns, garbled-row pattern, layout lists), read by `ccStatementLineRules.ts` and `ingest/python/cc_statement_line_rules.py`; `classifyCcStatementLine` / `classify_statement_line` place every non-installment line for the import gate and the parse check; both suites assert `ccStatementLineSectionCases.json`. A USD payment line is never section 3; BCI section-3 rows net by layout. The file is in the parse-cache version (editing it re-parses once).
- **A charge named «PAGO …» is a charge** (`_is_printed_payment`, `parse-cc-statement-pdfs.py`): «PAGO FACIL», «PAGOS.FLOW.CL (WEB)», «PAGOS.F*COMUNIDAD …», «PAGOS MASIVOS …» print positive. A payment is an exact payment name, or opens like one and prints negative. The reconcile's operaciones check is skipped when the period had a payment, so **`operaciones_shortfall`** (`cc_statement_reconcile.py`) fails a text-read Santander peso statement whose operaciones fall more than a peso short of «TOTAL OPERACIONES» (more is a refund set aside; OCR scans exempt).
- **Two identical-ish lines on one statement are two charges:** `oneShotLineFuzzyMatchExists` ignores lines this import wrote into the same statement (`excludeLineIds`); lines already on file still dedupe re-imports.
- **Stale copies from an old card-group assignment** (`scripts/replace-stale-cc-statement-copies.ts`, report-first, IMMEDIATE): the card group comes from the file name, so a regrouped statement gets a second copy keyed by the new group while dedupe leaves the lines on the old one (double-counted). The script deletes the stale rows and re-imports the twins in full, carrying assignments, failing when a paired payment loses evidence. Statements the parse reads as zero rows keep their old rows (the only record).
- **International rows come from whole printed lines:** a vertical chunk is a line only with exactly one line's two amounts and no «N DE M» counter; otherwise it is `VERTICAL_MULTI_LINE`, keeps its merge slot (stable row ids and fingerprints) and is dropped unless a whole row replaces it (else page numbers, other lines' US$, or a payment's origin become charges). In the layout reader PAÍS is the two-letter cell before the two amounts, and it is the merchant's country, not the charge currency. Cleanup: `server/scripts/remove-cc-intl-extraction-artifacts.ts` (report-first, `--apply`).
- **Payroll labels read their own line:** `amount_after_label` never crosses a line break and skips a rate before the amount («Isapre 7%»); Talana header columns go through `amount_in_header_column` (0 when blank). After a parser change review `import:payroll-liquidaciones -- --dry-run`.
- **Card line origin currency is decided by value, on import (`ccOriginCurrency.ts`):** the parser writes MONTO MONEDA ORIGEN as printed and leaves `orig_currency` empty (column kept so peso fingerprints don't move); `importCcStatementsMerge` labels with `ccLineOriginCurrency`: `usd` when origin = US$ to the cent, `clp` when origin ÷ US$ is within 5 % of `fx_daily` on/before the date (+ half a cent ÷ |US$|), else null — never by country — and refuses pre-labelled records. The JSON writes its origin as the same Chilean cell (`ccOriginAmountCsvCell`); web paste has none. Nothing computes with these fields. Relabel stored lines with `scripts/relabel-cc-line-origin-currency.ts [--origins-csv=…] [--apply]` after the parse + import that follow a parser change.
- **One parser per format.** dd/mm/yyyy → ISO: `parseDdMmYyToIso` (`server/contracts/src/formats/dates.ts`; card code via `normalizeTransactionDateIso`; a line's date is `statementLineDateIso`, transaction else posting, `ccInstallmentPayBy.ts`) and Python `parse_dd_mm_yy_to_iso` (`ingest/python/statement_values.py`), both asserting `server/contracts/data/ddMmYyToIsoCases.json` (one case file per format in `server/contracts/data/`); ISO → dd/mm/yyyy: `ddMmYyyyFromIso` (throws on non-ISO). Far-future fixtures use ISO dates (a dd/mm year past 2038 reads as a jammed token). Numbers: each source declares its style, never guessed from separators — `parseChileanNumber` / `parseUsNumber` (`formats/numbers.ts`, throw; `isChileanNumber`, `parseOptionalChileanInteger`) and Python `parse_clp_amount` (ties-to-even). Own copies remain in BCentral, Quetalmi, `ingest/src/brokers/brokerEmail.ts`, the Racional crawl (`ingest/src/racional/steps.ts`, `racionalMovements.ts`).
- **Cartola rows without a year** (`ingest/src/formats/cartolaMovementDate.ts`, `cartola_row_date` in `ingest/python/cartola_layout.py`): candidates are the years DESDE→HASTA where dd/mm is a real date in the period; exactly one, else error (both assert `server/contracts/data/cartolaMovementYearCases.json`). PDF parsers add `PdfCartolaRowDates`: an ambiguous row (annual cartolas) takes the earliest candidate on/after the previous row, else the parse fails naming file and row. The XLSX never spans a year and has the base rule only.

Runtime code must not use `fs.readFile*` / `readSemicolonCsv` on `cfraser/` except in `server/scripts/` and import helpers. Import-sync document coverage (`GET /api/import-sync/…`) may list files for admin tooling only.

## Server runtime modes (local vs hosted demo)

One binary, env-driven (`server/src/httpSecurity.ts`, root `.env` loaded at boot):

- **The primary runs as LaunchAgent `com.user.nw-tracker-server`** (`server/com.user.nw-tracker-server.plist`, installed by `server/scripts/install-server-agent.sh`, which refuses while the port is taken): `tsx src/index.ts` without watch, KeepAlive, log `cfraser/server.log`. Restart: `launchctl kickstart -k gui/$(id -u)/com.user.nw-tracker-server` (alias `nw-server-restart` bootstraps it if unloaded). Its scheduler owns the nightly ingest runs (`docs/ingest-split-plan.md`), so it must not depend on a terminal.
- **The primary is a home server deploying from GitHub `main`** (`scripts/server-deploy.sh`, agent `com.user.nw-tracker-deploy` via `scripts/install-deploy-agent.sh`). An always-on Intel MacBook Pro (`pmset disablesleep`) runs server and ingest service; the laptop runs no agents and no live DB (dev servers use test/demo data or a snapshot copy with `BACKGROUND_JOBS_ENABLED=0`). Every 3 min the agent fetches `origin/main`; on a move it enqueues a `deploy` job (see «The job queue»; one queued, moved to the newest commit) and polls its claim ~150 s, else leaves it for the next run (stale after 15 min, so a dead agent can't drain the queue forever). The claim needs nothing else running (`server-restart`): in-flight ingest finishes, nothing new starts once it is due, and after the restart the executor holds every job until the agent completes (or the 20-min lease lapses → lost, badged). Then: snapshot `before-deploy-<sha>`, fast-forward, `npm ci` only when a package file changed, build into `client/dist.next` and swap with two renames (old kept as `dist.prev`; never build into `client/dist` in place — the live server 404s meanwhile), restart the server (and the ingest service when `ingest/`, `server/contracts/` or a package file changed), wait for `/api/health` to report the commit, complete; on failure roll back, complete as failed, never retry that commit. With the server down it deploys without the queue. Refuses commits not on `origin/main` or modified tracked files. Outcomes in `cfraser/deploy.log`, the job and app messages (failure = notification); `scripts/server-deploy.sh --check` dry-runs. Read-only deploy key. Machine differences a deploy must not undo: poppler/qpdf from MacPorts (`/opt/local/bin`, on the installed plists' PATH; tracked plists carry laptop paths, adapted at install), Intel builds of `ingest/python/.pdf_deps` / `.numbers_deps` (`pip install --target`), and npm 11 skipping unallowed install scripts (the deploy checks `better-sqlite3` loads).
- **Open tabs reload onto a deployed client** (`server/src/clientBuild.ts`, `client/src/clientBuild.ts`): the live-quotes stamp poll carries `client_build` = the served `client/dist/index.html`'s hashed `/assets/` script/stylesheet URLs, sorted, space-joined (cached on mtime+size; null without a served client). The tab compares its own boot-time id (both sides assert `server/src/test/clientBuildIdCases.json`) and, when different, reloads at its next in-app navigation or once hidden, never under a form. One reload per build (sessionStorage `nw-tracker.clientBuildReloadedFor`); a still-differing tab only warns. Production builds only.
- **`HOST`** — default `127.0.0.1` (keeps the unauthenticated API off the LAN); the demo sets `0.0.0.0`.
- **`CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`** — comma-separated allowlist; default Vite dev/preview (`localhost:5173`/`4173`). Never reflect any origin — any website could read the API.
- **`DEMO_MODE` is the only access control; there is no login.** Local mode relies on `127.0.0.1`; the demo is **read-only**: `demoReadOnlyMiddleware` (`httpSecurity.ts`, mounted with `DEMO_MODE=1`) answers `403 { error: "demo_read_only" }` to non-GET/HEAD/OPTIONS `/api` requests, which the client maps to `demo.readOnly` (`client/src/api.ts`). Exempt: the analytics beacon (`DEMO_PAGEVIEW_PATH`) and the edit allowlist («The demo is writable, a little»). **Never put real data behind `DEMO_MODE`.**
- **`DB_BACKUP_ENABLED` / `DB_BACKUP_KEEP` / `DB_BACKUP_DIR` / `DB_BACKUP_DIR_KEEP`** — daily snapshot while the server runs (`dbBackupScheduler.ts`; default on). Retention (`dbSnapshotRetention.ts`, pure): one per day for `DB_BACKUP_KEEP` days (30), then one per month, for every label (`auto-daily`, `before-deploy-<sha>`, hand-made `db:snapshot`). The second copy in `DB_BACKUP_DIR` (`~/Documents/backups/nw-tracker`, root `.env`) keeps `DB_BACKUP_DIR_KEEP` days (60). Pruned after each snapshot; by hand `npm run db:prune-snapshots -w nw-tracker-server [-- --dir=<folder> --apply]` (report-first).
- **`CACHE_WARM_ENABLED`** — dashboard cache warmer (`dashboardCacheWarmer.ts`; default on): rebuilds page bundles at boot, at 00:05 Chile, on an external `data_version` change, and 30 s after in-process invalidations; not on live price moves (the requesting request rebuilds, see «Polls follow the market clock»). The warmed object is the served response (`dashboard.page_bundle|<unit>`). CC billing detail is cached as `cc.billing_detail|<accountId>` (day/data_version fresh, dropped by CC write hooks). **Requests go first** (`apiRequestsInFlight.ts`): warm steps start only after 250 ms with no `/api` request in flight (health, beacon, `/api/ingest`, `/api/jobs` don't count; the stamp poll does; requests >20 s stop counting; a wait caps at 60 s). Only gaps between steps wait — a request may be awaiting the step's promise.
- **Flags are parsed once by `server/src/config.ts`:** `1/true/yes` on, `0/false/no` off, unset/empty default, anything else refuses to boot; precedence explicit env > demo defaults > background-jobs defaults > built-in. `initServerConfig()` runs after `loadRootDotenv()`, so a flag change needs a restart (credentials `BCENTRAL_*`, `COINGECKO_API_KEY`, `INGEST_TOKEN` are read live). Leaf modules read `deploymentProfile()` (demo, read-only API, sources never run, AFP source, market awake gate, usage-log file), never `DEMO_MODE`.
- **`BACKGROUND_JOBS_ENABLED=0`** defaults `GLOBAL_SYNC_ENABLED` / `LIVE_QUOTES_SYNC_ENABLED` / `DB_BACKUP_ENABLED` / `CACHE_WARM_ENABLED` / `USAGE_LOG_ENABLED` to 0 (an explicit per-job flag wins) and turns off the ingest scheduler and job-queue executor regardless. Set on every `.claude/launch.json` server so agent dev servers never sync or back up the shared DB.
- Express async handlers must be wrapped in `asyncHandler` (GUIDELINES.md).

## Hosted demo (Render)

Single Render web service (**`render.yaml`**) serving synthetic data, open and read-only; everything env-gated.

- **Release branch `demo`** (`scripts/release-demo.sh`, `npm run release:demo [-- <sha> | --yes | --rollback <sha>]`): Render builds only `demo` (`render.yaml` `branch: demo`; Blueprint linked to it, so `render.yaml` changes wait for a release). The script fast-forwards `demo` to a commit on `origin/main` (tip by default), lists and asks; refuses non-`main` commits, non-fast-forwards (`--rollback` moves back with a lease) and a dirty tree. Never push `demo` by hand or unasked — releasing is the user's call (the repo is public; `demo` only carries published `main` commits).
- **`DEMO_MODE=1`** (`server/src/demoMode.ts`): requires `NW_TRACKER_TEST_DB` pointing at a dedicated file (throws otherwise — never the real DB); an empty DB is filled at boot (`loadDemoRealMarket(demoMarketSpec())` + `generateDemoDb({ person: DEMO_PERSON, market: "real" })` = `generate-demo-data.ts --real-market`). **On Render the build generates it** (`npm run demo:generate -w nw-tracker-server` ending `buildCommand`): the persistent disk means Render stops the old instance before booting the new one (no zero-downtime; «Restart service» starts the latest build), so fetching at boot is downtime, while a failed build leaves the old version serving. Demo defaults: `DB_BACKUP_ENABLED=0`, `GLOBAL_SYNC_ENABLED` / `LIVE_QUOTES_SYNC_ENABLED` / `USAGE_LOG_ENABLED=1`; warmer on.
- **Real market data** (`demoData/demoRealMarket.ts`, `demoMarketActivity.ts`): Yahoo closes of `DEMO_TICKERS` (BTC-USD included) into `equity_daily`; `.SN` tickers from the committed Bolsa snapshot `server/data/demo/santiago-closes.csv` plus Yahoo bars after it (Yahoo lacks history for thin Santiago tickers and Render gets HTML from the Bolsa API; refresh with `npm run demo:refresh-santiago-snapshot -w nw-tracker-server` from a machine that reaches it; a snapshot older than `DEMO_SANTIAGO_SNAPSHOT_MAX_AGE_DAYS` = 7 is refused; `equityBolsaSantiagoEod.ts` stays in `src` for it); every `equity_with_dividends` benchmark ticker with its `equity_dividends` (else the benchmark row reads «—»); CLP=X; Banco Central dollar, euro, UF, UTM, IPC (`BCENTRAL_EMAIL` / `BCENTRAL_PASSWORD`, Render secrets `sync: false`; required). History to yesterday (crypto: last completed UTC day). Each source retries ~10 min (`DEMO_MARKET_RETRY_DELAYS_MS`) then fails the build. Unit math reads stored closes (`dbMarketReader`). **AFP** (`afpUnoApi.ts`, `loadDemoPensionMarket`): «Modelo-A» (`afp_modelo_cuota_a`) switched into «UNO-A» (`afp_uno_cuota_a`) at `DEMO_AFP_SWITCH_MONTH` (an `afp_fund_switch` with both cuota counts). The SP refuses Render, so Modelo uses a committed snapshot (`server/data/demo/afp-modelo-fund-a-2018-2020.csv`, `parseDemoAfpModeloCsv`) and UNO its own API (`POST https://www.uno.cl/api/valor-cuota-buscar/historico`, `{fechaInicio, fechaFin}`; newest first, decimal comma, one row per calendar day, full history from 2019-10-01; `codigo` ≠ "0" throws — `parseUnoValorCuotaHistorico`), both into `pension_fund_unit_official`. `afpUnoOfficialSource()` (`afpUnoOfficialSync.ts`) is `uno_api` under `DEMO_MODE`, `sp` otherwise — the one choice point; it picks the runner (`syncAfpUnoFromUnoApi` re-reads 60 days back) and adds `AFP_UNO_SOURCE_EXTRA_LAG_BUSINESS_DAYS` (UNO publishes a business day after the SP). **AFC** is a synthetic book account (`afc_cic` «Not configured»), marked each Chile business day at ~3,5 %/yr (`writeAfc`, `demoData/generate.ts`). **Syncs run only while someone browses:** real visitor requests (bots never) note activity; global sync and live quotes skip ticks while asleep and run on wake (`onDemoMarketWake`); sleep after 30 min idle (`DEMO_MARKET_IDLE_MS`). Author-only sources (`DEMO_SYNC_DISABLED_SOURCES`: Fintual goals, official-serie check, Risky Norris composition, CMF filing) read «Not configured», never poll or dim. `demoModeEnabled` lives in leaf `demoModeFlag.ts`. Rehearsal: `server-demo` / `client-demo` with the Banco Central values set.
- **The home server publishes the demo's market; Render downloads it** (`demoData/demoMarketArtifact.ts`, `demoMarketRelease.ts`, `demoMarketPublishJob.ts`, `scripts/publish-demo-market.ts`, `docs/demo-market-publish-plan.md`). Nightly at 01:00 Chile (and at boot when the last success is >20 h old) the primary runs the loader in a child process on a scratch DB (`server/data/demo-market-publish.db`; never the live one — the loader writes through the process `db`), Santiago from the Bolsa (`{santiago: "bolsa"}`), exports `DEMO_MARKET_TABLES` (fails if any other table moved, `assertOnlyMarketTablesChanged`) as gzipped JSON (`schema_version`, `generated_at`, `complete_through`, spec, columns/rows) to the fixed release `demo-market` (`demo-market.json.gz` + `demo-market.meta.json`; upload as `.next`, delete, rename; never the latest release). Needs `DEMO_MARKET_PUBLISH_TOKEN` (fine-grained, Contents read/write) and `DEMO_MARKET_PUBLISH_ENABLED` (default on, off with `BACKGROUND_JOBS_ENABLED=0`, never on the demo); failure = notification, retried after 15 and 45 min. Render's build (`npm run demo:generate` = `generate-demo-data.ts --market-asset`) downloads it tokenless and refuses another schema version, `complete_through` older than `DEMO_MARKET_ASSET_MAX_AGE_DAYS` = 3, a smaller spec or other columns; `release:demo` checks the header first. `--market-asset-file=<path>` rehearses locally; `--real-market [--santiago=bolsa]` (`demo:generate:fetch`) is the rollback. `BCENTRAL_*` stay on Render for wake syncs. Optional, off: `NW_DEPLOY_RELEASE_DEMO=1` + `RELEASE_DEMO_PUSH_REMOTE` in the deploy plist release each healthy deploy (`release_demo_after_deploy`).
- **Synthetic databases are a person told through documents** (`server/src/demoData/`, `docs/demo-generator-plan.md`). `person.ts` holds `DEMO_PERSON` (the demo's hand-tuned story; no personal data) and `testPerson(today)` (Vitest: ~20 months, contrato then contractor job, rented flat, Santander + BCI cards with cuotas, SPY + one play, a Fintual-style fund, AFP, AFC, APV — short for speed). `simulate.ts`: day-dated events from seeded per-month streams (`rng.ts`: adding a month never moves a past one), balances in calendar order, never negative; two cash bands (`CHECKING_BAND` / `SAVINGS_BAND`): checking keeps 1–2 months of trailing expenses (topped to 1,5 from savings at payday, trimmed above), the cushion (3–6 months + the next six months' known needs: cash car, December APV, pie, move-in, gap month) sits in `Person.savings` (a money-market fondo «Fondo Mutuo Money Market», series `demo_fm_money_market`, term-deposit curve − 0,3 %; the DAP principal comes from it), a need moves to checking at the prior month's payday, the excess goes to the broker; with no savings vehicle (Vitest) the savings band sits on checking; lifestyle ratchets toward a concave function of smoothed income. `render.ts` produces documents and `generate.ts` imports them through the real functions: cartolas (`applyBankAccountStatements`; own-account transfers written first so the cartola row is kept as the transfer), the current checking listing (`applyBankAccountMovements`), card facturaciones in both currencies as parser rows (`applyParsedCcStatements`) + the open cycle (`importCcWebPasteLines`), `convertCcPaymentMirrors`, Previred parameters and liquidaciones (`applyPayrollParameters`, `applyEmploymentPayslips`). Written directly: the dollar checking account, broker/exchange ledgers, AFP cuota ledger (Modelo → UNO), AFC/APV/money-market book marks (cartola «TRANSF A AGF ANDES FM MONEY MARKET» / «RESCATE …» rows kept as transfers), contractor payslips (USD, `origin = 'rebuilt'`, no deposit link — wires land in the dollar account), the mortgage ledger, the official IPC from the UF. Contractor years: boleta self-retention paid by F29 monthly, April F22 settles IGC net of independent contributions (credited to the AFP in July); APV goes régimen B when the marginal rate passes 15 %. **April is the tax module's** (`taxDocuments.ts`): the generator writes the year's documents (`sii_issued_fee_receipts`; `movement_dividend_details` with 15 % US withholding; in March DJ 1887 from liquidaciones via `payrollTaxYear`, DJ 1899 APV-B, DJ 1898 via `mortgageInterestForYear`), then `buildF22Draft`'s 305 plus code 900 (fee contributions, not drafted) is the settlement. It re-simulates from a checkpoint (`saveDemoPristineCopy` / `resetDemoDatabaseToPristine` on `<db>.pretax`) feeding `simulate(…, { aprilSettlements })` until every settlement matches (`MAX_TAX_PASSES` 6), then stores each past return as filed (`sii_f22_filed`) linked to its movement (`f22_settlements`). The simulator's own estimate only seeds pass 1 and picks the APV regime. The tax page shows on the demo. `invariants.ts` fails the build on balance mismatches vs the story, a cartola account below zero, an unpaired payslip or card payment, or net worth >25 % from US$400k.
- **The demo's front door** (`docs/demo-about-plan.md`). With `VITE_DEMO_MODE`, `/` is About and the dashboard is `/dashboard` (`client/src/dashboardPath.ts`: `DASHBOARD_PATH`, `isDashboardPathname`; personal instances serve both). Both render one `DemoHomePage` that keeps the dashboard mounted: on About it is scaled and tilted by a CSS transform at full content width (never laid out narrower — the grid follows the viewport); «Open the dashboard» transitions the transform away on the same instance. Below-the-fold tiles are folded (`DashboardFoldContext`, `dashboardFold.ts` → `GroupInfoBase` `fold`): `cut` until idle on About, then `hidden` (rendered, invisible, inert via `BelowFoldHiddenContext` → `.page-tile--fold-hidden`, no wrapper so grid placement and identity survive; their queries off), then `full` on the frame's `transitionend` (fallback 1,2 s) — a style switch only. Loading tables keep loaded height: placeholder rows (`FlowRows` `placeholderRows`, the detalle's placeholder months), the disabled filter bar, the disabled pager (`PaginatedTable`), blank styled lines in the returns table until returns + benchmark load. Line charts (`ValuationLineCharts`) size from their layout box (`AppLineChart` → `useElementSize`) and draw the legend as HTML (`.chart-box--legend`), because Recharts measures with the on-screen (tilted) box and keeps those sizes. Page wrappers are `.page-root` (`page-grid.css`). About: description, «How it works» diagram (Sources → Ingest service → Server → Client) with links, GitHub/privacy/contact; sidebar «About» above «Dashboard», «Demo tour» · «Privacy» in the footer. **Tour** (`DemoTour.tsx`): dashboard only, for 24 h from first visit until dismissed or finished; targets outlined above the ticker (`.demo-tour-target`; `fit: "content"` rings text and icons). `/privacy` (demo only) states what is recorded. Scroll position is remembered per URL for the page load (`useScrollMemory.ts`). `client/public/og-image.png`: `node scripts/render-og-image.mjs --url=<demo>`; `release:demo` notes when client code changed after it. **Layout:** desktop text column 45 % on a translucent panel; the tilted dashboard covers the last ~60 % behind it, cut below the net-worth legend (`usePreviewHeight`, from the tile's untransformed offset). Both directions animate 0,7 s (transitions on each destination state); the frame pivots on its top-right corner in both states (pivot changes don't animate) and takes its natural height during the move. **Pinned preview:** a zero-height sticky `.pin` (`page-grid`) holds it at `--about-frame-top` (measured); on About `.layout-main` only clips sideways (`overflow-x: clip`) because its `overflow: auto` is the sticky container. From a scrolled About the page stays `leaving` (decided in the route-change render) while text fades (0,2 s) and it scrolls to top; then the pin releases and the move starts; the dashboard opens at top (`skipNextScrollRestore`). Ticker and currency selector (`AppDisplayPreferencesBar`) mount only after the move (`demoTickerGate.ts`; strip reserved, query warm). On the About preview the net-worth lines wipe in over 3 s the first time (`DashboardAboutPreviewContext` → `drawInMs`, CSS clip on `.recharts-line`). About's tab title is the bare app name. **First paint:** the preview (and the demo's `/dashboard` content) stays invisible until cards carry real numbers (bundle, held bundle or stored snapshot, never the zero placeholder; `DashboardStripReadyContext`) and its height is measured (strip observed too); then it fades in rising 1,25rem (`translate`, 1,2 s), never dims inside, and the draw-in waits (`DashboardPreviewShownContext` → `drawInHold`). `index.html` preloads (`<link rel=preload as=fetch>`) the sidebar tree, the page bundle in the stored unit and, with no chart preference, `overview-daily?days=1096`; the warmer builds that series in both units after the bundles. **Loading frame:** `ChartEmptyState` with `legend` (and the lazy `LineChartPanel` frame with `ChartLegendPlaceholder`) keeps plot + legend layout, draws no stand-in grid; real grid and axes fade in. The ATH marker waits for the lines' first draw (80 % of the slow wipe, else 300 ms) and fades in.
- **The US chapter** (`docs/demo-us-chapter-plan.md`, `demoData/usPayroll.ts`): contractor at Northwind Labs, then its W-2 employee in Austin (`UsW2Job`: salary, 4 % April raises plus a one-off market adjustment that later raises compound on, `raiseExtraUsd`; March bonus by year, `bonus.fractionByYear`). Biweekly stubs via `applyEmploymentPayslips` with `jurisdiction: "us"` (FICA, percentage-method withholding, 401(k) with the match as a `memo` line, medical, dental, HSA), each paired with its net credit on «Checking (US)» (`usd`, under Checking accounts, `bank_account_numbers` row). US checking keeps the checking band; cushion and reserves (car, Roth contribution, December flight, April 1040) sit in «DEMO BANK TEXAS SAVINGS» (`Person.savings.usd`, `usd` ledger under Cash savings, same issuer), moved to checking the month before use; excess goes to IBKR by ACH; «ONLINE TRANSFER TO / FROM SAVINGS» statement rows are kept as transfer legs (never gastos); month-end `savings_earnings` at `usdYieldPct(year) − 0,3` (1040 line 2b, F22 1104). The monthly wire to Chile = checking band target × `chileFixedCosts(month)` (cuotas as charged, gastos comunes, contribuciones, Chilean subscriptions, minus rents net of commission) + `chileUpcomingNeeds`; the Chilean fund keeps the savings band's low × those costs (the rest to the broker in the move month). Retirement splits Chile / US (`ensureRetirementCountrySplit`): 401(k) and HSA are cuota ledgers on USD series `demo_us_target_2060` (90 % SPY, 10 % at 3,5 %/yr); the Roth buys SPY each October through its sweep «Roth IRA cash» (`usd` leaf, `rothCash`: contribution lands there, then `stock_buy`, so one cash leg); Roth dividends pay whole into the sweep and are reinvested the same day (sweep ends each day at 0 → chart-inactive, out of the nav). Roth growth is income to neither return: `usRetirementAccountIds("roth_ira")` (`usRetirementAccounts.ts`, by `usRetirementAccountImportKeyPrefix`) is the one rule, read by `loadF22Dividends` (Roth from-legs stay out of 1104/748/1018) and the generator (no `movement_dividend_details` row), never a name (`usRetirementAccounts.test.ts`). The 1040 draft reads no dividends. W-2s are derived from stubs and checked, not stored. `testPerson` has no US chapter. **Rentals and the US card:** `houses: House[]` (Centro, DFL-2, lender «HIPOTECARIA PACIFICO»; Vitacura), each with its own property/loan import keys, `lender_pattern` role, pie, cuotas (Chilean card line under the lender merchant) and prepagos; the agency forwards gross rent and debits 7 % the same day; rent credits carry a `rent` override; gastos comunes and contribuciones are bill rows (Centro's halved while `dfl2_contribuciones_halved_through` lasts; rent re-indexed by UF yearly, one vacancy month a year). `generate.ts` must call `syncMortgageExpenseDepositLinksFromSheet` right after card statements and mortgage payments, or each cuota counts as a gasto beside its dividendo deposit; `invariants.ts` asserts `cash_unexplained` within 1.000 pesos of the story's conversion costs each month (conversions between checking accounts sold at `fx × 0,992` / bought at `÷ fx × 0,995`, dollars held at the sell rate, half spread on dollar flows counted at the mid; `cash_fx` carries the fx move on dollars held). The US card «Lonestar Rewards» (closes on the 15th, US$95/yr) carries US variable spending, paid by dollar `pago_tarjeta` autopay from US checking; rent, utilities and the wire stay debits. `assignPurchasesToRealEstateExpenses(items)` (`realEstateExpenseMatching.ts`) writes a bill batch in one transaction reading expense lines once. Trap: `syncExpenseLinksNow()` inside a transaction is very slow (`dbStateMemo` bypassed, `listRealEstatePropertyAccountIds` rebuilds the nav model per call) — the generator runs the mortgage sync outside one; every app category write runs that sync. Net-worth target US$460k (`netWorthTargetUsd`, ±25 %). About tells the chapter (`demo.about.story` / `storyTax`); the tour has «Retirement, Chile and US» (Retiro page) and «Two returns each April» (tax tabs) steps.
- **Lifestyle = one multiplier over a per-city cost table** (`demoData/costOfLiving.ts`): `CityCostOfLiving` (`santiago`, `austin`) gives the monthly baseline per category in the city's currency at a reference month (hand-tuned assumptions, stated in the file) and how each is paid: `drawn` (merchant pool), `bills` (household shares), `list` (list prices, no multiplier) or `housing` (lease/owner models). Budget = m × baseline × season × city price index (Santiago real IPC via UF; Austin 3 %/yr), m dimensionless (`lifestyleReal` in Santiago basket units, m = level ÷ 525k). Abroad the ratchet reads last month's US net pay at Austin purchasing power (`santiagoBasketPerUnit`).
