Austin: groceries, rent, utilities, the wire and arrival one-offs are debit; once the Lonestar card opens, drawn rows go on it, with Austin seasons (December gifts, Thanksgiving, SXSW, ACL) and a May Mexico City trip whose charges each carry a 3 % «FOREIGN TRANSACTION FEE» (section-3, `fee: true`); no interest. Subscriptions: no service on both sides; Chilean side adds some from the move (`onlyAbroad`), each Chilean card still bills monthly. Category rules are global per vendor, so a new merchant must not share a vendor with another category's.
- **Generated ingest history** (`server/src/demoData/ingestHistory.ts`): the last generator step writes `person.ingestHistoryDays` (45 demo, 3 Vitest) of scheduler records ending last night — a 22:00 `ingest.nightly` job + `ingest_runs` row and :30 hourly polls with the balance poll — through the real writers (`enqueueJob` / `claimJob` / `completeJob`, `markIngestRunRequested`, `recordDailyRun` / `recordHourlyEmailRun`, `recordBankCupoCapture`, `applyBankAccountBalances`, `cc_bank_cupo_check`), re-dated. Mails land in the hour polls import them; a login fails ~monthly and a mail step ~1/240 polls, recovering next run; per-day seeded. On the demo import-sync hides «Run now» / «Retry» / «Cancel» / «Next» and says the history is generated; Notifications don't mark read.
- **The demo is writable, a little** (`server/src/demoWritePolicy.ts`, `demoWritable.ts`): allowed — a line's category («Único»), assigning an existing big group, marking a refund, a purchase's cuotas count, line pieces, manual expenses with a description from a fixed list (free text would show to everyone). All else `403 demo_read_only`; the client hides those controls (`client/src/demoEdits.ts`). 100 writes per daily visitor id (`429 demo_write_limit`; no id = bot = 403). After a write, once idle 30 min or at the first check after 04:00 Chile, the DB is restored from `<demo db>.pristine` (`VACUUM INTO` copy from the generator, or made at boot): tables emptied and refilled from the attached copy in one FK-off transaction, `mark_input_changes` cleared, `clearAggregationCache()`.
- **`SERVE_CLIENT_DIST=1`** (`server/src/staticClientDist.ts`): serves `client/dist` with an SPA fallback for non-`/api` GETs (same origin, no CORS). Hashed `/assets` immutable; missing assets 404 (never the fallback); `index.html` `no-cache`. Fails at boot without `client/dist/index.html` (`npm run build -w nw-tracker-client`).
- **`render.yaml`**: build `npm install --include=dev` (Render's `NODE_ENV=production` skips devDeps) + client build; start `npm run serve -w nw-tracker-server` (**tsx from source** — compiled `dist/` lacks `server/migrations/*.sql`); health `/api/health`; `PORT` injected.
- **Deploy:** Render → New → **Blueprint** → this repo. **The repo is public; tree and history hold no personal data** (amounts purged by the squash, numbers redacted, cfraser data purged, card last4s scrubbed; per-card config in gitignored `cfraser/*.json`, synthetic cards in tests). Only the author's name/email as attribution (and the landing page's contact link in history), intended. Pre-scrub history bundle: `~/Documents/backups/nw-tracker/`.
- **Visit analytics** (`server/src/demoAnalytics.ts`): one `demo_visits` row per visitor per day; no login, cookie or stored IP — id = `hmac(daily key, ip|user-agent)` with the day in the key (no cross-day correlation), hashing Cloudflare's `CF-Connecting-IP` (behind Render's edge `req.ip` changes per request); referrers cut to origin; bot/uptime agents skipped. Page views and timings are the usage log's (page views tagged with the visitor id, bots not recorded); `?src=` rides the beacon onto the visit — publish tagged links (`?src=linkedin` / `cv` / `homepage`; README `?src=github`). Stored in a **separate SQLite file** (`DEMO_ANALYTICS_DB` → `/var/data/…`), since the demo DB is regenerated each deploy (visits + usage tables; `usageDb.ts` applies migration 247 there); salt `DEMO_ANALYTICS_SALT`. The demo refuses `/api/usage/*` (panel tab hidden via `VITE_DEMO_MODE`, derived from `DEMO_MODE` in `vite.config.ts`). **Owner visits:** `?owner=<DEMO_OWNER_CODE>` (Render-generated; Environment tab) sets HttpOnly `nw_demo_owner` for 5 years (`demoOwnerCookieMiddleware`); visits with it are flagged `demo_visits.owner` (sticky within the day), excluded from every table and listed last; per browser/device; no code = off. Report `npm run demo:visits` (from the Render shell). Local mode records no visits.
- **Domains (Namecheap `crfrsr.io`):** `nw-tracker.crfrsr.io` on the web service (`starter` plan, no spin-down). The landing page is the [crfrsr-site](https://github.com/cristianfraser/crfrsr-site) repo (`~/Projects/crfrsr-site`) on **Cloudflare Pages** for `crfrsr.io` + `www`. DNS: `@`/`www` → Cloudflare Pages, `CNAME nw-tracker` → the nw-tracker-demo `.onrender.com` host; keep the `poketeam` CNAME. Render issues TLS once DNS verifies.
- **Local rehearsal:** `.claude/launch.json` `server-demo` / `client-demo` (ports 3210/5210; `DEMO_MODE=1` / `VITE_DEMO_MODE=1`, analytics to `server/data/demo-analytics.db`), or `PORT=… DEMO_MODE=1 SERVE_CLIENT_DIST=1 NW_TRACKER_TEST_DB=/tmp/demo.db npm run serve -w nw-tracker-server` after a client build.

## Schema baseline (fresh DBs)

`initSchema()` runs `server/src/schemaBaseline.ts` (full schema as of migration 155, all `IF NOT EXISTS`) and marks ≤ 155 applied on a new DB; those files are deleted (copies in `~/Documents/backups/nw-tracker/{removed,squashed}-migrations/`). Only the 8 reference-row seeds in `BASELINE_REFERENCE_DATA_MIGRATIONS` (db.ts) remain and run on fresh DBs. Recorded-but-missing files are harmless, so numbering gaps are expected; 156+ apply normally. A migration may pair with a `POST_MIGRATION_HOOKS` entry in `db.ts` (pure TS, inside the migration transaction — e.g. `legacyNoteBackfills.ts` for 157). A table a migration drops must also leave the baseline, or every boot recreates it empty. To squash: `npx tsx scripts/regenerate-schema-baseline.ts --last <newest-migration>` (from `server/`), then delete the covered files (keep the seeds). Migration SQL is split naively on `;` — no triggers or `;`/`--` inside literals; use a post-migration hook.

## Server verbose logging

- **`DEBUG_VERBOSE=1`** — all channels below (stderr).
- **`DEBUG_HTTP=1`** — `[api] -->` / `[api] <--` per request (method, path, status, ms).
- **`DEBUG_HTTP_OUT=1`** — `[http-out]` for outbound `fetch` (BCentral, Yahoo, SP, Fintual).
- **`DEBUG_DB=1`** — `[db]` for statements slower than **`DEBUG_DB_SLOW_MS`** (20); **`DEBUG_DB_ALL=1`** logs every statement.
- **`DEBUG_PERF=1`** — `[heavy] …ms` for labeled work (`server/src/heavyWork.ts`).

## Usage and performance log (migration 247, `usageLog.ts`, `usageReport.ts`, `usageDb.ts`, `/panel/usage`)

- **Recorded so optimization follows real use** (primary and demo). Every `/api` request (`usageLogMiddleware`, mounted first): duration to last byte, status, pre-compression size (`res.locals.usageBodyBytes` from `jsonResponse.ts`, else `Content-Length`; none for 304), cache outcome (`requestCacheStats.ts` per-request `AsyncLocalStorage`; `getAggregationCached` reports each lookup: warm = all hits, cold = any miss, neither = none). Aggregated per UTC hour × method × route pattern (`req.baseUrl + req.route.path`, `(unmatched)` for 404) × work-changing params (`USAGE_KEY_QUERY_PARAMS`: `unit`, `days`, `period`, `portfolio_group`, …; never `account_id` or free text) into `usage_endpoint_hourly` (count, total/max ms, 4xx/5xx, bytes, warm/cold, 15-bucket histogram `USAGE_HISTOGRAM_BOUNDS_MS`, `h00..h14`, for p50/p95). Every route change (`useUsagePageTiming`, `client/src/usagePageTiming.ts`, beacon `POST /api/usage/page-view`, untimed) into `usage_page_views`: path, route (numeric segments → `:id`), first-page flag, settle time (route change, or page start for the first, to the last query fetch once 400 ms quiet; left/hidden/still fetching after 60 s = no time, `outcome`). Only the path is stored (plus the daily `visitor` hash on the demo).
- **Requests only touch memory:** flushed once a minute in one transaction (`flushUsageLog`, upserting the hour); endpoint rows >90 days deleted daily; page views kept.
- **Where:** `usageDb()` — main DB on the personal instance, `DEMO_ANALYTICS_DB` on the demo. `USAGE_LOG_ENABLED` (default on) defaults to 0 under `BACKGROUND_JOBS_ENABLED=0` so dev servers never write the primary's DB; the demo defaults it to 1. When off, the beacon answers 204.
- **`/panel/usage`** (`GET /api/usage/report?days=1|7|30|90`, `GET /api/usage/endpoint-trend?method=&route=&params=&days=`): top pages (views, exact settle p50/p95/max, unsettled views, mean queries), endpoints by p95 (count, p50/p95/max, total time, warm share, errors, mean size), and a picked endpoint's daily trend over every Chile day. The server computes all figures. Demo: 404 `usage_report_not_public` (use `npm run demo:visits`).

## Dashboard performance profiling

- **`npm run profile:dashboard`** (from `server/`): sequential builder timings on the test/dev DB (`NW_TRACKER_TEST_DB` or `server/data/nw-tracker.test.db`).
- **`DEBUG_PERF=1`** (or `DEBUG_VERBOSE=1`) logs `[heavy]` spans during requests.
- **`GET /api/dashboard/page-bundle`**: the home page's one call, served from `dashboard.page_bundle|<unit>` (built by the warmer; the cached value is the in-flight promise so concurrent cold requests share a build). Dropped by every explicit invalidation (`invalidateCcBillingDetail`, `invalidateMarketDataAggregations`); only requests in an invalidation→rewarm gap pay the build.
- **`GET /api/dashboard/nav-context`** (sidebar/group pages): account rows, card metrics, value map, no net-worth series; fetched on group/liabilities routes and account pages whose nav node has routable children (home uses the bundle). **Scoped** by required `?root=<nav node slug>` (`navContextScope.ts`): card metrics of the subtree and of groups its nodes host (`linked_card_slugs`), the account rows those nodes hold plus every liability account (the property card names the mortgage), the value map under the node; bucket totals, layout and liabilities breakdown whole. The Pasivos tree is searched first (net worth has a childless `liabilities` node). The client keys on the root and merges into the stored nav snapshot (`mergeNavContextIntoSnapshotCache`; never writes without one — a slice doesn't cover the tree); the warmer builds visited group pages' slices. It is requested beside the page's own requests and the strip shows as soon as it lands, dimmed until then.
- The dominant cost is usually the portfolio-group totals inside **`getDashboardValuationTimeseries`** / **`getGroupValuationTimeseries`** (`@heavy` in source).
- **JSON is serialized once and compressed** (`jsonResponse.ts`): `jsonResponseMiddleware` replaces `res.json` on `/api` with the same body, `Content-Type` and weak ETag (over uncompressed bytes, so 304s work across encodings); bodies ≥1 KB get `Vary: Accept-Encoding` and brotli (preferred on q ties; browsers offer it on HTTPS only) or gzip, never over an existing `Content-Encoding` or `no-transform`. A payload marked with `markJsonResponseCacheable` keeps bytes, ETag and compressed forms in a WeakMap keyed by the object (lives and dies with the cache entry). Marked: group and account daily-series responses (`groupDailySeriesResponse.ts`; account as `daily.series|response|account|`, dropped by API writes), `cachedGroupResponse`, the page bundle, nav snapshot/context, the account detail bundle, both card ledgers. **Only immutable cached objects may be marked** (a mutated object serves stale bytes); others are stringified per response at gzip 1 / brotli 1 (cached ones gzip 6 / brotli 5, once per build).

## Server Vitest (SQLite)

- Vitest uses **`server/data/nw-tracker.test.db`**, never `nw-tracker.db` (both gitignored, `server/data/*.db`).
- **`NW_TRACKER_TEST_DB`** is set in `server/vitest.config.ts` (`test.env`) and the server **`npm run test`** script, before `db.ts` loads.
- **`server/src/vitest.globalSetup.ts`** generates the **Vitest person** when the test DB is missing (`generate-demo-data.ts --test`, `testPerson`); tests must not depend on personal data. It is never refreshed automatically: a stale pre-synthetic copy keeps being used (symptom: missing `demo:*` fixture accounts or real-data invariants) — delete it to rebuild. `NW_TRACKER_TEST_FROM_DEV=1` copies the local `nw-tracker.db` instead (reproducing against real data).
- **`server/src/vitest.setup.ts`** → **`server/src/test/vitestDbSeed.ts`**: ensures the isolated master `credit_card_master|santander|vitest-fixture` for merge/import tests.
- Override with `NW_TRACKER_TEST_DB` (absolute path, basename under `server/data/`, or `:memory:`). `pool: "forks"`, one sequential fork (`fileParallelism: false`): better-sqlite3 segfaults in worker threads at teardown, and parallel forks race migrations on one file.
- Clean fixture rows out of **`nw-tracker.db`**: `sqlite3 server/data/nw-tracker.db < server/scripts/cleanup-cc-vitest-pollution.sql`.

## UI headings

Headings follow `client/src/styles/base.css`: `h2` 1.1rem, `h3` 1rem, `h4` 0.95rem, weight 600, `var(--muted)`. Levels are the outline (page section `h2`, surfaces `h3`); components never set a heading's size, weight or colour inline. Exceptions: `.chart-panel-title` keeps chart titles at the `h2` look at any level; the checking ledger-anchor form heading matches its form text (`--font-size-ui`).

## UI tables — desktop/mobile sync

Parallel-renderings rule: GUIDELINES.md. Desktop columns are `<td className="desktop-only">`; mobile is `<TableMobileCard>` / `<TableMobileCardRow>` inside `<td className="mobile-only">`, usually in the same component or a companion (e.g. `CreditCardSections.tsx` + `CreditCardPurchaseMobileCard.tsx`).

**A table shows its cards whenever its desktop rendering does not fit** (`useTableFit.ts`, `Table` `fitToWidth`): below 880 px, and at any width when the desktop columns exceed the `.table-wrap` — `Table` measures after every render and wrap resize (class removed, measured, reset; synchronous, before paint, no state) and sets `.table-wrap--cards`, styled exactly like the narrow viewport (rule sets side by side). Default for every `table--parallel-mobile` table, so both renderings switch together; a fixed breakpoint can't work because a table is as wide as its data. `PeriodReturnsTable` (one markup, fixed layout, 34rem / 42rem-with-benchmark floor) flips by container query on its wrap (`period-returns`). Never fix overflow with a scroll box. A right-edge card's number-flow mask spills ~6 px, so card blocks at the content edge use `overflow-x: clip` (`.cards-strip`, home «Flujos» period cards). Check pages at 375 / 599 / 600 / 879 / 880 / 1024 / 1279 / 1280 / 1439 / 1440 / 1600 / 1920 for `.layout-main` scrollWidth = clientWidth.

**Expense line lists** (`CreditCardExpenseLinesTable.tsx`): month, facturación, «sin categoría», big-group and deposit-matched lists are one responsive list (no horizontal scroll, same rendering at every width; a container query moves pills under the merchant when narrow). A row: origin icon (`ExpenseOriginIcon` from the structured `origin`: card / bank / manual / payslip, account in a hover/tap card), date, big-group pill once set, category pill (`PillPicker`: option-pill popover, × on hover clears, «Único» inside; an unset big group is picked from the extras), merchant with note and an ⓘ card (counterparty, receipt, deposit link), amount with installment detail in superscript, and a ⋯ extras button (big group while unset, note, pieces, cuotas, delete) with a red badge when the line waits on the user. Header = three sort buttons (date, category, amount). A popover inside a `Modal` renders into its `<dialog>` (`AnchoredPopover`), since `showModal()` puts the dialog in the top layer.

**The flows list** (`FlowRows.tsx`, under `FlowsTable`'s filters): a row list, not a table. When every row fits on one line, rows are subgrids under a header — date, type pill, accounts, amount (units superscript), note (the one flexible, ellipsized column); below the container breakpoint (`flow-rows-wide` 58rem when both accounts are named, `flow-rows-narrow` 44rem on an account page) a row is three lines: date + type + amount, accounts, note. **An intra-group transfer shows once** (`assembleFlowRows`, `flowsApi.ts`): the origin's row, `internal`, unsigned, with a side-neutral type (`internalTransferFlowType`: «Traspaso <CODE>», or «Cambio de divisas» across currencies; share purchases and card payments keep their type); account/bucket filters match either side (`counterpart_bucket_slug`). A transfer leaving the group keeps its signed leg. Every row carries `from_account_*` / `to_account_*` and `money_direction` (`moneyDirectionForAccount`: by leg for transfers, else by signed amount in its own currency, then units; null only for a zero opening anchor; anything else undecidable throws). Labels naming a currency take it from the movement (`movementFlowTypeLabel(type, {currency, counter_currency})`). Accounts cell (`flowAccountsCell`): on a group/dashboard origin → target («→ account» from outside, «account →» leaving); on an account page the page's account is omitted and the arrow shows left (→) or arrived (←), then the other account.

**Names an expense row shows** (`expenseDisplayNames.ts`, `vendorCatalog.ts`, `expenseNameText.ts`, `chilePlaces.ts`, migrations 248–250). Display only — `merchant` stays the bank's text (rules and the receipt/real-estate matchers read it). `display_merchant`: bank clutter dropped (« (T)», «COMPRAS P.A.T.», «, SANTIAGO», a leading account number, «Agustinas », «COMPRA» / «Compra Nacional»), processor prefix moved to `merchant_processor` (MERPAGO\*, PAYU \*, HAULMER\*, DL \*, DP \*, PAYPAL \*, SQ \*…), then the vendor catalog. If the catalog doesn't know the name and the shop's or processor's own receipt is linked (Shopify, Flow, Pago Fácil, shop order mails — never a marketplace: Uber Eats, MercadoLibre, App Store), the receipt's payee becomes the merchant and the redundant label is hidden. A payslip line shows the employer without legal suffix, words spelled («Impuesto Único · Empresa Chile»). A merchant cut by the listing (15 chars on the web table and pending feed rows, spaces collapsed first, so length proves nothing) reads as the longer merchant it starts on the same account, only when all such read the same through the catalog. A checking transfer with a paired mail reads «Transf a <label>». `link_label` (`arrow`, `text`, `nights`, `inline`): a receipt's payee (never the processor; « - X» and brand + branch as «Brand (X)»; MercadoLibre sellers title-cased with «+N»; a URL's host; e-mails/placeholders nothing; a stay's city + nights), an App Store app before its tagline, a transfer by RUT through the catalog else the RUT's known name (a user-named contact over the bank's capitals, then the most frequent), companies without legal suffix, people as first name + first surname; `inline` false when the merchant already contains it. The label follows the note, the extras repeat it, the ⓘ card has full detail plus bank text. **Vendor catalog** (`vendor_catalog`: `exact` / `prefix` / `branch` «Brand (Branch)» / `rut`, folded patterns): a branch the name already sets apart («(Isidora)», after « - ») is unwrapped; a legal-form-only branch is dropped; places are spelled («PENALOLEN» → «Peñalolén»); a name possibly cut by the statement's 22-char column completes a mid-word branch to the one place it starts («VINA DEL MA» → «Viña del Mar», store numbers kept); several possible places → `branch_choices`, red ⋯ badge, «¿Qué sucursal?», the pick stored as a `choice` row (`POST /api/flows/expenses/vendor-branch-choice`; matched after user rows, before seed rows; untouched by the loader). Generic brands are migration seeds; the user's people/RUTs/places come from untracked `cfraser/vendor-catalog.json` via `server/scripts/load-vendor-catalog.ts [--apply]` (report-first, replaces all user rows; matched before seeds). `server/scripts/report-expense-link-labels.ts` lists labels, counterparties and printed merchants to grow it. **Catalog changes carry category rules** (migration 325, `vendorCatalogChange.ts`): a new name changes the vendor key («SB 731» → «SALCOBRAND»), so every change goes through `applyVendorCatalogChange` (migrations and `load-vendor-catalog.ts`, whose report run rolls back): a new key without a rule takes its lines' majority category; a card line the rule would change keeps its category as a per-line override; an uncategorized merchant takes the brand's; unreachable rules are deleted (listed). Additional-cardholder lines carry `additional_card`, never vote, and a general rule only they reach is deleted; a category change on such a line is always its own «Único» row (`ownCategoryOnly`). An exact entry «Brand (branch)» whose Brand is another entry shares its rule (`exactBrand`); a branch keeps a store number when the bank prints no place («Salcobrand (731)», «Cruz Verde (Las Condes 1234)»); renamed chains follow `docs/vendor-catalog-rebrands.md`. **In-event charges:** an event vendor charge (`IN_EVENT_VENDORS`: Glovox, Toliv) without receipt, on a ticket receipt's event start day or the next, links as a guess to that event. «Namecheap Order Summary» is a dollar receipt (`namecheap`) labelled by what it bought. Mail-written transfer notes omit the payee's bank («Transf a <payee>»); transfer notices are repaired of UTF-8-as-Latin-1 on import (`textRepair.ts`); `server/scripts/archive/sanitize-transfer-mail-notes.ts [--apply]` rewrites stored ones (copying merchant rules to the new key).

**Display units are currencies** (migration 312, `displayUnits.ts`, `rates.ts`, `fxPairsSync.ts`, `docs/any-currency-plan.md`). The toggle offers every **core** currency in `currencies` (CLP, USD, EUR; `GET /api/currencies` → code, symbol, minor digits, tier, read by the picker and formatters). Requests name `?unit=<code>` (`requestDisplayUnit`; `include_usd` / `include_uf` ignored) and every payload is built in that one unit — no `*_usd` twins, fixed-unit `Record`s or `unit === "usd"` branches outside `rates.ts` / `valueConvert.ts` (dashboard rows, nav cards, value map, bundles (`nav_strip` 20, `group_shell` 3), valuation/daily series, performance, Rentabilidad, benchmarks, export, all flows payloads, latest movements, cards/liabilities, ticker, watchlist, market series, projections, wealth percentile). **Rates:** `rates.ts` tries a direct pair, then via USD, then via CLP (UF and Banco Central EUR/CLP are peso pairs). EUR/USD is in `fx_rates` (Yahoo `EURUSD=X` from the portfolio start, write-once, dated on the London day, due after London midnight; source `fx_pairs`, `scripts/sync-fx-pairs.ts`); EUR/CLP reads `eur_daily`. Every core pair has a live rate (see «Every core pair has a live rate»); today's EUR/CLP crosses EUR/USD × USD/CLP. A flow converts at its day's rate (`flowEventInUnit` / `convertFlow`: own dollar leg in USD, buy rate pesos → dollars, day mid otherwise). **Native currencies are not display units:** a card's dollar sub-balance, `facturado_usd`, the card billing ledger and facturaciones (pesos), the tax return and 1040, groceries, fx bid/ask gaps. `ZERO_CLOSE_EPS` (`perfKernel.ts`) needs each currency's smallest coin. EUR projections use the USD inflation default (2,5 %). **Fx coverage** (`fxCoverage.ts`; `fx_coverage` on the bundle and market series; `FxCoverageBanner`) checks every pair the conversion into the unit walks from each held currency (both movement legs, valuations, plus pesos and dollars; walk = `conversionPairs`, rows `pairStoredDates` / `pairHasStoredQuote`): `{unit, pairs: [{base, quote, complete, first_missing_date, …}], …}`, copy naming pair and unit. Warmers build every core currency (`warmedDisplayUnits`). The synthetic market writes EUR/USD ~1,10 and EUR/CLP = USD/CLP × EUR/USD; the demo's real market loads EUR/USD and its asset carries `fx_rates` (asset schema 2 — publish a new asset from the home server before the next demo release).

**An expense line's money is its own currency** (`lineMoney.ts`, `wireExpenseLine.ts`, contract `Money` / `LineConverted`). Wire: `amount: {value, currency}`, `converted: {value, currency, rate_source} | null` in the requested unit (every lines endpoint takes `?unit=`; null when already in it), `value_clp` (pesos, for peso documents: facturación/month modal totals, financing candidates). `rate_source`: `native`; `paid` / `live` / `pay_by` (card dollars at the facturación's paid rate, today's while unpaid, pay-by − 1 when none on file: `facturacionUsdRateWithSourceResolver`; a dollar payment line at the pesos paid, `usdPaymentLineClpResolver`); `day`. Into a third currency a line goes through its pesos and keeps their source. `amount_clp` / `amount_usd` / `amount_usd_at_expense` are server construction data (in-pipeline matchers read them); finished-line readers use `lineValueIn(l, unit)` / `lineAmount(l)` / `linePesos(l)`. The client never converts: `formatLineAmount` / `MoneyAmount` show native + converted («US$13,30 (≈ $13.083)», «≈» = `live`), only converted below 450 px of list width (container query). A peso document (utility bill, demo place bills) matches only peso lines; real-estate bills and declared recurring payments still read a dollar line's pesos. Manual expenses may be any core currency (migration 316; flow triggers read the row's amount and currency).

**A card-to-card facturado payment is one transfer** (migration 311 `card_to_card_payments`, `cardToCardPayments.ts`, `scripts/sync-card-to-card-payments.ts`): each part of a facturado payment record — a one-shot charge (line pesos and day) or a `finances_facturado` plan (principal on purchase day) — is a `pago_tarjeta` transfer paying master → paid master, recorded in `card_to_card_payments` (movement, financing link, part kind + ref; deleting the row deletes the movement). Derived: `syncCardToCardPaymentTransfers` creates/replaces/deletes them after every card write (after the facturado matcher), after a hand-made financing link changes, and at boot; a dollar part throws (as `usdDebtPaymentsForAccount` would). Both card lines stay evidence; every money reader skips the transfer (deposits, cash, owed, P/L unchanged). Guards: the nightly mirror converter never offers a PAGO a card-to-card transfer explains (`cardToCardPaymentEvidenceClaim`: a part, a day's parts or the whole record within `MATCH_WINDOWS.financing_payment`); the manual card-payment task refuses a card source.

**A card payment is named by what it paid** (`expensePaymentNames.ts`, `payment_name` on lines, `display_brand` on `CC_ISSUERS`), from payment records, never bank text: a one-shot charge paying another card's facturado (`cc_facturado_payment_charges`) and every line of a `finances_facturado` plan («Total» and cuotas) → «Pago <brand> ·<last4>» (`pays_card`); the paid card's payment line → «Pago con tarjeta <brand> ·<last4>» (`paid_by_card`) when it is the whole facturado, or, paid in parts, one payment per charge/plan summing to it, dated within `MATCH_WINDOWS.financing_payment` of the latest paying day and matched by `ccPaymentEvidenceKey` (so a statement PAGO replacing a planted one keeps the name); the card leg of a `pago_tarjeta` from a bank account → «Pago desde <account>» (`paid_from_account`; a divisas abono by its dollars). Ambiguity names nothing. The client words it via i18n (`expenses.creditCard.paymentName.*`, `expensePaymentName`) in place of the merchant; the ⓘ card keeps store and bank text.

**Expense category rules are global, keyed on the vendor** (migration 252, `expenseCategoryRules.ts`, `bankMerchantName.ts`): `expense_category_rules` holds a general rule per vendor and specific rules per vendor + link. The vendor (`vendorRuleKey`) is the catalog brand of the bank text (a `branch` entry without its branch — every Jumbo is «JUMBO»; processor prefix off — «PAYU \*UBER EATS» = «UBER EATS»), else the cleaned name; a rule learned on a cut name reaches the full one (`vendorRuleKeyCandidates`). The link is a receipt's store (by brand), an App Store app, or a transfer recipient (RUT, else name); recipient rules ignore the transfer wording (vendor `TRANSFERENCIA`). Precedence: the line's own category («Único», per-line override) > specific rule > general rule > «sin categoría»; the general rule applies in `resolveCcExpenseCategory`, the specific one late, once the link is known (`withSpecificCategoryRules`, after the subscription step, for card and checking lines whose category only the bank name decided, including an «Único» row without category). Lines carry `category_link` (kind, key, label, `specific_default`) and `category_specific`; the picker's «Específico» checkbox (default on, off for food delivery: Uber Eats, Rappi) picks which rule a change writes, sent as `rule_link`. `server/scripts/report-expense-category-rule-migration.ts --db=<file>` lists migration 252's merge of the old per-account rules and its conflicts (read-only).
